SECTION ONE -- METHOD
[SEC:BASIC_QUALITIES_OF_COHERENCE]
==========================================================================
BASIC QUALITIES OF COHERENCE
Organized by Nested Cycle Model [primary -- see THEORY section].
Tower vocabulary retained as nesting-depth descriptor.
C1 [fundamental] / C2 [epistemic] / C3 [temporal].
Q-numbers preserved for case library reference.

NESTED CYCLE STRUCTURE OVERVIEW:
  FOUNDATION -- preconditions enabling all cycles
  C1 [fundamental] -- generative domain, core: GENERATIVE CYCLE
  C2 [epistemic] -- readable domain, core: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  C3 [temporal] -- transmissible domain, core: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
  L3.5 -- inter-system interface, core: RECOGNITION [unchanged]
  L4 -- meta domain, core: REFLEXIVITY [operational, unconfirmed]

  Level vocabulary: L1=C1, L2=C2, L3=C3. All existing tests and
  case citations valid. Tower order = nesting depth, not dependency
  sequence. See NESTED CYCLE MODEL in THEORY section.

TOWER CODEPENDENCE MODEL [legacy reference -- superseded by Nested Cycle]
  Qualities: tension unit cores and constants only.
  Former support qualities deprecated as qualities; content converted
  to level tests. Case library citation cleanup complete.
  Each integer level has one tension unit [3 similar but different
  qualities; middle difficult but possible = floor of next level].
  Half levels are static analysis checkpoints between integer levels.

  TENSION UNIT QUALITIES [12 confirmed + 3 candidates]:
    L1: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION / CORRECTION CAPACITY
    L2: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY / REFERENCE INTEGRITY
    L3: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY / CAUSAL AXIS
      GROUNDING
    L3.5: RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY [confirmed]
    L4: REFLEXIVITY / INTEGRATION / PROPAGATION [candidates --
      structure sound, no primary case confirmed. Promote when
      case library has L4 primary entry.]

  CONSTANT QUALITIES [required from L1 upward]:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY -- fires at every level [also L1 tension unit]
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY -- scopes every quality's operation
    TENSION MAINTENANCE -- holds all levels in shape
    Post-L0.5 only. L0.5 pre-constant zone, foundation only.

  FOUNDATION [preconditions -- not tension units]:
    GENUINE AXIS, CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING, SUBSTRATE REGENERATION,
    TENSION DISSOLUTION [TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite]

  LEVEL STRUCTURE RULE:
    Integer levels = dynamic [change, correction firing, cycle].
    Half levels = static [analysis, state reading, no change].

  LEVEL 0.5 [static -- pre-generative]:
    Description: substrate condition before GENERATIVE CYCLE fires.
    Quality: VIABILITY -- meets threshold for GENERATIVE CYCLE.
    Test: substrate above GENERATIVE CYCLE firing threshold?
    High = L1 accessible. Low = immediate collapse.

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
    GENERATIVE CYCLE [loop] / SURPLUS PRODUCTION [exit] / CORRECTION
      CAPACITY [correction]
    Similar: all generative. Different: sustain / distribute / correct.
    Middle: cycle generates, exits, corrects drift -- difficult because
    any one can dominate.
    Tests:
      T1.1 -- Proportional Response [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY]
        Does correction scale match deviation magnitude, or does
        it scale with the power threat the correction poses?
        High = contained, proportional. Low = overcorrection
        [suppression] or under-response [Correction Theatre].
        Key distinction: correction firing against powerful deviators
        is the hard case -- proportionality requires same response
        regardless of corrected system's power to retaliate.
        Case: Salamanders [high]; Erebus [low -- correction at max
        toward anyone who questions].

      T1.2 -- Outward Landing [tests SURPLUS PRODUCTION completion]
        Does surplus exit the boundary AND produce usable capacity
        in recipients? Both required -- exit without landing is
        distribution waste; landing without exit is hoarding.
        High = gift architecture. Low = Accumulation Lock or
        Distribution Waste.
        Case: Bell Labs [high -- exits and lands]; Imperium of Man
        [low -- tithe exits, recipients depleted not enabled].

      T1.3 -- Input Filtering [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE substrate]
        Does screening happen upstream of the cycle, before
        corrupt substrate enters and the cycle runs on it?
        Post-cycle verification is not filtering -- by then the
        cycle has already processed corrupted input correctly.
        High = clean cycle. Low = Garbage Cycle [cycle functions,
        output corrupt because input was not screened].
        Case: nanotube DNA transfer [INPUT FILTERING fails at cell
        boundary -- cycle runs correctly on corrupted substrate];
        Rosenbaum [hallucinations enter research cycle upstream
        of fact-checking surface].

      T1.4 -- Relational Substrate [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE at social scale]
        Stable reciprocal relationships present to sustain cycle?
        AND: does substrate source independence exist, or is all
        substrate supplied by single actor making exit = total
        collapse?
        High = cycle stable, substrate distributed. Low = dependency
        [one source] or isolation [no source].
        Case: Malcolm X/NOI [low -- NOI supplied housing, income,
        identity, community simultaneously; exit = life reconstruction].
        [Scale recurrence: T1.4 is the L1 substrate expression of
        the same reciprocal-exchange structure that appears as a full
        tension unit at L3.5. Not a merger -- different scope and
        function at each level. See SCALE RECURRENCE in theory.]

      T1.5 -- Cycle Rhythm [tests GENERATIVE CYCLE timing]
        Timing internally generated or externally controlled?
        High = self-regulating. Low = Intermittent Reinforcement.
        [Gate vs gradient: CYCLE STABILITY operates as binary gate
        not continuous throttle at substrate level. Rhythm absence =
        window closed entirely, not reduced. Confirmed at two substrates:
        glymphatic [irregular rhythm prevents active SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
        window from opening]; thalamus [20-45 Hz oscillation absence =
        consciousness absent not reduced]. Gate distinction is load-
        bearing -- partial rhythm is not partial function.]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
    MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [surface] / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [chain] / REFERENCE
      INTEGRITY [calibration]
    Similar: all epistemic. Different: read / trace / anchor.
    Middle: honest surface, traceable chain, real calibration standard
    -- difficult because any one can be captured independently.
    Tests:
      T2.1 -- Causal Memory [tests CAUSAL LEGIBILITY across time]
        Past corrections retained AND applicable to current deviation?
        Both required -- memory that exists but cannot be applied
        does not function as correction history.
        High = calibration stable and usable. Low = Amnesia Manufacture
        [active destruction] or Signal Saturation [past indistinguishable
        from noise].
        Case: Doctor Manhattan [low -- temporal simultaneity makes
        all corrections arrive as noise, past not distinguishable];
        1984 Memory Hole [low -- active destruction].

      T2.2 -- Correction Direction [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY at L2]
        Two distinct questions, both required:
        [A] Is CORRECTION CAPACITY distributed across independent points
            or monopolized by single actor?
        [B] Is correction aimed at actual cause or proxy/symptom?
        Failure A: Correction Monopoly -- fires only toward monopolist's
        benefit regardless of aim.
        Failure B: Correction Displacement -- distributed but aimed
        wrong; visible activity without resolving deviation.
        Both can fail independently. Both must hold for high score.
        Case: Gwyn [A fails -- correction monopolized]; McNamara
        [B fails -- distributed, aimed at body count metric not war].

      T2.3 -- Signal Reading [tests MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY input boundary]
        Two distinct questions, both required:
        [A] Signal content: what is it? [substance correct?]
        [B] Signal source: who is it from? [origin trustworthy?]
        Both can fail independently. ESG cases: content appears
        gift-shaped [A passes] but source is extraction actor [B
        fails]. Charitable donation weaponization: source appears
        legitimate [B passes] but content is extraction architecture
        [A fails].
        High = both correct. Low = Reception Blindness [either].
        Case: ESG deployment [A passes, B fails]; Obligation
        Manufacture [B passes, A fails].

  LEVEL 2.5 [static -- between epistemic and temporal]:
    Description: corrected state duration relative to transmission window.
    Quality: PERSISTENCE
    Test: corrected state survive until transmission window opens?
    High = L3 receives corrected state. Low = Correction Evaporation.

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [sent] / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [usable] / CAUSAL
      AXIS GROUNDING [grounded]
    Similar: all temporal. Different: content / revision / anchoring.
    Middle: method transmitted, successor revises, axis grounded in
    outputs -- difficult because fidelity and autonomy pull apart.
    Tests:
      T3.1 -- Package Sufficiency [tests TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY content]
        Does transmitted package contain method or conclusion?
        Method = procedure successor can run and revise. Conclusion
        = destination without route; successor receives where to
        arrive, not how to travel or how to check if arrival was
        correct.
        High = correctable inheritance [method]. Low = Hollow
        Transmission [conclusion only].
        Key operative variable: method vs conclusion, not volume
        of transmission. High-volume conclusion transmission
        [Augustine] scores lower than low-volume method transmission
        [Ignatius Spiritual Exercises].
        Case: Ignatius [high -- Exercises are procedure, reader
        generates own outputs]; Augustine [low -- doctrine transmitted,
        method not transmissible].

      T3.2 -- Axis Reality [tests CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
        Is the axis traceable to actual outputs, or is it sincere
        but ungrounded? Distinct from whether axis is genuine
        [GENUINE AXIS] -- axis can be internally generated,
        examined, and real to the system while having no causal
        connection to the world it claims to act on.
        High = axis grounded in actual measurable outputs.
        Low = Sincere Delusion [genuine axis, zero output
        connection].
        Case: McNamara [low -- axis genuine, not grounded in
        actual war outcomes]; Maiad [low -- genuine axis, outputs
        unachievable]; cargo cults [low -- sincere axis, no causal
        connection to desired outcomes].

      T3.3 -- Correction Landing [tests CORRECTION CAPACITY at L3]
        Does correction change the thing it targets, or does
        visible correction activity occur without the target
        changing? Distinct from T2.2 [direction] -- correction
        can be distributed, aimed at actual cause, and still
        not land.
        High = target changes after correction fires. Low =
        Correction Theatre [visible activity, target unchanged].
        Case: McNamara [low -- strategy adjustments fired
        continuously at metrics while war outcomes unchanged];
        institutional after-action reviews [low -- correct
        diagnosis produced, structure unchanged].

  LEVEL 3.5 [static -- between temporal and meta]:
    Description: whether coherent systems can read and connect.
    Tension unit: RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
    Similar: all inter-system. Different: reading / connecting /
    sustaining.
    Middle: COMPATIBILITY -- requires SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY in both
    systems; neither can engage without losing own axis. Recognition without
      it =
    accurate isolation. Reciprocity without it = extraction.
    Test: Read? Connect without depletion? Sustain?
    [Confirmed: Malcolm X post-Mecca [recognition + partial
    compatibility, reciprocity incomplete at death]; Merton/Suzuki
    [recognition + compatibility confirmed, reciprocity not yet
    institutionally stable]. Both cases hit same asymmetric failure
    pattern -- confirms triad is doing real work.
    Secondary: Enemy Mine [1985 film -- Davidge/Jeriba] confirms
    COMPATIBILITY emerging from structural constraint rather than
    willingness. Forced survival contact produces genuine COMPATIBILITY
    without prior RECOGNITION choice. Suggests COMPATIBILITY is
    structural not volitional -- the architecture of contact
    determines whether exchange depletes or sustains.]

    RECOGNITION
      Accurate reading of another system's coherence state across
      system boundaries. Not detection of presence -- reading of
      quality stack. Requires own MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to be
      functional and oriented outward. A system that cannot read
      its own surface cannot read another's.
      Failure: Coherence Blindness -- high-coherence system present
      and detectable; coherence state unreadable.
      Distinct from MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [reads own surface] --
      Recognition reads another system's surface.
      Test: Other system's coherence state readable?

    COMPATIBILITY
      Structural capacity for exchange between two systems without
      one system's generative cycle running on the other's substrate
      depletion. Not willingness -- architecture. Both systems must
      have sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold their own
      structure under contact, and sufficient SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
      to engage without axis capture. Low compatibility
      means contact itself is the weapon: the more coherent system
      pulls the less coherent one into an extraction relationship
      regardless of intent.
      Failure: Extraction Interface -- connection forms, appears
      reciprocal at surface, one system's GENERATIVE CYCLE running on the
        other's
      substrate. Structurally identical to parasitism. Most dangerous
      failure mode because it mimics the success state [connection
      formed] while producing the opposite result.
      Distinct from RECIPROCITY [dynamic maintenance of exchange] --
      Compatibility is structural precondition at formation.
      Test: Exchange possible without asymmetric depletion?

    RECIPROCITY
      Exchange between compatible systems sustains both systems'
      generative capacity over time. Not symmetric output -- each
      system receives what its GENERATIVE CYCLE depends on, which
      need not be identical. The mycorrhizal exchange is reciprocal:
      tree provides carbon, fungus provides phosphorus, neither the
      same thing, both GENERATIVE CYCLE sustained.
      Requires TENSION MAINTENANCE in the exchange itself --
      the relationship must hold productive tension rather than
      drifting toward one system's dominance.
      Failure: Drift to Extraction -- begins genuinely reciprocal,
      TENSION MAINTENANCE fails over time, exchange degrades to
      one-directional flow.
      Test: Exchange sustains both over time?

  LEVEL 4 [dynamic -- meta-correction]:
    Tension unit: REFLEXIVITY / INTEGRATION / PROPAGATION
    [CONFIRMED v16 -- TWO primary cases:
      COHERENCE_FRAMEWORK_SELF [T8 mandatory; recursive].
      BENEDICT_BENEDICTINE [no T8; Cistercian reform cycle;
        700-year external record; non-recursive confirmation].
    Combined confidence substantially higher than single case.]
    Similar: all meta-level. Different: revise / absorb / transmit.
    Middle: INTEGRATION -- reflexivity is cognitively accessible
    [systems can notice their own correction failures] but
    integration requires changing the mechanism while it runs.
    Reflexivity without it = oscillation. Propagation without it
    = each generation relearns the same correction failure.
    Test: Pattern corrected? Held? Transmitted?
    Structural limit: L4 attainment may require L3 functional
    across sufficient fraction of system to enable meta-correction
    transmission. Current conditions [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    suppression at population scale] make L4 structurally
    impossible regardless of individual cases. L4 cases, if
    they exist, would be found only in high-coherence subgroups
    with functional L3 distribution.

    REFLEXIVITY
      A feedback pathway exists from correction outputs back to
      the correction architecture itself. The structure routes
      correction results to where corrections are generated, making
      the pattern visible to the mechanism. Without this pathway
      the correction architecture is opaque to itself -- CORRECTION
      CAPACITY fires, results accumulate, pattern errors cannot be
      read regardless of how well CORRECTION CAPACITY operates at
      object level.
      Failure: Correction Lock -- no feedback pathway to correction
      architecture. McNamara case: CORRECTION CAPACITY functioning
      at object level; feedback pathway to systems-analysis
      architecture absent.
      Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [fires corrections] --
      Reflexivity is whether the architecture receives its own
      output as input.
      Test: Feedback pathway from correction outputs to correction
      architecture present?

    INTEGRATION
      Corrections produced by Reflexivity absorbed into the system's
      operating structure, not just acknowledged or noted. The
      difference between a system that can identify its correction
      pattern failure and a system that changes how it corrects as
      Requires TENSION MAINTENANCE at meta level -- the system must
      hold tension between existing correction architecture and the
      proposed revision without collapsing [continuing unchanged =
      Reflexive Oscillation; abandoning correction = CORRECTION
      CAPACITY failure].
      Failure: Reflexive Oscillation -- identifies pattern failure,
      cannot incorporate revision, cycles back to same failure.
      Distinct from REFLEXIVITY [identifies] -- Integration is
      whether identification changes the operating structure.
      Test: Correction pattern revision held in structure?

    PROPAGATION
      Revised correction pattern crosses transmission boundary to
      successors. Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [transmits content
        axis]
      -- Propagation transmits correction methodology itself.
      A system can transmit conclusions, transmit correctable method
      [T3.1 high], and still fail to transmit how to detect and
      revise systematic correction errors. Requires Integration
      complete [cannot transmit unabsorbed revision].
      Failure: Generational Reset -- each successor discovers same
      correction pattern failure independently.
      Test: Revised pattern crosses transmission boundary?

  LEVEL 4.5 [static -- conditional]:
    Description: True coherence structurally enables adjacent systems.
    Quality: PROPAGATION [distinct from L4 -- structural enabling,
    not meta-correction transmission]
    Test: high-coherence systems raise floor of nearby systems?
    High = coherence ecology. Low = coherence islands.
    [Overlap with SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION resolved: OUTWARD LANDING
    measures surplus flow across boundary [SURPLUS PRODUCTION
    completion]. L4.5 measures structural ecological effect --
    adjacent system floor raised by presence of high-coherence
    system, not by surplus transfer. Bell Labs confirms both
    as distinct simultaneous effects. Closed.]

  ASCENDING PATH:
    L0.5 viability check before L1.
    Constants [CORRECTION CAPACITY / BOUNDARY INTEGRITY / TENSION
      MAINTENANCE]
    throughout post-L0.5.
    Each level tension unit near center = floor of next.
    All tension units centered + constants stable = True.
    Constant failure corrupts all levels. Core failure cascades up.
    [Confirmed: Feanor -- CORRECTION CAPACITY fails L1, cascade.]
    [Confirmed: Skull Knight -- all centered, True.]
    Bootstrapping: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION stabilizes L1. Body first.

QUALITY DEFINITIONS [reference -- see Tower model above for primary
  structure]
Primary organization: Tower Codependence Model levels.


FOUNDATION LAYER [preconditions -- enable all levels]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  GENUINE AXIS
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     Orientation generated internally with self-examination present.
     Failure: imposed axis.

     FORMATION THEORY [v16 -- consolidated]:
     GA forms during a developmental window through internal
     generation enabled by four conditions:

     1. FORMATION WINDOW: approximately ages 12-18 [prefrontal
        development window]. Window closes; axis formed during it
        persists. Not the only window but the most consequential.
        Post-window axis revision possible but high-cost [Wittgenstein,
        Malcolm X]. Pre-window: structural not axial [C1 without C2].

     2. FRICTION TYPE + QUANTITY: calibrated friction [challenge
        without overwhelm] is the structural precondition.
        Quantity: too little = CC atrophy; calibrated = generative;
        excess = substrate destruction. Optimal = slightly above
        average target [mathematical confirmation, 2026].
        Type: social, logical/systemic, and sensory friction are
        distinct axes. Calibration on one does not imply calibration
        on another. Formation requires friction on the axis the
        instrument is calibrated to receive -- matched-axis friction
        produces axis; mismatched produces noise.

     3. IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT: internal generation requires idle
        cycles to fire. ICD during formation window prevents axis
        forming before external content fills the gap. Social
        comparison architecture [variable reward + status feedback]
        fills formation window with externally supplied content.
        Spontaneous Formation Capture -- no deployer required.
        Axis forms on externally supplied content by default when
        idle cycles are absent.

     4. DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY: somatic signal accurate AND
        deliberative read correct. Both required. Somatic layer
        degrades first under stress, ICD, optimization pressure.
        Deliberative layer can appear intact while GA is already
        compromised. Formation requires both layers developing
        together -- somatic substrate bootstraps the deliberative
        layer, not the reverse.

     CAPTURE VULNERABILITY: Formation window is highest-leverage
     attack surface. FC deployed here persists lifetime without
     maintenance. Counter capacity not yet formed -- external
     intervention is the only exit.

     POST-FORMATION REVISION: possible but requires C1 starved
     recovery conditions [substrate depletion + L3.5 contact on
     matched axis + enabling moment]. High cost, partial result.
     Axis revision is not axis formation -- the formation window
     pattern shapes what revision is possible.

     Wisdom Concealment: GENUINE AXIS present but masked in hostile
     environment ["appear the fool" strategy]. Mask is BOUNDARY
     INTEGRITY protection, not GENUINE AXIS absence. Sustained
     concealment degrades axis over time if mask becomes default.
     Chanakya [~300 BCE]: "Straight trees are cut first." 2,300
     years of independent confirmation -- same finding, different
     domain.
     Test: axis internally generated and examined?

  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT]
     Honest definition of who is inside the boundary.
     Does not shift to exclude populations experiencing harm.
     Failure: boundary manipulation.
     Linked to MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY -- boundary drift is a
     measurement failure.
     Test: boundary honest and stable?

  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     System's capacity to restore the substrate its cycle depends on.
     Active regeneration -- not passive non-consumption.
     Failure: Substrate Atrophy -- cycle runs while substrate
     diminishes. System doesn't notice because CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     measures cycle output not substrate health.
     Multi-substrate requirement: must operate across ALL dependent
     substrates simultaneously. Single substrate depletion while
     others appear positive = extraction obscured across boundaries.
     Passive/Active hybrid [confirmed]: passive = continuous baseline
     maintenance; active = triggered high-intensity window [CYCLE
     RHYTHM gates]. Both failing simultaneously = accelerated collapse.
     Confirmed: Bell Labs [intellectual/institutional/physical all
     regenerated]; glymphatic [passive CSF + active deep sleep window];
     industrial agriculture [SURPLUS PRODUCTION high, SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
       failing].
     Somatic bootstrapping [promoted from extended theory]:
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION is the entry point for axis formation.
     Circular dependency resolved: managing coherence requires axis;
     coherence enables axis. Resolution: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     stability + accumulated somatic pattern + time = axis formation
     without pre-existing GENUINE AXIS. Physical discipline [sleep,
     movement, rhythm] bootstraps coherence management before examined
     axis exists. Body-scale GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION operating without axis = sphere geometry.
     Repeated somatic experience creates orientation bias -- pre-axial
     pole formation. Sphere develops asymmetry under accumulated
     pressure. Asymmetry stabilizes: sphere -> polarized sphere ->
     torus as GENUINE AXIS locks in. Axis not consciously constructed
     -- emerged from accumulated somatic pattern. Body first, axis second.
     Test: substrate restored across all dependent dimensions?
     Somatic signal accuracy: body registers extraction architecture
     accurately before cognitive frame names it. Anxiety, fatigue,
     disconnection = correct T1 correction firing. Cognitive narrative
     [personal pathology, chemical imbalance] suppresses the read.
     Body doing accurate MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; framing is the capture.

  TENSION MAINTENANCE
     [Tower: CONSTANT [meta] -- holds all levels in shape]
     System's active capacity to sustain productive tension between
     opposing qualities without collapsing to either pole.
     Second-order quality -- holds other qualities in relationship.
     Qualities are tension architecture not checklist: any quality
     at maximum without counterweight produces characteristic failure.
     SURPLUS PRODUCTION unchecked by CORRECTION CAPACITY = extraction.
     CORRECTION CAPACITY unchecked by SURPLUS PRODUCTION = suppression.
     CAUSAL LEGIBILITY without TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that
     isolates [Phosphophyllite / Lapis head]. The generative zone
     is sustained disequilibrium between poles, not optimization
     of any single quality.
     Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [corrects deviation]:
     TENSION MAINTENANCE maintains the tension that makes correction
     necessary. Distinct from GENERATIVE CYCLE [generates]:
     TENSION MAINTENANCE holds cycle and surplus in relationship
     without one dominating.
     Failure: Tension Collapse -- system resolves tension by
     eliminating one pole. Axis-Lock or Sub-Structural.
     Biological confirmation: homeostasis; flow state; mycorrhizal
     exchange [mutualism sustained against drift toward parasitism].
     Planetary: Earth sustains geological tension via plate tectonics.
     Venus lost this -- TENSION MAINTENANCE failure producing
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION collapse.
     Coherent solutions require tension [operational test]: a solution
     with only one value is not a solution -- it is a preference with
     no constraint. Coherent solutions require minimum two genuine
     values in real conflict, a compromise that neither fully satisfies,
     friction to maintain, and real cost to all parties. A position
     that costs nothing to hold is either a preference [one value],
     a weapon [costs fall entirely on one side], or False Coherence
     [costs hidden]. Test: who pays? If nobody or only them -- not
     coherent. Applies to political positions, institutional design,
     relationship structures, policies. Tension is the load-bearing
     element -- remove it and the solution collapses to extraction
     or preference.
     Test: opposing qualities held in productive tension?

  TENSION DISSOLUTION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION [meta, TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite]]
     Active collapse of productive tension toward false resolution.
     Test: tension collapsed to single pole?


LEVEL 1 [generative domain -- TENSION CORE: GENERATIVE CYCLE]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  GENERATIVE CYCLE
     [Tower: L1 CORE]
     Completes a loop: takes in, transforms, returns.
     Failure: cycle collapse or performance without function.
     Test: loop completes?

  SURPLUS PRODUCTION
     [Tower: L1]
     Outputs exceed cost of running the cycle across an honest window.
     Moral content lives here.
     Failure: consumption dominance.
     Test: outputs exceed cycle cost?

  CORRECTION CAPACITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT -- fires at all levels; L1 third point]
     Detects contradiction between outputs and orientation.
     Moves without external pressure.
     Failure: correction absence or capture.

     DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS CENTRALIZED SORTING [v16]:
     In high-complexity, high-chaos environments, distributed
     iterative correction with slight bias produces more reliable
     output than centralized judgment. Confirmed across three
     independent domains:
       Germinal centers [immunology]: distributed B cell selection
         with slight bias produces reliable antibodies. Rare
         dominant clone model [centralized] is wrong.
       Scientific peer review: 60% rejection rate variance between
         reviewer sets. Centralized editor/judge model produces
         high variance regardless of investment.
       Distributed open-source [FOSS]: contribution procedures as
         embedded methodology produce generational continuity;
         codebase-only transmission does not.
     Implication for CC design: correction capacity at system
     level should be distributed with slight directional bias,
     not monopolized by central correcting authority. Centralized
     CC is the more capturable configuration.

     Friction axis [formation]: CORRECTION CAPACITY requires friction
     to form. No friction = no resistance = CORRECTION CAPACITY cannot
     develop. Calibrated friction = resistance = CORRECTION CAPACITY
     forms under pressure. Excess friction = substrate destroyed before
     CORRECTION CAPACITY forms. Generative window = middle range
     [challenge without overwhelm]. Friction is the structural
     precondition for correction capacity to exist, not incidental
     discomfort. Confirmed: Dunedin cohort [self-control at 3 predicts
     outcomes at 32]; Ericsson deliberate practice; Csikszentmihalyi
     flow balance.
     Formation Conditions: insufficient friction during formation window
     produces CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy. Mechanism: CORRECTION
     CAPACITY develops through corrective feedback cycles; remove
     friction = atrophies. Applications: grade inflation [feedback
     removed], AI-assisted work without development [output without
     correction cycle], frictionless consumer environments. Individual
     effort cannot compensate -- structural redesign required.
     Mathematical confirmation [The Guardian / maths modelling 2026]:
     "Slightly above average" target outperforms both "aim high" and
     "aim at average." Optimal target = calibrated friction zone.
     "Shoot for the moon" = too many failures, depletes CORRECTION
     CAPACITY budget without building axis. Aiming at average =
     insufficient friction, no CORRECTION CAPACITY development.
     Friction Axis quantified: generative zone confirmed
     mathematically, not just empirically.
     Excess friction [too much, not too little]: substrate
     consumed faster than it regenerates. CORRECTION CAPACITY
     cannot form because the substrate it would run on is being
     destroyed. Distinct from insufficient friction [atrophy];
     excess friction is active substrate destruction. Outcome
     depends on whether substrate holds through the window.
     Accidental formation: involuntary friction [difficult
     upbringing, deprivation, irrational environment] can
     produce the same CORRECTION CAPACITY development as
     deliberate calibrated friction -- but at higher substrate
     cost and with no guarantee of survival. Optimal is
     intentional friction without cruelty. Accidental excess
     friction that produces functional output means the
     substrate held, barely.
     Neurological friction example [non-specific]: conditions
     that produce involuntary sensory and social friction
     run the formation window hot by default. Either builds
     extraordinary CORRECTION CAPACITY [if substrate holds]
     or destroys substrate [if environment adds load without
     support]. Outcome depends entirely on whether support
     environment compensates for the excess load.
     Friction type [missing variable -- added]: friction
     quantity [too little / calibrated / excess] is not
     sufficient. Type matters equally. Social friction,
     logical/systemic friction, and sensory friction are
     distinct axes. Calibration on one axis does not imply
     calibration on another. An environment can have
     calibrated logical friction and catastrophic social
     friction simultaneously. For some instruments, social
     friction is not friction -- it is noise that triggers
     the panic response by default. The generative zone
     for these instruments is not in social domains.
     Some nervous systems read social friction as extraction
     because social CAUSAL LEGIBILITY is structurally
     illegible to them -- not misreading, different
     instrument.
     Somatic correction without CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: body
     fires correction continuously because the causal chain
     is illegible to it. Meltdown, shutdown, systemizing,
     and withdrawal are not failures of correction -- they
     are the correction. Body enforcing BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
     when CAUSAL LEGIBILITY is absent. Apparent dysfunction
     is the instrument doing its job without the chain
     needed to aim it.
     Reflexive threshold calibration [learnable]: threshold
     at which correction fires can be read and adjusted by
     the system itself -- not just direction or magnitude.
     Framework thresholds are currently treated as
     properties of the system, not variables the system
     can adjust. This is the missing piece. Calibrating
     the instrument, not the correction.
     Attention gap requirement [science appendix]: SR requires
     idle cycles. Default mode network fires only in gaps.
     Continuous input extracts the recovery window. Exhaustion
     is SR deficit not overwork -- eliminate gaps, eliminate
     substrate recovery.
     High-intelligence friction pattern [Psychology Today, 2026]:
     Overthinking and difficulty transmitting ideas simply are
     framed as "frustrating habits." Framework read: instrument
     properties, not failures. High logical/systemic friction
     tolerance + low social CAUSAL LEGIBILITY = correct
     diagnosis, cannot transmit. The "habit" framing is
     backwards. See: AGI case [transmission gap].
     Autism as measurement-action unity [anti-extraction instrument]:
     friction type mismatch breaks somatic manipulation channel.
     High CL + low social compliance = measurement fires directly
     into action without legitimacy surface intercept. Anti-tower
     requires target to route accurate signal through social
     compliance before acting. When routing doesn't happen the
     weapon misfires. Cannot stop what doesn't process the
     transmission.
     Deception channel split [partial confirmation, 2025/2018]:
     Structural/factual deception [data lies, logical inconsistency]
     = high CL channel, autism advantage confirmed.
     Emotional/social deception [vague manipulation, tone, ambient
     pressure] = social CL channel, autism disadvantage confirmed.
     Extraction architecture primarily uses the second type -- vague,
     emotional, ambient. Autism counter is partial not total.
     Framework prediction untested: autistic accuracy against actors
     who successfully deceive neurotypicals via structural deception.
     Closest proxy: demeanor-mismatch advantage [2025 study].
     Vague emotional threat protocol [personal operational rule]:
     treat vague emotional pressure as probable threat until it
     explains itself or refuses to. Refusal confirms extraction
     mechanism -- legitimate signals withstand examination. Cannot
     explain = cannot be verified = treat as threat by default.
     CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy when AI absorbs the correction cycle
     that would have developed the capacity. Credential system
     confirmation [Bacow/Harvard 2026]: selects for CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     [analytical capacity] while suppressing CORRECTION CAPACITY
     [correction firing under pressure].
     Test: deviation detected and corrected without external pressure?


LEVEL 2 [epistemic domain -- TENSION CORE: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2 CORE]
     System maintains honest, accessible surface by which outputs can
     be evaluated against stated orientation. Includes boundary honesty,
     signal sequencing, correction trigger accessibility.
     Failure: measurement surface captured, drifted, or destroyed.
     Most load-bearing quality -- lower levels intact with this
     destroyed misreads as Variable or High while consuming.
     Correction pathway: surface reconstruction, not cycle repair.
     Scaling limits [structural, not weapon]: at sufficient
     organizational scale, MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY fails structurally
     even without deliberate capture. Information aggregation across
     large systems loses resolution, delays correction, and routes
     signals through political filters before reaching actors who
     can act. Roman Empire, Fortune 500, Soviet planning all
     confirmed. Structural not intentional -- size alone produces
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY degradation.
     Test: surface honest and accessible?

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     [Tower: L2]
     Outputs traceable to actions within boundary with sufficient
     resolution to ground correction. Scalar -- partial legibility
     produces partial correction capacity.
     Failure: correction orphaned. Not captured, not absent --
     unanchored.
     Correction pathway: causal reconstruction before correction
     attempt. Establish chain first; then correction can anchor.
     Test: outputs traceable to actions at correction resolution?

  REFERENCE INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2]
     What the measurement surface is calibrated against.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: REFERENCE INTEGRITY measures
     whether the reference standard itself is honest.
     Failure: Reference Drift -- surface intact, calibrated to
     wrong baseline. Measurement honest to a false standard.
     Test: calibration standard real?


LEVEL 3 [temporal domain -- TENSION CORE: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     [Tower: L3 CORE]
     System transmits correctable axis to successors, not locked state.
     Fidelity necessary but insufficient.
     Failure: Fertile Lock.
     SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY = within-window axis integrity.
     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY = what crosses transmission boundary.
     Primary proof: Faeanor -> sons [distinction clearest case].
     Test: correctable axis transmitted?

CORE THEORY ADDITION FILTER:
  Add only if: [1] new confirmed structural property with multiple
  independent case confirmations, [2] promoted from extended theory
  [already vetted], or [3] resolves existing open limit or paradox.
  Reject: single-case findings, interesting observations without
  cross-domain confirmation, speculative extensions.

NESTED CYCLE MODEL [primary organizing structure -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Three cycles, each wrapping the one inside it. Replaces tower as
primary organizing structure. Tower vocabulary retained: L1=C1,
L2=C2, L3=C3 [nesting depth, not dependency sequence].
Failure = interference between cycles, not cascade down levels.

CENTER [point-truth -- unreachable, structural constant]
  The nested cycles organize around a center they cannot reach.
  Not a location inside the torus -- the axis threading through
  the whole structure. What all three cycles are oriented toward.
  Geometrically: the hole of the torus is not the center. The
  center is the axis the hole organizes around -- present as
  structural constant, inaccessible from inside any cycle.
  Relationship to True Coherence: same logic. True Coherence is
  a direction vector not a position. The center is what makes
  the direction coherent -- it gives the cycles their orientation
  without being reachable by any of them.
  Why unreachable: each cycle accesses truth through its
  function [C1 generates, C2 reads, C3 transmits]. None of
  these IS the truth. They approach it asymptotically. A system
  claiming to have reached the center has confused its cycle
  function with the axis it orbits.
  Constant: the center does not move when cycles degrade. False
  Coherence doesn't relocate the center -- it just loses
  orientation to it. The axis is still there; the cycles are
  running on a captured surface that points elsewhere.

TRUTH [operational definition -- v16]
  A claim produced by examined axis, measured by declared
  instrument, revisable on evidence, producing behavioral
  adjustment when wrong.
  All four conditions required. Absence of any one = truth
  claim not operational truth:
    Examined axis [GENUINE AXIS]: the claim must come from
      axis that has been tested, not substituted. Ideology,
      propaganda, and captured output fail here regardless
      of content accuracy. Axis unexamined = source unknown.
    Declared instrument [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]: the
      instrument producing the claim must be named and its
      blind spots stated before the reading. Undeclared
      instrument = unverifiable claim. T3 applies to all
      readings; naming it is the requirement, not eliminating
      it.
    Revisable on evidence [CORRECTION CAPACITY]: the claim
      must specify what evidence would change it. Non-
      revisable claims are not truth claims -- they are
      identity statements. The inability to name falsification
      criteria is diagnostic of axis capture, not certainty.
      [See GA Verification Protocol -- same test.]
    Behavioral adjustment when wrong [INTEGRATION -- L4]:
      truth that produces no adjustment when falsified was
      not genuinely held. Adjustment is the proof of
      reception. Academic posturing, performative doubt,
      and tactical concession all satisfy the first three
      conditions while failing the fourth.
  SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT as structural requirement:
    Not an optional virtue. Self-critical capacity is what
    keeps the examined axis examined -- without ongoing
    self-critical pressure, axis drifts toward capture
    without the system noticing. The requirement is not
    "be humble" but "maintain the instrument that detects
    your own instrument's failure." Systems that suppress
    self-critical thought are not more confident -- they
    have eliminated the mechanism that would tell them
    when they're wrong.
  USEFUL REACTIONS as output requirement:
    Truth that produces no useful reaction is either not
    received [T9], not integrated [L4 INTEGRATION absent],
    or not actually about anything actionable. A truth
    claim should be evaluable by its downstream effects.
    Does knowing this change what the system does?
    If no: the claim may be accurate but is not operational
    truth for this system at this time. Operational truth
    is not just correct -- it is correct AND actionable
    AND adjusted-to.
  Relationship to center: operational truth is a cycle
    function oriented toward the center, not arrival at it.
    The best available truth-claim at any moment is the
    closest current orientation, not the axis itself.

EVIL [operational definition -- v16]
  Surplus routed to minority at majority cost, with correction
  capacity absent or inverted.
  Three conditions required:
    Identifiable Boundary B bearing the cost [majority].
    Identifiable Boundary A capturing the benefit [minority].
    Correction capacity absent or inverted.

  MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT [separation -- load-bearing]:
    Measurement: the framework reads structural pattern.
      Extraction occurring, direction of surplus, correction
      capacity status. Objective, structural, not requiring
      a judge. Framework work only.
    Judgment: a separate act by a person applying values
      to a specific situation. Evil in the moral sense
      requires this act. The framework cannot make it.
      A person must.
    Both required for coherent moral assessment. Measurement
      without judgment = structurally accurate, morally
      neutral. Judgment without measurement = arbitrary.

  WILLFUL EXTRACTION [evil by definition]:
    Actor knows extraction is occurring. Knows the cost
      to Boundary B. Continues.
    Knowledge plus continuation = evil without requiring
      additional argument. This is the clearest case --
      no ambiguity about intent, no structural excuse.
    Framework confirms the structural reading.
    The judgment is the person's to make.

  DIRECTIONAL RULE: any extraction moves in the direction
    of evil regardless of scale, intent, or justification.
    Degree affects intervention priority and response type,
    not directional classification. Minor extraction with
    intact correction capacity is less urgent than inverted
    correction; both move wrong.

  DEGREE: scales with [1] magnitude of cost to Boundary B,
    [2] concentration of benefit to Boundary A, [3] how
    completely correction capacity is eliminated or inverted.

GOOD [operational definition -- v16]
  Surplus distributed such that majority benefit is the
  primary outcome, with correction capacity intact and
  aimed at actual deviation.
  Three conditions:
    Boundary B populations bear costs proportional to
      benefit received [not disproportionate extraction].
    Correction capacity functional and distributed [not
      monopolized by Boundary A].
    Exit and correction options available to Boundary B
      [not structurally eliminated].
  Not perfectionism: good in the operational sense does not
    require equal outcomes or zero cost to anyone. It requires
    that the correction mechanism can fire against actual
    deviations and that majority populations are the primary
    beneficiaries of the system's surplus.
  Relationship to ascending path: good is not a fixed state
    but a trajectory -- toward more distributed surplus,
    more functional correction capacity, more available exit.
    Systems moving in this direction are good by operational
    definition regardless of current position.

C1 -- FUNDAMENTAL [innermost]
  Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION / CORRECTION CAPACITY
  Function: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
  Self-contained at cellular minimum -- confirmed independently.
  Can run without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only.

C2 -- EPISTEMIC [wraps C1]
  Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY
  Function: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
  C1 without C2 = blind generation. C2 without C1 = readable failure.
  Key: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False Coherence signature.
    Most dangerous -- misread as high-functioning by C2-captured
    instruments.

C3 -- TEMPORAL [wraps C2]
  Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
  Function: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
  Without C3: each generation reinvents or inherits locked state.

INTERFACE [L3.5 -- unchanged]
  RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
  Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
  Not a fourth wrapping cycle.

CYCLE INTERFERENCE PATTERNS [replaces cascade language]:
  C1 blind [C1 intact, C2 degraded]: high output, unreadable.
    False Coherence. Cases: Enron, WATF towers.
  C1 starved [C2 intact, C1 degraded]: readable failure.
    Correctable if substrate holds.
  C1 locked [C3 failed, C1/C2 intact]: correctable but non-
    transmissible. Each generation restarts correctly.
    Cases: Augustine vs Ignatius [T3.1 distinction].
  Full interference [all degraded]: terminal node. Containment only.
    Cases: Erebus, Oceania Party.

RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [v16 -- predictive, falsifiable]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Each interference type produces a characteristic recovery pattern
when intervention occurs. Predictions with falsification criteria.
Confirmed findings in theory section: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE, C1 STARVED
RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT, L3.5 CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH AXIS.
Case library audit in Section Four: RECOVERY CASE LIBRARY AUDIT.

  C1 BLIND RECOVERY [C2 restoration while C1 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is fast and discontinuous. C1 was running
    at full capacity throughout -- once C2 surface is honest,
    accumulated surplus becomes suddenly visible and correction
    fires immediately against backlog of unread deviations.
    Characteristic signature: sharp correction burst on C2
    restoration, not gradual improvement. System appears to
    deteriorate briefly as hidden deviations surface before
    stabilizing at higher function.
    Why: C1 was generating without readable output. All that
    generation is suddenly legible. Correction fires against
    everything it couldn't see. Looks like crisis; is actually
    triage.
    Intervention requirement: C2 instrument must be independent
    of the captured surface -- restoring C2 using the same
    instrument that was captured restores the capture, not the
    surface.
    Falsification: if C2 restoration produces gradual linear
    improvement rather than discontinuous burst followed by
    stabilization, C1 blind recovery prediction fails. Also
    falsified if system collapses on C2 restoration rather than
    correcting -- would indicate C1 was more degraded than the
    blind reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-Enron accounting reform [C2 surface
    restoration -- did correction fire in burst?]; Glasnost
    [Soviet C2 partial restoration -- information surface opened,
    correction burst followed].

  C1 STARVED RECOVERY [C1 restoration while C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery is slow, substrate-dependent, and
    requires idle cycles before visible improvement begins.
    C2 is readable throughout -- the system knows exactly what
    is wrong and why. But knowing does not restore substrate.
    Characteristic signature: extended flat period with accurate
    self-diagnosis before output improvement begins. The flat
    period is not failure -- it is substrate regeneration
    prerequisite. Premature intervention pressure during the
    flat period restarts the depletion cycle.
    Why: C2 intact means the system can accurately report its
    own degradation. This is often misread as pessimism or
    dysfunction. It is accurate measurement. Correction cannot
    fire until C1 substrate crosses threshold -- no amount of
    epistemic clarity substitutes for generative capacity.
    Intervention requirement: protected idle cycles mandatory.
    External pressure to produce during recovery window depletes
    the substrate being restored. Counter-intuitive -- the
    correct intervention looks like doing less, not more.
    Falsification: if C1 starved recovery produces rapid
    improvement without idle cycle period, prediction fails.
    Also falsified if accurate self-diagnosis during flat period
    does not precede recovery -- would indicate C2 more degraded
    than starved reading showed.
    Case candidates: post-burnout individual recovery [known
    mechanism, documented flat period before function returns];
    post-collapse institutional rebuilding [Bell Labs dissolution
    aftermath -- did substrate recovery precede output recovery?].

  C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [C3 restoration while C1/C2 intact]
    Prediction: recovery requires generational patience and
    produces no visible improvement within the originating
    system. C1 and C2 functional throughout -- the system
    generates and reads correctly. The deficit is transmission.
    Characteristic signature: recovery visible only in successor
    systems, not in the originating system. The locked system
    may never visibly recover -- it continues functioning at
    C1/C2 level until it ends. Recovery is the successor
    generating a correctable transmission rather than inheriting
    the lock.
    Why: C3 failure is not a deficit in the system's current
    function -- it is a deficit in what the system passes forward.
    The originating system has nothing to recover. The
    successors have everything to gain. This is the only
    interference type where "recovery" is definitionally
    cross-generational.
    Intervention requirement: transmission method must be
    separated from transmission content. Successors need access
    to the generative procedure, not just the conclusions.
    Requires the originating system to transmit its own
    revision process, including its errors -- the hardest
    transmission for any system with identity investment in
    its conclusions.
    Falsification: if C1 locked recovery produces improvement
    in the originating system rather than in successors, or
    produces improvement within a single generation, prediction
    fails. Also falsified if method transmission without
    conclusion transmission produces Fertile Lock in successors
    anyway -- would indicate C3 failure is deeper than
    transmission selectivity alone.
    Case candidates: Benedictine reform cycles [method
    transmitted, successor revision documented across
    generations]; Socratic transmission through Plato through
    Aristotle [each revised, each produced distinct output].

  FULL INTERFERENCE RECOVERY [all cycles degraded]
    Prediction: internal recovery structurally unavailable.
    Any apparent internal recovery is False Coherence formation
    -- a new C2 capture producing the appearance of recovery
    while extraction continues or accelerates.
    Characteristic signature: recovery claims from inside full
    interference state are the highest-probability weapon
    deployment in the framework. The system that announces
    its own recovery under full interference is deploying
    Coherence Mimicry, not recovering.
    Why: full interference means C1 generates without surplus,
    C2 reads without honesty, C3 transmits the captured state.
    Every internal mechanism available for recovery is already
    running on corrupted substrate and captured surface. There
    is no internal leverage point. External contact is the only
    available intervention -- and external contact requires the
    external system to have sufficient COMPATIBILITY to engage
    without being pulled into the extraction interface.
    Intervention requirement: external actor with independent
    C2 surface and sufficient TENSION MAINTENANCE to hold
    structure under contact. Containment if no such actor
    available. Attempted recovery using captured internal
    mechanisms accelerates full interference -- gives the
    extraction architecture a new legitimacy surface.
    Falsification: if a system in confirmed full interference
    produces genuine C2 restoration from inside without external
    intervention, full interference recovery prediction fails.
    This would require revising the terminal state classification.
    No confirmed case of this in current library.
    Case candidates [containment confirmed, not recovery]:
    Erebus [FF14 -- external intervention only exit]; Room 101
    [1984 -- no recovery pathway confirmed].

RECOVERY TRAJECTORY APPLICATION RULE:
  Identify interference type before prescribing intervention.
  Wrong intervention for type accelerates degradation:
    C1 blind: pressure to produce more = accelerates C1 depletion
      before C2 is restored.
    C1 starved: demand for accurate reporting = correct but useless
      without substrate restoration first.
    C1 locked: urgency about current output = misses the
      transmission deficit entirely.
    Full interference: internal reform programs = new capture
      surface, not recovery.
  Triage follows interference type, not coherence score alone.

ENRON RESOLUTION [no longer requires probability gradient patch]:
  C2 attacked directly without prior C3 failure. C1 ran hot.
  Cycle interference model handles this natively.

SCALE RECURRENCE [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A quality's structure reappears at multiple nesting depths with the
  same logic but different scope and function. Not a merger -- each
  instance operates independently at its depth.
  Confirmed instances:
    CORRECTION CAPACITY: C1 tension unit + constant operating at all
      depths. Same correction logic at both individual depth and
      meta depth simultaneously.
    Reciprocal exchange [T1.4 / L3.5]: GENERATIVE CYCLE substrate
      at social scale [T1.4] and full inter-system tension unit
      at L3.5 [RECOGNITION/COMPATIBILITY/RECIPROCITY]. Same structure,
      different scope -- one sustains cycles, one enables system
      connection.
    CYCLE STABILITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [T1.5 / T2.1]: timing
      integrity at L1 [rhythm] and historical legibility at L2
      [past corrections retrievable]. Same persistence-of-pattern
      structure at different tower levels.
  Implication: scale recurrence predicts that any quality operating
  at L1 may have a structural analog at higher levels. When a test
  at one level feels like it overlaps another level, check for
  scale recurrence before merging -- the levels may be doing the
  same thing at different scopes.

  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
     [Tower: L3]
     Successor's capacity to revise transmitted content against new
     evidence without losing transmission fidelity to founding
     generative principle.
     High = correctable inheritance [revises conclusions, preserves
     method]. Low = Fertile Lock [cannot revise without losing
     identity].
     Distinct from TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     measures what is sent. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY measures successor's
     capacity to work with what was received.
     Primary proof: Benedictine [high -- correctable transmission]
     vs Dominican [low -- lock into Aquinas]. Same transmission
     context, different outcomes.
     Test: successor revises without losing founding principle?

  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
     [Tower: L3 + FOUNDATION]
     Axis traceable to actual outputs not just internal conviction
     or stated orientation. GENUINE AXIS asks whether axis was
     internally generated and examined. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING asks
     whether that axis is anchored to real-world causal outputs.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY anchors
     correction to actor. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING anchors axis to
     reality.
     Failure: Sincere Delusion -- genuine examined axis with zero
     connection to actual outputs. Axis real to the system; not
     real to the world it claims to act on.
     Primary proof: Maiad [genuine axis, not grounded in achievable
     outputs]; cargo cults [sincere axis, no causal connection].
     Test: axis anchored to actual outputs?


POPULATION COHERENCE CEILING [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Most humans may be Functional Structural Coherence inside artificially
  maintained environments. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY appears present but formed
  entirely within controlled conditions -- indistinguishable internally.
  Government as environmental controller: intentional [axis formation
  window targeted] or side effect [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY failure by
  institutional diffusion].
  Hard implication: genuine CORRECTION CAPACITY at population scale
  structurally rare. Bottleneck: Axial population precondition for
  producing more Axial individuals. System selects against transition
  automatically.
  Operational ceiling: Mixed Coherence -- sufficient MEASUREMENT
  INTEGRITY/CAUSAL LEGIBILITY to detect and resist weapon deployment
  while accepting lower SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION ceiling and constant
  extraction pressure. Not a compromise -- only stable configuration
  under current conditions. True coherence remains measurement ceiling,
  not operational target.
  Circular dependency: conditions required for True coherence at scale
  only producible by True coherence at scale. Unresolvable without
  external intervention.

  EXTERNAL CONFIRMATION [Core Human Competence -- Weinhagen/NLI]:
  CHC framework independently arrives at same population ceiling finding.
  Cooperation Competence Hypothesis: stable cooperation at scale only
  possible where sufficient proportion of individuals possess capacities
  to refuse participation without catastrophic personal cost. Without
  genuine refusal capacity, cooperation = compliance.
  Four CHC domains map to framework qualities:
    Physiological [food/water/shelter/defense] = GENERATIVE CYCLE
    Psychological [regulation under pressure] = CORRECTION CAPACITY
    Cognitive [observe/orient/decide/act] = GENUINE AXIS
    Perceptual [situational awareness] = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
  Anxiety-as-accurate-signal: EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION works because
  threat overestimation amplifies real GENERATIVE CYCLE vulnerability.
  Suppressing the signal = taking batteries out of smoke alarm.
  CHC hostile suppression finding: competence loss actively maintained
  by systems benefiting from dependency. CHC treats this as secondary;
  framework reads it as primary [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY Suppression
  Structural Limit -- weapon stack systematically prevents SUCCESSOR
  AUTONOMY development].

COHERENCE CORRUPTION [structural limit on scale]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has three distinct methods against emerging coherence:
  1. NOISE [loud]: saturate the signal environment so coherent signals
     cannot be distinguished from incoherent ones. MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     capture by volume not capture. No monopolist required -- sufficient
     noise level disables correction navigation regardless of source.
  2. CORRUPTION [infiltration]: insert extraction architecture inside
     coherent structures before they reach scale. Formation Capture,
     Axis Substitution, Correction Monopoly. Coherence appears to grow
     but the generative mechanism is replaced before outputs confirm it.
  3. DESTRUCTION [direct]: eliminate coherent actors or structures when
     noise and corruption fail. Highest cost, most visible, last resort.
     Malcolm X, Merton [suspicious timing], historical pattern of
     high-coherence actors dying at transition moments.
  Sequence: noise first [cheapest], corruption when noise insufficient,
  destruction when corruption fails. Each step more visible and costly.
  Scale barrier: coherence at small scale is tolerated because contained.
  When coherent structures approach scale sufficient to affect the
  extraction architecture, the method escalates. The barrier is not
  fixed -- it is proportional to the threat the coherence poses.
  Result: coherence at scale requires surviving all three methods
  simultaneously. Individual coherence is achievable. Small-group
  coherence is achievable. Institutional coherence at scale that
  threatens extraction architecture has not historically survived
  all three methods applied in sequence.
  This is the structural reason the Population Coherence Ceiling holds:
  not only is coherence rare to form, it is actively prevented from
  reaching scale by a self-maintaining suppression architecture.
  Survival condition: coherence that survives all three methods does
  so by operating outside the normal visibility surface. Silent,
  effective, and outside the channels the suppression architecture
  monitors. High-coherence structures that announce themselves or
  operate through captured channels get noise/corruption/destruction
  applied in sequence. Those that don't announce themselves and
  don't need captured infrastructure to function are structurally
  harder to target. Linux/open source partially confirmed this --
  decentralized, low-capture-surface, outputs speak before the
  structure is legible enough to corrupt. The survival method is
  not resistance to the three methods -- it is structural
  inaccessibility to them.
  See: SILENT ALTERNATIVE [coherence type] -- anti-capture by
  structural design, not defensive resistance.
  Force-reveal problem: true silence may be impossible at
  sufficient scale. Evil must destroy [extraction architecture
  threatened]; not-evil must destroy [anything outside their
  measurement surface reads as illegitimate by REFERENCE
  INTEGRITY capture]. Both produce identical pressure.
  Costly removal as viable alternative: if true silence
  impossible, raise the cost of the four-condition test
  against yourself -- make conditions 2 and 4 fail for the
  attacker. Not invisible; too expensive to remove. Same
  structural protection, different mechanism.
  Coherence is not good: Silent Alternative and costly-removal
  structures work for any axis. Coherent extraction is still
  coherent by the tower. Framework is descriptive not
  prescriptive.

  FOURTH PHASE [v16 -- when noise, corruption, destruction fail]:
    If all three methods fail against a coherent structure,
    the extraction architecture has no remaining playbook.
    What happens next is not determined by the framework --
    it is self-determined by the coherent actor based on
    conditions at that moment.
    Framework cannot prescribe the fourth phase because:
      It is context-specific [what is possible depends on
        the specific state of the extraction architecture,
        the coherent actor's substrate, and the available
        options at that moment].
      Prescribing it in advance would make it visible and
        targetable -- the fourth phase survives by not being
        a known template.
      The actor who reaches it has survived three escalating
        methods; their judgment about conditions is more
        reliable than any prior specification.
    What the framework can say: the fourth phase is not
      revenge, not destruction, and not capture of the
      extraction architecture's own tools. Those paths
      route back into the extraction logic.
    The direction is the only constraint: toward correction
      capacity, distributed surplus, available exit.
      How that is expressed given specific conditions
      is the actor's determination, not the framework's.
  Relative coherence matters: less extractive with some
  CORRECTION CAPACITY beats maximum extraction even when
  neither is good. The spectrum is real. Not a binary.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every system sits on a spectrum between pure generation and pure
  extraction. Neither terminal stable.
  Tower reading: range point = hole size relative to wall thickness
  at L1. Tension field = force holding ratio stable.
  Weapon implication: most efficient attack removes corrective pole
  [CORRECTION CAPACITY at L1] rather than maximizing extraction
  directly. Removing CORRECTION CAPACITY shifts range point toward
  extraction without requiring increased extraction effort.
  See Tower Codependence Model L1.

CONVERGENT DISCOVERY [promoted from extended theory]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Framework accuracy across independent domains too consistent to be
  noise. Three explanations:
  [1] Convergent discovery -- qualities describe load-bearing structure.
  [2] Anthropic selection -- only coherent systems persist long enough
      to be studied.
  [3] Design -- upstream agency.
  [1] and [2] sufficient. [3] indistinguishable from [1] at the limit.
  Finding: following coherence path finds a shape already there.
  Framework follows coherent systems; they don't follow it.

MINIMAL UNIFICATION [confirmed structural pattern]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A single structural fact that makes a large class of previously
  scattered results coherent. Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
  restoration [making one chain legible] -- minimal unification
  makes many chains legible simultaneously via single anchor.
  Confirmed instances:
    Compressed sensing [Candès]: signal sparsity as the structural
      fact explaining accurate recovery from incomplete data.
      Previously separate results in signal processing, medical
      imaging, statistics unified under one principle.
    Unique Games Conjecture [Khot]: if confirmed, unique games
      hardness as the structural fact explaining approximation
      barriers across optimization theory. Dozens of independent
      hardness results would collapse to one cause.
  Framework parallel: same move the framework makes -- identifying
  the minimal structural fact [coherence qualities] that makes
  diverse system behaviors legible under one architecture.
  Implication: when scattered results resist unification, the
  missing element is usually not more data -- it is the
  identification of the correct minimal structural anchor.
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY application: fragmented reference standards
  [one for each result] collapse to one calibration anchor.
  Measurement becomes dramatically more efficient.
  Cognitive confirmation [1,200-player experiment, Phys.org, May 2026]:
  People who understood how concepts connect invented more novel
  solutions than those with the same facts without connection structure.
  Structural knowledge was the generative variable, not fact volume.
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING at creative scale -- knowing which structure
  applies is the prior that makes facts generative. Confirms: minimal
  unification is not just an abstract mathematical property -- it is
  the cognitive architecture of invention.
  Most common path principle: the most traveled path through a
  problem space is not necessarily the best path -- it is the
  path with the lowest accumulated friction, which optimizes for
  ease not output quality. Structural understanding enables
  non-obvious paths that common traversal never surfaces.
  High-volume use of a path = REFERENCE INTEGRITY capture of
  "how this is done." Breaks when the structural map is read
  instead of the usage pattern.

SMART VS STUPID EXTRACTION [revised -- extraction always wrong]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Previous framing: smart extraction [below innovation threshold] vs
  stupid extraction [past threshold]. Revised: both are wrong.
  Smart extraction is slower destruction -- the threshold is still
  crossed, just later. Smart death is still death.
  The distinction that matters: predation and competition [honest
  boundary, visible conflict] vs extraction [captured measurement
  surface, hidden mechanism]. Speed of destruction is irrelevant
  to the structural judgment.
  Explanatory residue retained: some systems generated genuine surplus
  despite extraction overhead [Song Dynasty, Bell Labs] because
  extraction floor existed below which suppression became unprofitable.
  This explains the mechanism without endorsing it.
  See: EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION [updated].

EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION [revised]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Both are extraction -- both wrong. Distinction is speed and
  legibility, not moral category. Centralized is faster destruction;
  distributed with limits is slower destruction.
  Useful structural distinction retained: centralized extraction
  fails faster [condition 2 absent, no cross-correction]. Distributed
  with limits sustains longer but endpoint is the same.
  Confirmation: mit'a → encomienda = slow destruction to fast
  destruction. Not slow destruction as acceptable alternative.
  Framework position: predation and competition are the honest
  alternatives. Extraction in any form is captured measurement.

TOPOLOGY FINDINGS + DISTRIBUTED CAPTURE NETWORK [merged]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every sustained evil: consumption-dominant terminal node fed by
  capture chains through intermediate nodes that believe they operate
  normally. Highest surplus under external pressure; lowest during
  dominance. Transition = highest-value measurement point.
  Distributed Capture Network type: nodes believe independent,
  terminal extraction point invisible from inside, correction and
  extraction share identical infrastructure. Mont Pelerin [complexity
  as illegibility], WEF [complexity as legitimacy]. Same Fertile Lock
  trajectory, different optics.

ANTI-TOWER [confirmed structural inverse]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has predictable structure operating as tower inverse.
  Noise → Corruption → Destruction sequence; weapon stack [MSC + ECD
  + Calibration Strip + Formation Capture]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
  suppression as primary mechanism. Not chaos -- inverted tower with
  consistent function.
  Unfalsifiability as confirmation: if anti-tower were absent, counter-
  attempts would succeed. The fact that every counter-attempt routes
  through the same mechanisms -- noise suppresses signal, corruption
  captures the counter-movement, destruction targets what survives --
  is the signature of a functioning structure. The inability to
  disprove the anti-tower is proof of the anti-tower.
  T8 partial: recursive closure is the anti-tower working as designed,
  not a measurement failure. Does not void the finding.

CAPTURE MECHANISMS [cross-ref to political -- confirmed at multiple scales]:
  Research MI capture, suppressed variable, sub-national RI capture.
  See: CAPTURE MECHANISMS [Section Five -- Political].

POWER STABILITY [cross-ref to political -- confirmed structural]:
  Extraction architecture stable across ideological transitions.
  Branding changes; mechanism stable. 10,000 years consistent output.
  See: POWER ARCHITECTURE [Section Five -- Political].


PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION [fundamental distinction]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Predation: honest violence. Boundary visible. The prey knows the
  relationship. A lion kills. Does not build a farm, keep prey
  mostly alive, and call it efficient resource management.
  Competition: honest conflict. Boundary visible. Both parties know
  the terms. Zero-sum possible but declared.
  Extraction: trickery as foundation. Requires prey to misunderstand
  the relationship, cooperate with its own consumption, and call it
  beneficial. Measurement surface capture is the mechanism.
  Slightly wrong is still wrong -- smart extraction is slower
  destruction. The innovation threshold is still crossed, just later.
  Smart death is still death. The crime is not the degree; it is the
  lie that makes cooperation possible.
  Framework: predation and competition are structurally honest.
  Extraction is not. Politics at scale = extraction [stealing] not
  predation -- requires the legitimacy surface that predation doesn't
  need.

MEASUREMENT-ACTION UNITY [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Measurement that produces no action = observation captured by the
  observer. Action without measurement = force without calibration --
  extraction's operating mode.
  Legitimate conflict [predation, competition]: measurement drives
  action, both visible to parties bearing the cost.
  Extraction: action separated from honest measurement. Requires the
  gap to function -- prey must not see the measurement surface.
  Test: is the measurement driving the action visible to the party
  bearing the cost? If no, extraction.

IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT [promoted from science appendix]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode network
  [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making] fires only in
  attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the recovery window before
  it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit not overwork.
  Applies at all scales: individual [attention gap elimination],
  institutional [no recovery time between correction cycles],
  civilizational [extraction past the regeneration rate].
  Test: does the system have protected idle cycles? If no, SR is
  being extracted.


DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [confirmed case type -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cross-ref: RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [cycle model -- predictive detail].
  C2 restoration in a system where C1 is already terminal produces
  collapse rather than recovery. The collapse is the correct outcome
  -- accurate diagnosis of a terminal state the False Coherence
  surface had concealed.
  Mechanism: False Coherence surface masks terminal C1 trajectory.
  C2 partial restoration makes accumulated deviations simultaneously
  legible. Correction burst fires. C1 cannot absorb correction
  load -- already terminal. Collapse follows.
  Distinct from:
    C1 blind recovery failure [C1 was recoverable -- correction
      burst should produce stabilization; Diagnostic Collapse means
      C1 was never recoverable before C2 opened].
    Simple collapse [no C2 restoration -- C1 just fails].
    Coherence Mimicry [false C2 restoration -- new surface capture].
  Confirmed cases:
    GLASNOST [primary]: Soviet C1 terminal 15+ years before
      Glasnost. C2 partial opening made terminal state legible.
      Collapse = correct diagnosis. Not recovery failure.
    ARAB_SPRING_DIFFERENTIAL [Libya]: 42-year deliberate C1
      destruction. C2 opening via protest + NATO. Diagnostic
      Collapse confirmed by paired comparison with Tunisia
      [C1 recoverable, same C2 opening, recovery trajectory].
  Intervention implication: C1 substrate audit required before
    any C2 restoration intervention. Opening C2 on terminal C1
    is irreversible -- correction burst fires regardless of
    whether C1 can absorb it. External actors [NATO, sanctions,
    information operations] enabling C2 opening without C1 audit
