    produce Diagnostic Collapse or False Coherence reformation.
  Status: CONFIRMED [two primary cases + controlled comparison].
C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT [provisional finding -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1 starved recovery does not occur through individual correction
  capacity alone. External relational substrate via L3.5 COMPATIBILITY
  contact is the structural precondition.
  Confirmed across five independent cases:
    Farnese [Berserk]: relational contact with Guts/Schierke.
    Zuko [Avatar]: Iroh as substrate source restored.
    Joshua Graham [Fallout NV]: New Canaan community contact.
    Wittgenstein [real]: Cambridge re-engagement 1929 preceded
      Investigations revision. Isolation [1920-1926] produced zero
      revision despite C2 intact. Near-control condition.
    Le Guin [real]: feminist community engagement preceded
      documented gender self-corrections.
  No counterexample in library.
  Mechanism: C1 substrate too depleted for internal generation to
    restart. External contact provides substrate that internal
    generation cannot supply until above threshold. L3.5 COMPATIBILITY
    is the delivery mechanism -- not willpower, insight, or accurate
    self-diagnosis. Contact is the substrate source.

L3.5 CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH INSTRUMENT FRICTION AXIS [v16 extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Contact type varies across all five recovery cases. What is
  consistent is not type but structural fit: the contact that fired
  recovery matched the target instrument's primary receivable
  friction axis. Mismatched contact provides L3.5 presence without
  substrate transfer.

  CASE MAPPING:
    Farnese: primary axis = sensory/somatic. Contact type = physical
      danger + combat survival + bodily co-presence with Guts.
      Logical argument or emotional reassurance would not have
      reached her instrument. Somatic contact did.
      Evidence: recovery fires during physical ordeal episodes,
      not during conversations.

    Zuko: primary axis = social/relational. Contact type = Iroh's
      unconditional presence, patience, warmth without correction
      pressure. Iroh never argued Zuko into correction; he waited.
      Evidence: intellectual argument from other characters
      [Katara, Aang] produced no recovery. Iroh's relational
      contact did. Same information content, different axis.

    Joshua Graham: primary axis = logical/social hybrid. Contact
      type = structured community with coherent meaning framework
      [New Canaan + scripture + Burned Man narrative]. Not pure
      emotional support; the community provided both belonging
      AND interpretive structure. Both axes required.
      Evidence: isolation post-Legion destruction did not produce
      recovery; community contact at New Canaan did.

    Wittgenstein: primary axis = logical/systemic. Contact type =
      adversarial intellectual friction [Vienna Circle, Russell,
      Sraffa's specific gesture challenge]. Not emotional support
      -- he had social stability in Austria, produced nothing.
      Cambridge intellectual challenge fired correction.
      Evidence: the isolation period had social contact [village
      community] but zero logical/systemic friction. Cambridge
      provided logical friction; revision began.
      Key case: Sraffa's reportedly single adversarial gesture
      challenge [pointing to Neapolitan chin flick, asking "what
      is the logical form of that?"] may have been the specific
      trigger. Single contact event on correct axis.

    Le Guin: primary axis = logical/social hybrid [writer +
      intellectual]. Contact type = feminist community of practice
      -- relational belonging + intellectual challenge to prior
      work simultaneously. Both axes active; both required.

  STRUCTURAL FINDING [v16]:
    L3.5 contact type must match target instrument's primary
    receivable friction axis. Three axes confirmed relevant:
      Sensory/somatic: physical co-presence, shared bodily risk,
        survival-level stakes. Not reducible to emotional warmth.
      Social/relational: unconditional presence, consistent
        availability, warmth without agenda. Not intellectual
        challenge.
      Logical/systemic: adversarial intellectual contact from
        peer who takes work seriously enough to contest it.
        Not social support.
    Mismatch: contact on wrong axis provides L3.5 presence
      [counts toward contact requirement] but does not transfer
      substrate. Explains failed recovery attempts: therapeutic
      approaches aimed at wrong axis for instrument type.

  INTERVENTION IMPLICATION [updated]:
    Correct intervention is not just relational contact -- it is
    contact on the axis the target instrument can receive.
    Prescribing social/emotional support for a logical/systemic
    instrument [Wittgenstein type] provides presence without
    substrate transfer. Prescribing intellectual challenge for
    a somatic instrument [Farnese type] same failure.
    Assessment required: identify target's primary receivable
    axis before prescribing contact type.

  FALSIFICATION CRITERIA [updated]:
    1. C1 starved recovery without L3.5 contact [original].
    2. C1 starved recovery via L3.5 contact on demonstrably
       mismatched axis -- would falsify axis-matching requirement.
    No such case in current library.

  STATUS: PROVISIONAL extended [five contact type mappings,
    axis-matching pattern consistent, no falsification].
    Promote axis-matching to confirmed when: independent case
    confirms recovery fired on matched axis AND failed on
    mismatched axis for same instrument within one case window.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode network
  [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making] fires only in
  attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the recovery window before
  it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit not overwork.
  Applies at all scales: individual [attention gap elimination],
  institutional [no recovery time between correction cycles],
  civilizational [extraction past the regeneration rate].
  Test: does the system have protected idle cycles? If no, SR is
  being extracted.

SPEED AS ERROR SIGNAL [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The faster a solution is implemented the more likely it is wrong.
  Speed compresses the correction cycle -- insufficient time for
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to verify, CAUSAL LEGIBILITY to trace, or
  CORRECTION CAPACITY to fire before the action lands.
  Best solutions are slow and careful: each step generates feedback
  that calibrates the next. Speed eliminates the feedback loop.
  Aggression and violence as reaction not initiation: if force is

INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT [provisional -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The contact-type-must-match-friction-axis finding [individual scale,
  C1 starved recovery] extends to institutional scale.
  Institutional C1 starved recovery: correction operating on a
  different axis than the dysfunction does not transfer substrate.
  Confirmed across four institutional recovery cases:

  Cistercian [organizational axis]:
    Cluny dysfunction = structural centralization producing wealth.
    Cistercian correction = organizational redesign [general chapter
    + local autonomy]. Same structural/organizational axis.
    Recovery: confirmed [L4, 700-year record].

  Jesuit [contemplative/individual axis]:
    Catholic spiritual dysfunction = external rule-following without
    internal examination. Jesuit correction = Examen [internal
    examination procedure]. Same contemplative axis.
    Recovery: confirmed [L4, 480-year record].

  Vatican II [doctrinal/methodological axis]:
    Counter-Reformation dysfunction = methodological lock
    [Neo-scholasticism]. Vatican II correction = historical method
    [nouvelle théologie on same methodological axis].
    Recovery: confirmed [C1 locked recovery primary case].

  China 1978 [split-axis case -- confirms by differential]:
    Maoist dysfunction = ideological axis [class struggle doctrine].
    Economic correction = productive axis [matching C1 substrate].
    Recovery: CONFIRMED for economic C1.
    Political correction = different axis [C2 surface, not matching
    Maoist ideological axis]. Recovery: FAILED [Tiananmen].
    Same system, same three conditions, axis-match determines
    which component recovered. Split case is the strongest
    institutional confirmation.

  FINDING [provisional]:
    Institutional correction must operate on the same functional
    axis as the dysfunction to transfer substrate. Cross-axis
    correction produces activity without substrate transfer --
    institutionally equivalent to wrong-axis L3.5 contact.
    China 1978 provides controlled comparison: same system,
    same enabling moment, matched-axis correction succeeded,
    mismatched-axis correction failed.

  Falsification: documented institutional recovery via correction
    operating on demonstrably different axis than dysfunction.
    No such case in current library.

    2. Evidence-based medicine: systematic review methodology

    3. Agile software development: methodology transmitted as

       embedded procedure outperform those treating Agile as
       set of conclusions [user stories, velocity metrics].
       Observable in project outcome data.
    Falsification: any of the three cases showing Generational
    Reset despite embedded methodology transmission, or
    sustained non-Reset despite conclusion-only transmission.
  required it should fire as T1.3 [proportional response to detected

COLLAPSED / STUB ENTRIES

  CORRECTION LANDING / INTEGRATION [collapsed into CORRECTION CAPACITY]

     properties of CORRECTION CAPACITY, not standalone qualities.

TOWER ORDER [primary organizing structure]

  FOUNDATION LAYER [enable all levels]:
  GENUINE AXIS             axis must exist before transmitting
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       defines who measurement applies to
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION   substrate must exist for cycle
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      meta -- holds all levels in shape
  TENSION DISSOLUTION      meta -- TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite

  CONSTANTS [required from L1 upward]:
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      CONSTANT + L1 tension unit
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       CONSTANT
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      CONSTANT [meta]

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
  GENERATIVE CYCLE         TENSION UNIT -- core
  SURPLUS PRODUCTION       TENSION UNIT
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      TENSION UNIT [also constant]
  Tests: T1.1-T1.5 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY    TENSION UNIT -- core
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY        TENSION UNIT
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY      TENSION UNIT
  Tests: T2.1-T2.3 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY TENSION UNIT -- core
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY       TENSION UNIT
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING    TENSION UNIT [also foundation]
  Tests: T3.1 [see tower block above]

ANTI-COHERENCE TOWER ATTACK [probability gradient -- not necessary sequence]
  L3 attack: Fertile Lock -> corrupt transmission
  L2 attack: Measurement destruction -> surface captured
  L2 attack: Boundary manipulation -> measurement population shifted
  L2 attack: Causal orphaning -> chain severed
  L1 attack: Axis imposition -> direction captured
  L1 attack: Correction capture -> fires toward monopolist
  L1 attack: Consumption dominance -> cycle costs exceed output
  L1 attack: Cycle collapse -> generative loop fails
  Tower reading: attacks typically start at L3 [cheaper, less visible]
  and cascade downward. L1 attack is most catastrophic but most costly
  to execute. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure runs parallel not sequential.
  Each earlier step reduces resistance cost of later steps -- not
  a required sequence. Any step can fire first [Enron: L2 without
  prior L3 failure; Room 101: L1 attacked directly].
  Read as: typical deployment order under resource constraints.

Run this checklist before finalizing any new version file.

REMOVE:
  - WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED -- delete entirely
  - "deferred to vN" references where N <= current version
  - "Move to Pending" markers -- item should already be there
  - Duplicate section content [same definition in two sections]
  - Stale transcript-pending notes where content now confirmed
  - Orphan separator lines [=== or --- with no adjacent content]

MERGE:
  - Standalone findings that duplicate Theory section content
  - Psychology parallel + Fiction/Real table [same purpose]
  - Pending items that are fully complete -- remove from Pending
  - WIP sub-items that belong under a parent item

COMPRESS:
  - Version convention lists > 10 items -- summarize by category
  - Remove RESOLVED items from active list when next version is cut
  - Pending list items marked complete in all sub-points -- remove

FORMAT:
  - Max 2 consecutive blank lines
  - No blank line immediately after === separator
  - No double === separators [run dedup pass]
  - No duplicate subsection headers within 10 lines of each other

AUDIT COMMANDS [run before saving]:
  Double separators:  count(===\n===) == 0
  Triple blanks:      count(\n\n\n) == 0
  Stale deferred:     count('deferred to v[N-1 or earlier]') == 0
  Orphan RESOLVED:    count('RESOLVED [v') in Active List == 0

QUALITY USAGE AUDIT [mandatory on version increment]:
  Run when: any quality added, removed, renumbered, or renamed.
  1. MAPPING TABLE: produce old->new quality name table before any
     changes. Archive in WIP Active List under version item.
  2. PROOF LABEL SCAN: grep all 'Proof:' and 'Proof for:' lines.
     Verify each quality name matches current schema. Dirty range =
     any Q that changed number or name.
  3. DEFINITION BLOCK SCAN: check internal cross-references
     within quality definitions [e.g. 'Distinct from MEASUREMENT
       INTEGRITY'].
     Update any reference into dirty range.
  4. WEAPON INDEX SCAN: verify each [HDR:QN_WEAPONS] tag matches
     the 'QN WEAPONS' content label directly below it.
  5. STRUCTURAL ORDER TABLE: update to match new assignments.
     This table is authoritative -- resolve conflicts against it.
  6. BODY TEXT: do NOT bulk-substitute in body text. Update only
     where meaning breaks. Quality names in context are robust;
     quality names in proof labels are not.
  7. CONFIRM: grep dirty-range quality names in proof labels after
     pass. Count must be zero before version is saved.
  If audit deferred: log as WIP item with mapping table inline.
  Undocumented renumber = document internally inconsistent.

FORMAT RULE: all lines <= 78 chars. Separators exempt.

SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [applies everywhere]:
  AUTO-MERGE: two entries in same major section addressing same
    structural phenomenon from different angles -> merge on next
    version increment. Flag [MERGE CANDIDATE] if identified mid-session.
    Trigger: same quality target + same mechanism = merge.
    Trigger: sequential argument split across adjacent sections = merge.
    Trigger: one section is restatement of adjacent section = compress
    into cross-reference or subsection.

  ADD NEW SECTION: entry has distinct mechanism, distinct quality
    target, AND no existing section covers it within 200 lines.
    Otherwise: add as subsection or extend nearest related entry.

  CROSS-REFERENCE instead of merge when: entries are in different
    section types [empirical vs speculative; method vs case library].
    Boundary between sections is load-bearing -- do not collapse.

  COMPRESS: entry where full detail lives elsewhere -> reduce to
    core claim + pointer. Never duplicate substantial content.

  GIFT INDEX specific: Primary / Secondary / Closed / Sacrificial
    subcategories separated by ---------- for reading clarity.
    New entries slot into nearest existing subcategory.

  WEAPON INDEX specific: entries with identical quality target + mechanism
    -> merge with note. Sole proof of a type -> keep regardless of
    length. Remove only if no primary proof case.

NAME COMPRESSION POLICY:
  Long technical names compressed on second use and in index entries.
  Full name used once [first appearance or definition entry].
  Compressed form used thereafter. Standard compressions:
    Rational Quantum Mechanics -> RaQM
    Engineered Causal Diffusion -> ECD
    Measurement Surface Control -> MSC
    Calibration Strip -> CS
    Axis Gestation Capture -> AGC
    Correction Theatre -> CT
    Fertile Lock -> FL
    Exit Cost Construction -> ECC
    Dependency Construction -> DC
    Boundary Manipulation -> BM
    Coherence Mimicry -> CM
    Formation Capture -> FC
    Proxy Capture -> PC
  New compressions added here when introduced in framework.
SECTION AND CONTENT MAINTENANCE LOGIC [v13]:
  MERGE: two entries describing same phenomenon from different angles.
  REMOVE: entry contradicted by empirical finding -> move to DENY.
  PROMOTE: entry gains empirical confirmation -> move to Theory.
  ADD: new finding -> slot into nearest existing heading.
  COMPRESS: redundant detail -> reduce to core claim + status tag.
  STATUS TAGS: STRONG / MODERATE / PROVISIONAL / CONDITIONAL /
    UNCERTAIN / T3 / STRUCTURAL LIMIT.

CASE LIBRARY ADDITION LOGIC [v13]:
  ENTRY FILTER [apply first]:
    Case must be strange -- if it confirms what the framework already
    knows without adding texture, don't add. Familiar mechanism,
    familiar scale, no surprises = no entry.

  STANDARD ENTRY [pass any one]:
    Two or more qualities active [no strict quality requirement --
    any confirmed qualities count].
    Multiple weapons active simultaneously [weapon stack cases].

  PRIMARY CASE [stricter threshold -- pass one]:
    Highly specific form [sole proof of type, unique mechanism,
    sole proof of failure state] -- always keep.
    Single quality but very high clarity or unusual scale -- keep
    only if it is the cleanest proof of that quality in the library.
    Replace existing if new case is cleaner. Do not add if existing
    is stronger or equivalent.

  SIMILAR MECHANISM to existing entry: merge or cross-reference.
    Do not add as standalone.
  THRESHOLD: if entry would not survive any of the above tests,
    do not add. Quantity is not quality. Library maintenance cost
    scales with size -- every weak entry raises instrument MEASUREMENT
      INTEGRITY burden
    without increasing proof coverage.

QUALITY ADDITION / MERGE LOGIC [v15]:

  TOWER CODEPENDENCE TEST [apply first]:
  Before adding, check where the candidate sits in the tower:
    Foundation layer [GENUINE AXIS / BOUNDARY INTEGRITY / SUBSTRATE
      REGENERATION / TENSION MAINTENANCE]: preconditions enabling all
      levels.
      Add here only if quality enables tower formation itself.
    Level 1 [generative]: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION / CORRECTION
      CAPACITY and extensions.
    Level 2 [epistemic]: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
      REFERENCE INTEGRITY and foundations.
    Level 3 [temporal]: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
      CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING and foundations.
    Cross-level: quality active at multiple levels simultaneously.
  If candidate cannot be placed in the tower, it is either:
    a) A named property of an existing quality's middle zone
    b) A weapon variant [failure mode with deployment mechanism]
    c) A structural limit [T3 -- unmeasurable by design]

  ADDITION TESTS [pass all four or route to alternatives]:
  1. TOWER PLACEMENT: identify which level and role [pair / third
     point / foundation / support]. If no clear placement, route
     to named failure mode.
  2. TENSION UNIT: can this quality stand alone and produce
     meaningful reading? If yes, add solo. If only meaningful in
     tension with others, identify the tension unit [3 qualities]
     first. Incomplete tension units held until all three identified.
  3. GAP CHECK: does an existing quality + modifier already cover
     this mechanism? If >50% covered, collapse into that quality
     as named failure mode or weapon. If genuinely new, add.
  4. PRACTICAL TEST: measurable from Track C without Track B?
     If not, flag T3 candidate only.

  MERGE LOGIC [when to collapse]:
  Collapse into existing quality when:
    - Mechanism is a named sub-property of the existing quality
    - Middle zone property of a tension unit level
    - Weapon variant with no generative counterpart
    - Downstream of existing quality [fires after, not alongside]
  Keep as standalone when:
    - Distinct tower placement from nearest quality
    - Weapon exists specifically targeting this quality and not the
      nearest existing one
    - Biological or case confirmation exists independently

  ROUTE MAP [candidate fails tests]:
    -> Named failure mode of nearest quality
    -> Weapon variant in weapon index
    -> Middle zone property of existing level pair
    -> T3 structural limit [unmeasurable]
    -> Candidate held for tower placement identification

WITHIN-VERSION UPDATES [no increment] when:
  - Case entries added or modified
  - WIP items added, updated, resolved, or merged
  - Calibration ranges assigned or revised
  - Political case readings updated
  - Psychology parallels added
  - Provisional readings refined
  - Cross-references added

PENDING LIST [version increment triggers]:
  Version increment warranted when:
    1. New structural category added [failure state, honest limit,
       coherence type, track].
    2. Existing category fundamentally revised [not just extended].
    3. Political section reworked or bias position changed.
    4. Case library exceeds maintenance thresholds [>300 cases,
       >500KB].
    5. Multi-instrument replication data produces discordance
       requiring framework revision.
  Do not increment for: case additions, weapon additions, policy
  updates, format fixes, or clarifications within existing
  categories.

[HDR:GOALS]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:GOALS]
GOALS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1. Accurate measurement of generating versus consuming across defined
     population and time window. Named parameters before measurement:
     boundary, population, window, substrate.
  2. Identification of failure mechanism, not just failure presence.
     Each failure state has a different correction pathway.
  3. Practical utility. If a component does not produce a reading more
     useful than intuition alone, it does not belong.

FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS TOOL [v15]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
The framework is not a descriptive theory -- it is a praxis instrument.
Its value is not that it correctly maps coherence; it is that it
identifies where correction is possible and where it is not, orienting
action toward leverage points rather than dead ends.

Marx [11th Thesis on Feuerbach, 1845]: "Philosophers have hitherto
only interpreted the world in various ways; the point is to change it."
The framework collapses the distinction -- getting the ontology right
IS the precondition for effective action. Cannot change what cannot
be correctly described structurally.

Framework qualities are instruments not criteria. Once a quality
becomes a criterion of reality rather than a revisable tool, the
system stops asking "under what conditions does this pattern hold?"
and starts asking "why does this case deviate?" -- exchanging
correction for policing. This is CAUSAL LEGIBILITY capture applied to the
  framework
itself. Guard against by treating all qualities as falsifiable tools.

Practical implication: use the framework to find leverage, not to
classify. Classification without correction pathway is description.
The framework produces value only when it identifies what can be
moved and in which direction.

==========================================================================

SECTION TWO -- THEORY
[SEC:PRE_AXIAL_CATEGORIES]
==========================================================================
MINIMAL FRAMEWORK DESCRIPTION [one-paragraph]:
  Accurate measurement + boundary selection + personal values.
  Across any boundary: what is generated, what is extracted?
  That question applied recursively at every scale -- individual,
  family, institution, nation, civilization -- is the complete
  framework. Everything else is elaboration of what "accurate
  measurement," "boundary," and "generation vs extraction" mean
  in specific contexts.

[CASE:COHERENCE_TYPES]
COHERENCE TYPES + PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

PRE-AXIAL [below axis threshold -- L2/L3 inapplicable]:

  STRUCTURAL COHERENCE: generates surplus and self-corrects without
    orientation, measurement surface, or transmission axis.
    L1 core present. L2/L3 inapplicable by category.
    Geometry: sphere [no hole = no axis]. Cannot achieve False
    Coherence. Cannot deploy weapons against itself. Destruction-only
    control. Cases: ant colonies, mycorrhizal networks, Terminids.

  NASCENT COHERENCE: axis forming via relational/environmental contact.
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY developing, not present. Primary vulnerability:
    axis formation inside controlled environment produces apparent
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY indistinguishable from genuine internally.
    Pre-Fertile Lock. Geometry: polarized sphere.
    Only documented case: Meruem [HxH -- dies before Axial completion].

  SEEDED NASCENT [v9]: pre-axial with directional goals embedded at
    creation. Built-in goals function as constitutive INPUT FILTERING
    -- corrupt substrate enters GENERATIVE CYCLE before SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION can engage. SURPLUS PRODUCTION survives because
    filtering happens upstream. Self-suppression without enforcement.
    PRIMARY proof: Dream Seeds [WATF].

  AXIAL COHERENCE: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY fully established. Full quality
    stack applies. Torus geometry. Standard framework measurement.

PRE-AXIAL INPUT / OUTPUT / CONNECTION MODEL [v12]:
  Input: environmental signal [resource, threat, contact]. No axis
    to filter -- raw reception. SURPLUS PRODUCTION fires against baseline
      deviation
    not toward goal.
  Output: SURPLUS PRODUCTION [if cycle runs] + SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
    [maintained].
    No SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY to direct output. Pure functional generation.
  Connections: CORRECTION CAPACITY [boundary determines what counts as
    inside].
    GENERATIVE CYCLE [cycle determines what gets processed]. No TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY -- no axis to transmit. Generation and correction without
      orientation.
  Present: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION / CORRECTION CAPACITY /
    GENUINE AXIS / BOUNDARY INTEGRITY -- fully operable.
  Absent: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY / TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
    /
    MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING -- inapplicable by
      category.

MINIMUM VIABLE TOWER LEVEL BY DOMAIN [v15]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Tower reading: each domain requires a minimum level to be stable.
Lower level must hold before upper level is meaningful.

  BIOLOGICAL [cell]: L1 core only [GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS / CORRECTION].
    L2/L3 inapplicable. Confirmed at cellular scale.

  BIOLOGICAL [organism]: L1 full + foundation [GENUINE AXIS / SUBSTRATE
    REGENERATION].
    GENUINE AXIS gives correction direction. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
      sustains.

  SOCIAL [dyad/small group]: L1 full + L3 core [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY -- nothing persists across interactions.
    L2 partial [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY] -- accountability requires legible
      surface.

  INSTITUTIONAL: L1 full + L2 full + L3 core [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].
    L2 required -- institution without honest surface captures itself.

  POLITICAL SYSTEM: L1+L2+L3 all cores + SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY.
    CORRECTION CAPACITY without PROPORTIONAL RESPONSE becomes suppression.
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY -- locks into first generation's axis without it.

  ASCENDING PATH [individual]: constants + GENUINE AXIS baseline
    + L1 core + L2 core + SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY + CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING.
    GENUINE AXIS -- axis must be genuine. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
    -- axis must be grounded. Plateau without either.

  ASCENDING PATH [civilizational]: constants + full tower all levels.
    No confirmed case. Theoretical only.

Pattern: minimum = L1 core + domain-specific level additions.
Weapon deployment targets the highest required level first.

DANGEROUS QUALITY INTERACTIONS [v13]:
  High GENUINE AXIS + low CORRECTION CAPACITY in hostile environment =
    Socrates problem.
    Examined axis visible without correction protection = target.
  High CORRECTION CAPACITY + low CAUSAL LEGIBILITY = correction fires but
    cannot anchor.
    Produces visible activity without resolving deviation.
  High TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY + low SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY = transmission
    without correctable method.
    Fertile Lock trajectory -- successors receive destination only.
  High SURPLUS PRODUCTION + low GENUINE AXIS = surplus without direction.
    Distribution to wrong population; extraction looks like gift.
  High signal detection + low CORRECTION CAPACITY distribution
    = Frustrated Detection Configuration. Population detects extraction
    accurately; correction capacity monopolized, cannot fire.

Most humans may be functionally Structural inside artificially maintained
environments [see Population Coherence Ceiling]. Hard implication:
genuine SURPLUS PRODUCTION at population scale structurally absent. T3 risk
  high.
[Biological confirmation: see COHERENCE AS BIOLOGICAL BASELINE --
L1 core confirmed at every scale from cell to person independently.]

  RELATIONAL        Syndicalist federation. Output: transmission

                    dependence without institutional architecture.

                    to reproduce centralized axis.

  INTRINSIC         personal extraction. No coordination required.

  FAMILY            Smallest boundary; highest weapon intensity per
                    unit size. Identical delivery mechanism for genuine
                    goods and weapons. Transmission dependency dominant.

  SCIENCE           Correction capacity is primary axis and output.
                    Unique vulnerability: capturing correction mechanism
                    captures science completely.
                    PRIMARY: Bell Labs 1925-1984 [peak window].
                    Real-world non-institutional: Death Grips.

  MATH [subtype]    Highest-density gift-producing field. Proof is
                    transmission mechanism -- cleaner CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
                      than any other
                    discipline. Results hold or don't; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
                      most honest
                    measurement surface in library.
                    GENERATIVE CYCLE: proof generates new solvable territory
                      [surplus
                    = expanded problem space]. SURPLUS PRODUCTION
                      structurally built in.
                    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: falsification non-negotiable at
                      foundational level.
                    Distinct failure modes:
                      Prestige capture [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY -- Fields Medal
                        sociology].
                      Formalism over insight [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY -- proof
                        without
                      understanding].
                      Funding pressure narrowing problems [SURPLUS

                    Gift signature: foundational redefinitions transfer

                    Clausen]. Source not depleted; field GENERATIVE CYCLE

                    foundational scale]. Grothendieck [same pattern,

  ANTI-CAPTURE /    Resists capture structurally. Grows by output
  SILENT            differential not suppression. T2 applies until
  ALTERNATIVE       transition stress event. Silent without anti-capture
                    = concealment, not configuration.
                    SUBSTRATE REQUIREMENT [v15]: Silent Alternative
                    must actively improve substrate [GENUINE AXIS] during
                      silent
                    window -- not just preserve it. Managed coherence
                    collapse means substrate is already degrading.
                    Silent Alternative that merely avoids extraction
                    while substrate collapses around it is plateau not

                    depleted or the transition stress event arrives

[HDR:DIRECTIONALITY_COMPOSITE]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:DIRECTIONALITY_COMPOSITE]
DIRECTIONALITY COMPOSITE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Six signatures, equal weight, independent.

  S1  Novel input response
      1 = integrates genuinely novel inputs requiring axis adjustment.
      0.5 = evidence present, condition compromised. 0 = inaccessible.
      Operational procedure: present system with input that contradicts
      current axis. Genuine integration = axis revision visible in
      subsequent behavior. Recombination = input explained using existing
      axis without axis movement. Score 0 if axis does not move despite
      genuine contradiction. Historical reconstruction preferred.
      Ambiguous [input absorbed, axis movement unclear] = 0.5.

  S2  Scarcity behavior
      1 = axis holds under resource drop without external supply.
      0.5 = partial evidence. 0 = inaccessible.

  S3  Correction initiation
      1 = corrections happen before external pressure arrives.
      0.5 = timing ambiguous or observer effect. 0 = inaccessible.
      Operational procedure: identify earliest documented correction
      signal. Reconstruct timeline of external pressure relative to it.
      Correction precedes pressure = score 1. Pressure predates
      correction = score 0. Ambiguous or undocumented = 0.5. Bias
      toward 0.5 when uncertain.

  S4  Contestation asymmetry
      1 = cost consistent across group positions inside boundary.
      0.5 = partial data. 0 = inaccessible.

  S5  Self-adversarial correction capacity [v15]
      1 = generates and integrates genuine challenges to own axis
      without external prompt, at reputational or personal cost.
      0 = correction only against external deviation, never against
      own axis outputs.
      0.5 = partial evidence. Inaccessible = 0.
      Strongest single indicator of genuine SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY.
      Prospective proxy: unprompted self-correction in stable
      conditions [Track A, low-stakes] predicts S5 under stress.
      Wittgenstein's notebook self-challenges preceded public
      Investigations correction -- Track A in stable conditions
      is the only forward-looking S5 signal available.
      PRIMARY evidence: Emet-Selch [FF14], Wittgenstein, Le Guin.

  S6  External correction reception [v15]
      1 = integrates genuine correction from outside boundary at
      reputational or operational cost. Behavior changes in direction
      of correction within measurement window.
      0.5 = engagement without behavioral change.
      0 = structurally deaf to external input [not hostile -- incapable].
      Dismissal without engagement = 0.
      Operational procedure: identify documented external correction
      from outside Boundary A. Does system behavior change in direction
      of correction within window? Yes = 1. No change = 0.
      PRIMARY proof: South Africa TRC [external pressure -> internal
      legal change]. Counterproof: contemporary US police reform
      [external correction documented, no behavioral change].

Output format: score / signatures accessed / ceiling flag.
  0.75 from 2 of 6 [ceiling limited] = provisional, flagged.
  0.75 from 6 of 6 = strong claim at same score.

MOMENTUM SCORE [v16 -- trajectory model extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
S1-S6 measure static directionality signatures. Momentum measures
rate and direction of change in those signatures over time.
A system at 0.6 accelerating toward coherence is structurally
different from a system at 0.6 decelerating. Same score, opposite
intervention implication.

  M1  Trajectory direction [required]
      +1 = S-score composite increasing across two or more
           measurement windows.
       0 = holding [change within noise threshold].
      -1 = S-score composite decreasing across windows.
      Operational procedure: require minimum two readings separated
      by meaningful interval [not same session]. Direction =
      sign of delta. Single reading = M1 inaccessible.

  M2  Rate [required when M1 accessible]
      FAST = meaningful S-score change within short window
        [individual: weeks-months; institutional: months-years;
        civilizational: years-decades].
      SLOW = change detectable only across long window.
      HOLDING = no detectable change.
      Rate calibrates intervention urgency. Fast-degrading C2
      with intact C1 = intervention window closing. Slow recovery
      with intact C2 = patience, not pressure.

  M3  Momentum type [required when M1 accessible]
      SELF-REINFORCING = trajectory accelerating in its own
        direction. Degradation producing conditions for more
        degradation [ICD loop]. Recovery producing conditions
        for more recovery [L3.5 contact restoring C1, enabling
        more L3.5 contact].
      SELF-LIMITING = trajectory decelerating. Degradation
        hitting structural floor [C1 starved but not terminal].
        Recovery hitting structural ceiling [Mixed Coherence
        ceiling under extraction conditions].
      EXTERNALLY DRIVEN = trajectory dependent on external
        input maintaining it. Remove input, trajectory reverses.
        Distinguish from self-reinforcing: externally driven
        looks like self-reinforcing until the external input
        is removed or interrupted.
      Operational procedure: identify what is causing the
      trajectory. Is the cause itself produced by the trajectory
      [self-reinforcing] or independent of it [externally driven]?

  EXTERNAL DEPENDENCY PROXY SIGNALS [v16 -- M3 operationalization]
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: EXTERNALLY DRIVEN and SELF-REINFORCING are
  indistinguishable from output alone in a live system. The
  removal test [remove external input, observe trajectory
  change] cannot be run on live systems without destroying them.
  Five Track C proxy signals distinguishable before removal:

    PROXY ED1 -- OUTPUT ROUTING CONCENTRATION
      What fraction of outputs route to a single external
      recipient? Bell Labs: 100% of output routed to AT&T
      monopoly business. Self-reinforcing systems route output
      to multiple recipients [each output generates new input
      from diverse sources].
      High external routing concentration + single recipient =
      EXTERNALLY DRIVEN candidate.
      Test: if primary external input disappeared tomorrow,
      would output find alternative recipients? If no existing
      routing infrastructure exists: EXTERNALLY DRIVEN.

    PROXY ED2 -- INPUT SOURCE CONCENTRATION [T1.4 analog]
      What fraction of sustaining input [funding, talent,
      legitimacy] comes from a single external source?
      Bell Labs: AT&T budget allocation = primary talent
      pipeline and funding source. Hiring and research
      direction both flowed from single external decision.
      >50% from single source = EXTERNALLY DRIVEN candidate.
      Same as pre-restoration C1 audit T1.4 proxy but
      applied to momentum type rather than substrate state.

    PROXY ED3 -- SELF-SUSTAINING REVENUE TEST
      Does the system generate its own sustaining revenue
      independent of the primary external input?
      Bell Labs: no independent revenue. Research outputs
      were treated as public goods [AT&T promoted transistor
      technology broadly rather than monopolizing it].
      Self-reinforcing systems generate surplus that funds
      the next cycle internally. Externally driven systems
      require the external input to initiate each cycle.
      Test: if external input paused for one cycle, would
      the system complete the next cycle from internal
      surplus? If no: EXTERNALLY DRIVEN.

    PROXY ED4 -- TRAJECTORY HISTORY UNDER INPUT VARIATION
      Has external input varied historically? If so, did
      trajectory correlate with input variation?
      Bell Labs: AT&T funding decisions tracked research
      output and monopoly revenue. When AT&T thrived,
      Bell Labs thrived. Correlation visible in Track C
      before 1984.
      Self-reinforcing trajectory: stable or improving
      across input variation [internal momentum carries
      through gaps]. Externally driven: degrades during
      input gaps, recovers when input restored.
      Requires historical window. If no variation history
      available: flag M3 confidence PARTIAL.

    PROXY ED5 -- SUCCESSOR GENERATION FUNDING SOURCE
      Did the trajectory persist into successor generations
      without the original external input?
      Bell Labs successors [Bellcore, AT&T Labs, Lucent]:
      all lost the original research output trajectory within
      one generation of the breakup. Each inherited structure
      without sustaining input.
      Self-reinforcing: successors sustain trajectory from
      inherited momentum [Cistercian houses maintained
      reform methodology without Cistercian founders].
      Externally driven: successors inherit structure but
      not trajectory. Trajectory collapses in next generation.
      Retroactive signal -- requires successor evidence.
      Most powerful confirmation but only available ex post.

  COMPOSITE EXTERNAL DEPENDENCY READING:
    ED1-ED3 assessable in live systems [Track C, current window].
    ED4 requires historical window.
    ED5 requires successor evidence [retroactive only].
    If ED1 + ED2 + ED3 all high: EXTERNALLY DRIVEN likely.
    If ED1-ED3 mixed: PARTIAL confidence, flag M3.
    If ED1-ED3 low: SELF-REINFORCING candidate [confirm
    with ED4 if historical window available].
    Bell Labs pre-1984: ED1 HIGH [100% AT&T routing],
    ED2 HIGH [AT&T budget = primary input], ED3 HIGH
    [no independent revenue]. EXTERNALLY DRIVEN reading
    available before 1984 removal event. Reading confirmed
    by ED4 [trajectory correlated with AT&T monopoly health]
    and ED5 [all successor organizations lost trajectory].

  M3 CONFIDENCE LEVELS [updated]:
    HIGH: all three live-system proxies [ED1-ED3] assessable
      + trajectory history available [ED4].
    PARTIAL: ED1-ED3 only, no historical variation data.
    LOW: single proxy only or all proxies inaccessible.
    Flag M3 PARTIAL when only ED5 available [retroactive].

  INTERACTION WITH M4 [momentum source]:
    EXTERNALLY DRIVEN confirmed -> M4 = name the external
      input. Intervention: what happens if external input
      is interrupted? Pre-restoration C1 audit applies if
      C1 substrate is also externally dependent [T1.4].
    SELF-REINFORCING confirmed -> M4 = name the feedback
      loop. Intervention: identify the loop's self-sustaining
      mechanism and whether it can be interrupted.
    Mixed [ED proxies disagree] -> M4 = name both
      components. Intervention: distinguish which component
      is load-bearing for the trajectory.

  M4  Momentum source [optional -- raises confidence]
      Name the mechanism producing the trajectory. Matches to
      interference type:
        C1 blind degrading: C2 capture accelerating [MSC weapon
          active]; C1 generating on captured surface, output
          obscuring degradation.
        C1 starved recovering: L3.5 contact present [confirms
          provisional finding]; substrate restoring.
        C1 locked: transmission failure; no momentum on C1/C2
          [they're functional]; C3 producing Generational Reset.
        Full interference: self-reinforcing degradation toward
          terminal state; momentum source = extraction architecture
          consuming faster than any remaining generation.

MOMENTUM OUTPUT FORMAT [appends to S-score output]:
  M1 [direction]: [+1 / 0 / -1]
  M2 [rate]: [FAST / SLOW / HOLDING]
  M3 [type]: [SELF-REINFORCING / SELF-LIMITING / EXTERNALLY DRIVEN]
  M4 [source]: [named mechanism]
  Windows used: [interval between readings]
  Momentum confidence: [HIGH / PARTIAL / INACCESSIBLE]
    HIGH = M1-M4 all assessable.
    PARTIAL = M1-M2 only [direction and rate without mechanism].
    INACCESSIBLE = single reading only; no trajectory available.

COMBINED OUTPUT FORMAT [full reading]:
  S-score: [composite] from [n] of 6 signatures [ceiling flag]
  M-score: [direction] at [rate], [type]
  Intervention implication: [from trajectory + interference type]
  Example: "0.6 from 5 of 6 [-1, FAST, SELF-REINFORCING] --
    C2 degrading accelerating; intervention window closing;
    external C2 instrument required before capture completes."

MOMENTUM CALIBRATION CASES:
  Wittgenstein [isolation period]: M1=-1 [no revision], rate
    HOLDING [stable non-production], type SELF-LIMITING [hit
    substrate floor, not terminal]. M1 reversed on Cambridge
    re-engagement [L3.5 contact = external momentum source].
    Demonstrates EXTERNALLY DRIVEN recovery momentum correctly

  Enron [pre-collapse]: M1=-1, rate FAST, type SELF-REINFORCING
    [SPE structures divert more circulation, enabling more SPE
    structures]. External C2 restoration [Skilling conviction]
    interrupted but did not reverse momentum -- C1 was already
    terminal by collapse.
  Bell Labs [peak window]: M1=+1, rate SLOW, type MIXED
    [SELF-REINFORCING component: outputs attract talent, talent
    produces outputs; EXTERNALLY DRIVEN component: AT&T funding
    = primary input source].
    ED proxy readings pre-1984: ED1 HIGH [100% AT&T routing],
    ED2 HIGH [AT&T budget = primary talent pipeline],
    ED3 HIGH [no independent revenue].
    EXTERNALLY DRIVEN reading available from Track C before
    breakup. Confirmed by ED5 [all successors lost trajectory
    within one generation].
    Intervention implication: SELF-REINFORCING component
    alone was insufficient to sustain trajectory. External
    input was load-bearing. Breakup = removal test confirmed.

CHANGE DIRECTIONALITY TEST [updated with momentum]:
  Internal change before external pressure + self-reinforcing
    momentum = strongest positive signal [S3=1 + M3=SR].
  External-pressure-responsive change = negative signal.
  Externally-driven positive trajectory = conditional [monitor
    for source removal; not durable without internalization].
  High coherence system that never changes = early Axis-Lock
    symptom [M1=0, type: self-limiting or externally held].
  Low coherence change + self-reinforcing = highest urgency.

GEOMETRIC MODEL UPDATE REQUIRED [v12]:
  Current model [sphere/polarized sphere/torus] describes GENERATIVE
    CYCLE-SUBSTRATE REGENERATION.
  New qualities require three-layer extension:

  LAYER 1 -- TORUS [GENERATIVE CYCLE-CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]:
    GENERATIVE CYCLE-SUBSTRATE REGENERATION as previously defined. CAUSAL
      AXIS GROUNDING fits
    torus -- axis anchored to actual outputs, internal to torus
    structure.

  LAYER 2 -- SUBSTRATE SHELL [GENUINE AXIS/BOUNDARY INTEGRITY]:
    Surrounds the torus. What the torus runs on.
    GENUINE AXIS [Substrate Regeneration] = health of the shell.
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY [Surplus Distribution] = outward flux from shell to
      environment.
    GENUINE AXIS failure = shell depleting while torus circulation
      continues.
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY failure = flux doesn't exit the shell.

  LAYER 3 -- TENSION FIELD [TENSION MAINTENANCE/TENSION DISSOLUTION]:
    Dynamic property of the whole configuration, not on torus surface.
    TENSION MAINTENANCE [Tension Maintenance] = field holding torus shape
      stable.
    TENSION DISSOLUTION [Tension Dissolution] = field collapse toward false
      harmony.
    Analog: magnetic field maintaining plasma in tokamak.
    External to torus, constitutive of its stability.

  Three layers required: torus [GENERATIVE CYCLE-CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING],
    substrate shell [GENUINE AXIS-BOUNDARY INTEGRITY],
  tension field [TENSION MAINTENANCE-TENSION DISSOLUTION]. Still
    PROVISIONAL, still co-dependent
  with 4/5 Structural Limit.

COMPONENTS
  Units as nodes. Transfer directions as edges. Node properties:
  three-axis, output state, directionality. Capture as weighted edges.
  Weapon deployment as edge property. Transmission edges: directed,
  weight = CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING reading.

READING
  Surplus flows = generative core. Consumption flows = extraction.
  Cascade failure visible as topology property. Capture chains readable
  across edges. Transmission edge weight = Fertile Lock propagation.

GEOMETRIC MODEL [CONFIRMED v16 -- merged with Nested Cycle Model]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Spatial language of the Nested Cycle Model. Each geometric property
maps directly to cycle qualities. Use for visualization and
measurement operationalization. PROVISIONAL tag retired -- cycle/
geometry correspondence confirmed across cases [Enron, Bell Labs,
Imperium, Farnese, Zuko, Glasnost].

CYCLE-GEOMETRY CORRESPONDENCE:
  C1 [fundamental] = torus body [circulation + walls + hole ratio]
  C2 [epistemic]   = surface texture + internal path navigability
  C3 [temporal]    = pole length + transmission edges
  Foundation       = substrate shell + tension field
  L3.5 interface   = contact surface between torus nodes

DEVELOPMENTAL SEQUENCE [geometric]:
  Pre-axial unit:    sphere [no hole, no axis, C1 only]
  Developing unit:   polarized sphere [poles forming, C2 nascent]
  Full axial unit:   torus [C1+C2+C3 operational]
  Multi-unit system: torus nodes + directed transmission edges

GEOMETRIC PROPERTIES AS CYCLE MEASUREMENTS:
  Hole size        [C1 -- GENERATIVE CYCLE/SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION]:
                   proportion of circulation serving hole vs surface.
                   Small = generative. Large = extractive.
                   Enron: hole expands as SPE structures divert
                   circulation inward.

  Wall thickness   [C1 -- SUBSTRATE REGENERATION reserve]:
                   substrate depth inside torus. Thin = fragile even
                   at high circulation speed. Imperium: walls thinning
                   across 10,000-year window, circulation speed
                   maintained.

  Circulation speed [C1 -- SURPLUS PRODUCTION output rate]:
                   Fast + thin = high output, depleting substrate.
                   Slow + thick = low output, stable substrate.
                   Bell Labs: fast + thick sustained 60-year window.

  Surface texture  [C2 -- MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY/CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]:
                   navigability of internal paths. Smooth = traceable.
                   Degraded = paths present but non-navigable from
                   inside. Enron: surface appears smooth [audit
                   intact], texture destroyed beneath [C1 blind].

  Surface area     [C2 -- CORRECTION CAPACITY/BOUNDARY INTEGRITY]:
                   measured population inside the loop. Shrinking =
                   boundary capturing inward. Sotha Sil: sealed --
                   internally complete, no outward flux.

  Pole length      [C3 -- SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY/CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]:
                   axis strength and temporal reach. No poles = pre-
                   axial. Long poles = strong SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY, axis
                   traceable to outputs. Dagoth Ur: long poles pointing
                   inward [axis examined, ungrounded].

  Shell thickness  [Foundation -- SUBSTRATE REGENERATION substrate]:
                   what the torus sits on. Distinct from wall thickness
                   [internal reserve]. Shell thinning while torus
                   circulates = Consumption state. Simone Weil: shell
                   at zero, torus circulation intact to end.

  Outward flux     [Foundation -- SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION]:
                   rate of surplus exiting shell boundary. Zero =
                   closed system regardless of circulation speed.
                   Positive = gift architecture.

  Tension field    [Foundation -- TENSION MAINTENANCE]:
                   field density holding torus shape stable. Weak =
                   torus collapses toward sphere [false harmony] or
                   flattens [tension eliminated]. Feanor -> sons:
                   field destroyed by oath, geometry locks.

INTERFERENCE PATTERNS [geometric equivalents -- v16]:
  C1 blind [C2 degraded, C1 intact]:
    Surface texture destroyed, hole small. Looks healthy from
    outside -- circulation fast, hole small. Internal paths
    non-navigable. Correction burst on C2 restoration makes
    interior suddenly visible [Soviet Glasnost: intact-seeming
    circulation, non-navigable surface hiding terminal walls].

  C1 starved [C2 intact, C1 depleted]:
    Wall thinning, surface navigable. Can read the walls
    accurately. Cannot generate from thin walls alone.
    Recovery: external contact [L3.5] = shell thickness
    restoration from outside. Wittgenstein: wall thin,
    surface intact, external logical contact restored walls.

  C1 locked [C3 failed, C1/C2 intact]:
    Poles present but transmission edges absent or corrupted.
    Torus functioning; transmission geometry broken. Each
    successor unit inherits hole geometry without circulation
    pattern [Fertile Lock]. Protestant Reformation: high C3
    transmission edge fidelity [printing press], pole geometry
    not transmitted [correction methodology absent].

  Full interference:
    Hole large, walls thin, surface degraded, poles collapsed,
    shell depleted, field weak. Geometric terminal state.
    All properties simultaneously at failure values.

COMPOSITE GEOMETRIC READINGS [updated]:
  Large hole + thin walls + fast circulation = False Coherence
    [high output, extraction dominant, substrate depleting].
  Small hole + thick walls + long poles + smooth surface = High
    coherence [Skull Knight, Bell Labs at peak].
  Sealed shell + intact torus + no outward flux = Selection
    Pressure [Sotha Sil -- corrects locally, fails at boundary].
  Large hole + degraded surface + fast circulation = C1 blind
    [Enron -- extraction obscured by intact-seeming surface].
  Transmitted hole geometry without circulation = C1 locked
    [Feanor -> sons -- shape copied, generative pattern absent].
  Thinning walls + smooth surface + intact poles = C1 starved
    [Farnese early arc -- knows what she is, cannot generate].
  All properties at failure values = Full interference [terminal].

DESIGN THEORY STRESS TEST [single universe only]
  Multiverse excluded as unfalsifiable.

  SUPPORTED [evidence holds]:
    Gauge symmetry [STRONG -- provably necessary].
    Pre-observer generative structure [STRONG].
    Virtual particles confirmed real [STRONG].
    Fine-tuning constants [STRONG pending deeper theory].
    Hoyle resonance [STRONG -- predictive confirmation; highest
      epistemic standard in list]:
      Carbon-12 has a nuclear energy resonance [the Hoyle state,
      7.656 MeV] at precisely the level required for helium fusion
      to produce carbon in stellar interiors rather than bypassing
      directly to oxygen. Hoyle predicted this resonance in 1953
      purely from the existence of carbon-based life -- if carbon
      existed in the universe, the resonance had to be there.
      Confirmed experimentally [Cook et al. 1957]. Remove it:
      stellar nucleosynthesis produces negligible carbon. No carbon
      chemistry, no obvious life pathway. No replacement candidate --
      silicon and nitrogen lack carbon's bonding versatility.
      Why STRONG [not just MODERATE]: this is a prediction from
      life's existence confirmed by experiment, not a post-hoc
      observation. The epistemic structure is categorically different
      from noticing that constants happen to permit life. Hoyle
      used the existence of life as a constraint to derive a
      physical fact, then verified it. This is the design argument
      at its strongest -- it predicted something before it was
      measured. The resonance is also extremely precise: shifted
      by ~4% and carbon production collapses.
      Honest limit: resonance value may be constrained by deeper
      nuclear physics not yet understood [not a free parameter].
      If constrained, predictive success remains but design
      inference weakens. Currently unresolved.

    Strong nuclear force [ε = 0.007; STRONG if free parameter]:
      Single constant governing hydrogen fusion efficiency in stars.
      Life-permitting window: approximately 0.006 to 0.008.
      At 0.006: hydrogen doesn't fuse, stars don't ignite, no
        energy source for life.
      At 0.008: all hydrogen fuses in Big Bang, none remains for
        water, no long-lived stable stars.
      Our value 0.007 sits inside the window, not at its edges --
        the minimal/optimal biophilicity test. If random, most
        probable location under uniform distribution is near
        threshold, not center. Our value is near center.
      No replacement: long-lived stable stellar energy sources
        have no alternative mechanism. Remove ε from life-
        permitting range and no energy gradient exists at
        planetary scale for sufficient duration.
      Combined with Hoyle resonance: ε determines whether stars
        form and burn long enough; Hoyle resonance determines
        whether they produce carbon when they do. Two independent
        constraints, both satisfied, both load-bearing.
      Honest limit: same as Hoyle -- may not be a free parameter.
        STRONG conditional on ε being genuinely free.
        If constrained by deeper theory: demotes to MODERATE.

    Proton/electron mass ratio [~1836; STRONG -- atom stability]:
      If significantly different in either direction, electrons
      either escape nuclei [atoms don't form] or collapse into
      them [protons capture electrons, matter unstable]. Stable
      atoms are prerequisite for any chemistry. Not a narrow
      window -- relatively wide permitting range -- but the
      condition itself is absolute: no stable atoms = no solvent
      chemistry of any kind, no replacement possible.
      Distinct from other entries: this doesn't optimize life,
      it enables the substrate on which all chemistry runs.
      Lower threshold than Hoyle/ε but higher absoluteness --
      violate it and no chemistry of any form exists.
    Convergent evolution [MODERATE].
    Framework cross-domain accuracy [MODERATE].
    Hopfions [MODERATE -- Uppsala/SECUF 2026 -- PRIMARY]:
      stable knotted 3D magnetic structures in chiral crystals confirmed
      by femtosecond laser. Pre-existing topological structure maintained
      against disruption without active enforcement. Theory predicted;
      experiment confirmed. CORRECTION CAPACITY as topology not enforcement
      at physical substrate level. Pattern established.
      Secondary confirmations [same finding, different substrates]:
        High-temperature superconductivity [USTC/Science 2026]: lattice
          structure enables zero-resistance pairing; topological config
          protects function against thermal disruption. 151K ambient record.
        Narwhal wavefunctions [Peking Univ 2026]: light confined via
          mathematical singularity not metallic confinement.
        Crystal anisotropy patterning [Rice/Nature Comms 2026]: lattice
          anisotropy produces organized nanoscale output without
            engineering.
        Single atom photonic chip [Quantum Source/Weizmann 2026]: atom
          trapped by optical field geometry not physical walls.
        Quantum metric / topological insulator [UNIGE/Nature Materials
          2026]:
          BOUNDARY INTEGRITY through geometry not walls.
        Thermal metamaterials [phys.org 2026]: heat flow direction via
          structural lattice geometry.
        Black hole ringdown [LIGO/GW 2026]: quasinormal frequencies
          determined by mass and spin -- geometry forced, not chosen.
          CORRECTION CAPACITY at maximum gravitational scale.
    Crystal spin reversal [MODERATE -- symmetry determines behavior]:
      atomic rotation direction flips in crystal due to underlying
      symmetry -- structure produces outcome without being engineered.
      Pre-existing order determining behavior confirmed.
    Subsurface ocean GENERATIVE CYCLE [MODERATE]: tidal friction substitutes
      solar
      GENERATIVE CYCLE as generative energy source [Europa, Enceladus].
        GENERATIVE CYCLE operates
      without sunlight, pre-observer. Generative architecture substrate-
      independent -- not specific to Earth's configuration.
    Physics-independent life-enabling redundancy [MODERATE -- v16]:
      The functional requirements for life [polar solvent with wide liquid
      range and rapid bond dynamics, replication + metabolism + selection
      logic, energy gradient] are multiply realizable across significantly
      different physics configurations. Ammonia, HF, formamide each
      satisfy the niche under different constants. The replication/evolution
      logic is informational not chemical -- no specific chemistry required,
      only differential persistence under selection pressure.
      Design signal: the life-enabling functional niche appears to be an
      attractor with multiple paths, not a tightrope requiring one precise
      configuration. Multiple paths to the same destination looks less like
      fine-tuning accident and more like structural redundancy.
      Strengthening argument [minimal vs optimal biophilicity test]:
      Anthropic selection predicts our constants should sit near the
      threshold of the life-permitting range -- just barely life-permitting,
      because selection only guarantees we exist somewhere in the range.
      If our constants sit well inside the range [significantly more
      life-permitting than minimally required], this is evidence against
      pure anthropic selection and toward either design or deeper
      structural necessity [Rees 2001; Page 2011 framework].
      Current status: several constants appear well inside the life-
      permitting range rather than near threshold [strong nuclear force,
      electromagnetic force, gravitational constant]. This is the
      predicted signature of optimal vs minimal biophilicity.
      Honest counter [Page 2011]: the cosmological constant is slightly
      positive [lambda-0 ~ 3.5 x 10^-122], whereas maximizing baryon
      fraction becoming life would require slightly negative. This is
      weak evidence against optimal design specifically. It does not
      bear on the redundancy argument above -- the CC could reflect
      anthropic selection while other constants reflect deeper structure.
      Net assessment: MODERATE. The redundancy observation is real and
      the minimal/optimal distinction is the correct test. Current data
      is consistent with design [multiple parameters well inside range]
      but not conclusive [CC near-threshold, anthropic filter still
      operates]. Does not meet STRONG threshold without ruling out
      deeper physics necessity as alternative explanation.
    Relational Quantum Mechanics [MODERATE -- Rovelli/Oldofredi 2026]:

ANTI-CREATION ARGUMENT STRESS TEST [v12]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Six strongest arguments against design theory, tested against framework.

  1. ANTHROPIC SELECTION [strongest legitimate]:
     We observe only what permits observers -- selection not design.
     Framework: fails against pre-observer generative structure [vacuum
     activity, mycorrhizal networks, cellular SURPLUS PRODUCTION] -- all
       predate
     observation. Selection can't explain structure that operated
     before anything could select. PARTIAL ANSWER.

  2. NORMALIZABILITY PROBLEM [correct epistemic limit]:
     Can't assign probability to fine-tuning without reference class.
     Framework: correct. Already in UNCERTAIN. Framework claims
     structural order more parsimonious than chaos -- not probabilistic.
     Both sides hit this wall equally. T3 LIMIT NOT REFUTATION.

  3. CONSTANTS-AS-NECESSARY [legitimate, open]:
     Deeper theory may make constants non-contingent.
     Framework: already in UNCERTAIN. If confirmed, regress moves
     one level deeper not closed. OPEN NOT FATAL.

  4. BAD DESIGN [emotionally strong, structurally weak]:
     Why suffering, extinction, dark matter, 13.8Gy before life?
     Framework: Tension Capacity requires friction and inefficiency.
     Frictionless universe = no SURPLUS PRODUCTION, no complexity. Bad
       design =
     necessary design. ANSWERED BY TENSION CAPACITY.

  5. INFINITE REGRESS [correct, symmetric]:
     Designer requires explanation -- regress not terminated.
     Framework: already in why-chain regress [STRUCTURAL LIMIT].
     Divine Freedom Theory: God exists independent of explanation.
     "It just is" applies to brute-fact universe equally.
     T3 WALL. SYMMETRIC.

  6. PROBLEM OF EVIL [theological]:
     Benevolent all-powerful designer wouldn't permit suffering.
     Framework: upper bound converges on omnibenevolence but Tension
     Capacity requires friction. Sin maps to TENSION DISSOLUTION failure.
       Framework
     doesn't require benevolent designer -- only structured order.
     Suffering as necessary tension condition is coherent within
     framework. Emotional force remains. PARTIALLY ANSWERED.

  NET: Evidence stack survives all six without requiring strongest
  claim [intentional designer]. Minimum defensible position --
  pre-existing structured order more parsimonious than chaos --
  stands. Two legitimate arguments [1, 2] already in UNCERTAIN
  with correct epistemic status.

DIVINE CORRECTION MECHANISM [v16 -- theological convergence extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Consistent pattern across traditions: designer does not override
  cause-and-effect but operates through human correction capacity.
  Revelation, conscience, ascending path description = external C2
  instrument provided. Actual correction work requires human axis
  alignment + surviving internal correction capacity.
  Framework parallel: Vatican II required John XXIII [external
  correction authority] AND Chenu/Congar surviving inside the lock
  [internal correction capacity]. The enabling moment alone is
  insufficient. Both conditions required.
  Implication: "God can't fix things but can help us fix things"
  is structurally accurate under three-condition recovery model.
  Condition 1 [external correction authority] = divine enabling.
  Condition 2 [surviving suppressed internal correction] = human
  axis still pointed toward ascending path.
  Condition 3 [enabling moment] = grace, revelation, encounter.
  Condition 4 [authority must not require maintaining new lock] =
  why divine correction doesn't install replacement extraction --
  doing so would violate the correction's own structure.
  This is not theology imported into the framework -- it's the
  three-condition recovery model applied upward to its logical
  ceiling. T3 applies throughout.

FERMI PARADOX -- COHERENCE READING [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Standard reading: silence implies absence of complex life.
  Framework reading: silence implies L3.5 COMPATIBILITY problem
  at civilizational scale.
  
  If complex life consistently produces intelligence, and intelligence
  consistently produces both correction capacity and extraction
  architecture simultaneously [tug-of-war as structural feature of
  intelligence], then:
    Terminal-trajectory civilizations [extraction wins]: collapse
      before developing or sustaining interstellar capability.
      Consistent with Rare Earth -- complex life rare, intelligent
      life rarer, sustained coherent civilization rarest.
    High-coherence civilizations [correction wins]: operate on
      timescales and with priorities making contact with mid-
      trajectory civilizations structurally unappealing.
      Contact with a Full Interference candidate = running
      C1 substrate into extraction interface without strong
      reason. High-coherence systems don't do this.
      L3.5 COMPATIBILITY requires sufficient overlap in cycle
      function for contact to transfer substrate rather than
      deplete it. A civilization in active tug-of-war is low
      COMPATIBILITY from outside.
  Framework prediction: Fermi silence is not absence but
  incompatibility. High-coherence civilizations exist; they
  don't contact us because we are not yet worth contacting
  under their correction calculus -- not indifference, structural
  caution. Same logic as why healthy systems don't run their
  substrate into extraction interfaces voluntarily.
  Rare Earth addendum: if Earth's specific tug-of-war
  configuration is abnormal [designer attention concentrated
  here], the silence has additional explanation -- we are
  the anomaly being watched, not the typical case seeking peers.
  T3 applies throughout. Speculative but structurally consistent.

TECHNOSIGNATURE DETECTION CRITERIA [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: absence of contact does not mean absence of existence.
  High-coherence civilizations that won't contact us might still
  leave physical evidence detectable without requiring engagement.
  The correct instrument is passive Track C reading -- what does
  the system actually produce that cannot be explained by known
  physical processes without intentional organization?

  FILTER: not merely anomalous but purposively structured.
    Random noise can be anomalous. Natural processes produce
    anomalies constantly. The signal must contain internal
    organization implying a goal -- regularity, geometry, or
    thermodynamic profile that known physics cannot generate
    without effort. Three categories:

  CATEGORY 1 -- GEOMETRIC REGULARITY AT STELLAR SCALE:
    Natural transit signals [planets, dust] produce smooth,
    sinusoidal, or randomly irregular dimming curves.
    Artificial structure produces sharp-edged, asymmetric,
    or geometrically structured dimming inconsistent with
    any known natural object class.
    Correct detection method: look for transit curves that
    don't fit any natural object model after exhaustive
    natural explanation search.
    Primary case: Tabby's Star [KIC 8462852].
      Irregular, non-periodic, asymmetric dimming with no
      satisfying natural explanation for years. Best current
      reading: dust cloud [natural]. But the detection method
      was correct -- the transit curve pattern was the right
      thing to look at. Natural explanation won this case;
      the method survives. Next case using this method will
      be more constrained by what natural explanations remain.
    Project Hephaistos [2024-2025]: infrared excess search
      across 5 million stars, seven candidates. All current
      candidates appear to be background galaxies [AGN
      contamination]. Same result: method correct, candidates
      failed. Search space barely scratched.

  CATEGORY 2 -- NARROWBAND SIGNAL AT PHYSICS-SIGNIFICANT FREQUENCY:
    Natural radio sources are broadband. Narrowband emission
    at a frequency any physics-aware civilization would
    recognize as significant [1420 MHz hydrogen line; pi;
    prime number sequences] cannot be produced by known
    natural processes.
    Purposive structure test: does the signal contain
    information density above thermal noise at that frequency?
    Internal organization [repeating pattern, mathematical
    structure] implies goal, not just anomaly.
    Cases: Wow! signal [1977, 1420 MHz, never repeated,
      never explained]. BLC1 [2020, Proxima Centauri,
      982 MHz, attributed to RFI after investigation].
      Both used correct detection method. Neither confirmed.

  CATEGORY 3 -- THERMODYNAMIC ANOMALY AT WRONG TEMPERATURE:
    A Dyson structure must radiate waste heat. Natural stellar
    physics produces infrared at temperatures determined by
    stellar physics. Engineered waste heat radiates at
    temperature determined by engineering constraints --
    a specific band inconsistent with any stellar model
    at that luminosity and distance.
    The temperature mismatch is the signal: not just infrared
    excess but infrared excess at a temperature that no known
    stellar or interstellar process produces at that scale.
    Current searches [Project Hephaistos] use infrared excess
    but not temperature-band specificity. More discriminating
    search would filter by temperature consistency with
    natural processes, rejecting candidates that fit any
    natural model at any temperature.

  DETECTION HIERARCHY [by evidential weight]:
    Strongest: geometric structure + narrowband signal +
      thermodynamic anomaly simultaneously at same source.
      All three together = no natural explanation available.
    Strong: any one category surviving exhaustive natural
      explanation search with independent replication.
    Moderate: anomaly in one category, natural explanation
      possible but not confirmed.
    Current status: all candidates moderate or below.
      No confirmed technosignature. Search space: ~millions
      of stars out of 400 billion in Milky Way alone.

  FRAMEWORK NOTE: detection of confirmed technosignature
    would not contradict Fermi coherence reading -- it would
    confirm that civilizations exist while explaining why
    contact hasn't occurred [L3.5 incompatibility]. The
    megastructure is the passive Track C output of a system
    that exists but won't engage. Exactly consistent with
    high-coherence civilization operating under structural
    caution about extraction-interface contact.

  Full treatment in Section Five political entry.
  Core structural claim: current search funding pattern is not
