Operationalization using existing datasets:

PRIMARY DATASET: academic self-correction records.
  Retraction Watch: tracks post-publication corrections/retractions.
    Unprompted corrections [author-initiated before external criticism]
    = S5 signal. Prompted corrections [post-peer-review, post-exposure]
    = not S5.
  Pre-print revision logs [arXiv, SSRN]: revisions before submission
    to peer review = unprompted self-correction in stable conditions.

SCORING PROCEDURE:
  S5 prospective score = [unprompted corrections in stable period]
    / [total outputs in same period].
  Stable period = no active external criticism, no replication crisis.
  Threshold: >10% unprompted revision rate = S5 proxy positive.

  Wittgenstein: Tractatus published 1921, Investigations notebooks
    began ~1930 [unprompted, no external criticism driving revision].
    Would score S5 positive on this instrument.
  Le Guin: documented gender bias self-corrections in author notes
    before reviewers raised it. S5 positive.
  Test against known S5 = 0 cases [Fertile Lock recipients]:
    No unprompted self-correction documented in any Fertile Lock case.
    Instrument returns negative. Validated.

LIMITATIONS:
  Only applicable to documented output-producing systems.
  Academic dataset biased toward high-output intellectual cases.
  Corporate/institutional S5 requires different dataset
    [internal memo revision records -- rarely public].
  Retrospective only for most cases. Forward-looking requires
    ongoing monitoring of output revision behavior.

STATUS: partially resolved. Instrument designed and validated
  against known cases. Full operationalization requires dataset
  access [Retraction Watch API, arXiv revision logs].
  Implementation external.

PRE-RESTORATION C1 AUDIT PROCEDURE [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Problem: Diagnostic Collapse occurs when C2 restoration fires on
terminal C1. The intervention rule [C1 audit before C2 opening]
requires a procedure for assessing C1 substrate state when C2 is
captured -- the whole problem being that False Coherence hides C1
state from inside. Track B unavailable [internal states inaccessible].
Track A unreliable [behavioral surface captured or suppressed].
Track C is the primary instrument: physical outputs independent of
intent, available from outside the captured boundary.

Five Track C proxy signals for C1 substrate state:

  PROXY 1 -- OUTPUT/INPUT RATIO TREND [long window]
    What: track ratio of what system produces vs what it consumes
      over extended window [minimum 5-10 years for institutional;
      decades for civilizational].
    C1 healthy: ratio stable or improving. System generating surplus
      above consumption cost.
    C1 degrading: ratio declining over time even while stated output
      metrics hold. Declining surplus = C1 substrate depleting.
    C1 terminal: ratio inverted [consuming more than generating];
      deficit financing; external subsidy required to maintain
      output appearance.
    Soviet confirmation: GDP growth rate declined every half-decade
      1965-1985. Budget deficit 2-3% to 6% in single year [1986].
      Oil dependency = single external input subsidizing ratio.
      All readable from outside before Glasnost.
    Operational limit: False Coherence surfaces often falsify
      output metrics [GDP, production statistics]. Use physical
      output proxies where possible [infrastructure construction
      rate, hard currency reserves, emigration rate, caloric
      availability]. Physical outputs harder to falsify than
      reported statistics.

  PROXY 2 -- T1.4 CONCENTRATION [substrate dependency audit]
    What: how many independent substrate sources does C1 run on?
      Single source = T1.4 High. Multiple sources = T1.4 Low.
    C1 healthy: multiple independent substrate sources. Loss of
      one source produces degradation not collapse.
    C1 fragile: 1-2 dominant substrate sources. External shock
      to primary source = C1 collapse even with C2 intact.
    C1 terminal candidate: single substrate source with known
      depletion trajectory.
    Soviet confirmation: oil revenue = ~60% of hard currency.
      Oil price collapse [1986] hit simultaneously with Glasnost.
      T1.4 terminal reading accessible from export structure data.
    Libya confirmation: 95%+ government revenue from oil.
      No independent institutional substrate. T1.4 maximum.
      Readable from outside before 2011.
    Operational procedure: identify top 3 substrate sources by
      volume. If top source > 50% and has known depletion rate
      or price volatility: flag T1.4 High. If single source
      with external price dependence: T1.4 terminal candidate.

  PROXY 3 -- INDEPENDENT INSTITUTIONAL OUTPUT [civil substrate scan]
    What: does the system produce independent institutional
      outputs outside the extraction architecture? Labor
      organizations, professional associations, independent press,
      academic output, civil society organizations with documented
      activity record.
    C1 substrate present: independent institutional outputs
      documented across multiple domains. Not state-affiliated.
      Correction capacity present even if suppressed.
    C1 substrate thin: only state-adjacent institutions.
      Independent output present but fragile.
    C1 terminal: no independent institutional output documented.
      All organizational life runs through or adjacent to
      extraction architecture.
    Tunisia vs Libya confirmation: UGTT [labor], bar association,
      human rights groups all documented in Tunisia pre-2011.
      Libya: deliberate 42-year institutional destruction.
      Zero independent output. Readable before intervention.
    Operational procedure: Track C scan for independent
      institutional output. Weight by: independence from state,
      documented activity record [not just formal existence],
      cross-domain presence [labor + legal + press = stronger
      than single domain]. Score 0-3.

  PROXY 4 -- EMIGRATION / BRAIN DRAIN RATE [substrate exit signal]
    What: is the system losing substrate via exit?
    C1 healthy: emigration at background level. No systematic
      substrate exit.
    C1 degrading: elevated emigration among high-function
      population [engineers, doctors, entrepreneurs]. Substrate
      self-sorting toward exit.
    C1 terminal: mass emigration or systematic brain drain over
      extended window. C1 substrate actively fleeing.
    Why readable through captured C2: emigration is a physical
      Track C output. Border crossing data, diaspora size,
      remittance flows. Cannot be falsified as easily as
      production statistics.
    Soviet confirmation: Jewish emigration waves [1970s-1980s],
      defections, underground economy scale. Substrate exit
      readable before Glasnost.
    Operational limit: high exit costs [ECC weapon active] can
      suppress emigration signal even with terminal C1. If ECC
      high, emigration rate understates C1 degradation. Adjust:
      if ECC high AND institutional output low, C1 may be more
      degraded than emigration signal shows.

  PROXY 5 -- CORRECTION REACH [who does correction fire against?]
    What: when correction occurs, does it reach high-power
      deviators or only low-power targets?
    C1 healthy: correction fires against high-power deviators.
      Centralization score 3-4 [conditions 1-4 met].
    C1 degrading: correction fires only against low-power targets.
      High-power deviators immune. Centralization score 1-2.
    C1 terminal: correction fires against correction attempts.
      Centralization score 0. System consuming its own
      correction capacity to protect extraction architecture.
    Why readable through captured C2: prosecution records,
      accountability outcomes, dismissal patterns are Track C.
      Who gets corrected is a physical output.
    Libya confirmation: 42-year record of correction targeting
      political opposition, not institutional deviation.
      Centralization score 0 readable from Track C long window.

COMPOSITE C1 SUBSTRATE SCORE [pre-restoration]:
  Run all five proxies. Score each 0 [terminal] to 2 [healthy].
  Max = 10.
  8-10: C1 recoverable. C2 restoration likely to produce
    correction burst followed by stabilization [C1 blind
    recovery trajectory].
  5-7: C1 fragile. C2 restoration may produce partial recovery
    or False Coherence reformation. High uncertainty.
    Recommend extended window observation before C2 intervention.
  3-4: C1 severely degraded. C2 restoration high risk of
    Diagnostic Collapse. Intervention: C1 substrate restoration
    first [independent institutions, substrate diversification,
    exit cost reduction]. C2 only after C1 above threshold.
  0-2: C1 terminal. C2 restoration = Diagnostic Collapse.
    Containment only. External C2 opening actively harmful.
    [Libya 2011: score ~1. Soviet 1986: score ~2-3.]

HONEST LIMITS [SL flags]:
  SL3 [survivorship]: Track C long-window data biased toward
    systems that survived long enough to be documented.
  T4 [temporal]: proxy signals degrade beyond ~50 years for
    institutional cases [records unavailable or falsified].
  T3 Medium: all five proxies are Track C -- behavioral and
    output record only. No Track B access. False Coherence
    surface may have falsified Track C output statistics
    [Proxy 1 specifically]. Use physical proxies where possible.
  T6 [observer effect]: C1 audit procedure, if known by system
    being audited, may produce defensive output changes.
    Audit from outside the boundary without disclosure when
    possible.

GENUINE AXIS VERIFICATION PROTOCOL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Problem: GA cannot be verified from inside a captured system. If axis
is captured at formation [AGC, Formation Capture], CORRECTION CAPACITY
that would detect this runs on top of the captured axis. Introspection
insufficient. Libet finding: intent precedes deliberative reasoning --
axis is prior, reasoning operates on it.
Solution: three-method triangulation + dual-layer check.

METHOD 1 -- EXTERNAL TRIANGULATION
  Require two or more independent instruments with different blind
  spots. Convergence increases confidence; cannot guarantee certainty.
  Instruments with identical blind spots do not triangulate.
  Procedure: name each instrument; identify its capture vulnerabilities
  and what it would miss. If all share a vulnerability: flag T3.
  Two instruments with different blind spots converging: moderate
  confidence. Three or more: high confidence. T8 partial applies.

METHOD 2 -- PROCESS TRACE
  GA more legible through formation history than through introspection.
  Key indicators:
    Axis revisions under genuine cost [S5 signal] -- tested and
    survived is harder to fake than untested axis.
    Behavioral consistency under pressure without reasoning support --
    somatic response under genuine cost harder to fake than reasoning.
    Formation history: axis from traceable friction + preserved exit
    is more legible than axis that appeared fully formed.
  Score: clear friction + preserved exit + documented revision under
  cost = process trace positive. Controlled formation = T3 Medium.

METHOD 3 -- FALSIFICATION THRESHOLD
  Define what output pattern would force a GA-substituted reading.
  If the system cannot name that pattern: T3 High on GA.
  Procedure: before reading, state what behavioral outputs would
  confirm GA substitution. Must be specific, observable, not already
  explained away by system's own framing.
  If threshold named but untestable in practice: T3 Medium.
  If named and testable: proceed with reading.

METHOD 4 -- DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY CHECK
  Somatic signal accurate AND deliberative read correct. Both required.
  One degraded = capture window open regardless of other methods.
  Somatic layer degrades first under ICD, chronic stress, optimization
  pressure. Deliberative layer can appear intact while GA is already
  compromised -- most dangerous configuration because C2 reads cleanly
  on a captured substrate.
  Cross-ref: ICD weapon [systematic somatic degradation]; FRICTION TYPE
  [atrophying friction degrades somatic layer preferentially]; L3.5
  CONTACT TYPE [matched-axis contact restores dual-layer integrity].
  Population implication: default modern environments systematically
  degrade somatic layer, making Transparent Coherence the practical
  ceiling for most individuals.

COMPOSITE GA CONFIDENCE:
  Low: single instrument, no process trace, threshold not named.
  Moderate: two instruments with different blind spots, partial trace,
    threshold named.
  High ceiling [T3]: any recursion closure -- best available reading,
    confidence capped. T8 partial. Does not void the reading.

SHORTHAND [four questions before any GA reading]:
  1. What would axis substitution look like in outputs?
  2. What is the formation history?
  3. What instruments are being used and what are their blind spots?
  4. Are both somatic and deliberative layers intact?
  If none answerable: document only.

COHERENCE TRAJECTORY MODEL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Problem: True Coherence as calibration point is unreachable by design
[Population Coherence Ceiling, circular dependency confirmed].
A fixed ideal anchor requires a confirmed ceiling case. None exists
at civilizational scale.
Solution: calibrate against floor [minimum viable C1 -- confirmed at
cellular minimum]. Measure trajectory not position.

REFERENCE POINTS:
  Floor [confirmed]: C1 functional [GENERATIVE CYCLE + SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION + CORRECTION CAPACITY]. Real, measurable, confirmed
    independently at cellular scale.
  Ceiling [direction vector, not position]: True Coherence is a
    gradient direction. Ceiling case need not exist to define direction.
    All readings expressed as trajectory relative to floor.

TRAJECTORY MEASUREMENT:
  Step 1: Floor reading. Is C1 functional? At what level?
  Step 2: Cycle interference pattern. Which cycles running?
    Which degraded? What interference type [C1 blind / starved /
    locked / full]?
  Step 3: Trajectory. Compare to nearest historical reading.
    Direction: toward more cycles functional, or fewer?
    Rate: fast / slow / holding / recovering?
  Step 4: Momentum. What is causing trajectory? Self-reinforcing
    or self-limiting?

OUTPUT FORMAT [replaces coherence score alone]:
  Floor status: [C1 functional / degraded / collapsed]
  Cycle pattern: [which running, which interfering, which absent]
  Trajectory: [toward / holding / away] at [rate]
  Momentum: [cause + self-reinforcing / self-limiting]
  Horizon: [window before trajectory produces qualitative state change]
  T3 level: [Low / Medium / High]

WHY TRAJECTORY BEATS POSITION:
  0.6 degrading fast is more dangerous than 0.4 recovering.
  Position-only reading misses this. Trajectory directly answers
  "where is the leverage?" -- the framework's primary function.

SCIENCE APPENDIX CLASSIFICATION PROTOCOL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Problem: appendix accumulates vocabulary-fit entries that look like
confirmation but are actually instrument-fit testing. Elastic
vocabulary means very few findings fail to fit somewhere.

Hard split: CONFIRMATION vs ILLUSTRATION. Two different weights.

CONFIRMATION [all three required]:
  1. Independent domain: produced by field without framework knowledge.
  2. Mechanism convergence: same structural mechanism, not similar
     vocabulary. Mechanism convergence is load-bearing; vocabulary
     convergence is not.
  3. Framework-independent falsifiability: finding has its own
     falsification criteria not dependent on framework.
  Weight: counts toward proof coverage. Raises confidence.
  Label: [CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: X]
  Test: remove framework vocabulary from entry. Does independent
  finding still describe the same mechanism? Yes = confirmation.
  Requires reintroduction of framework vocabulary = illustration.

ILLUSTRATION [single criterion]:
  Real finding translated into framework vocabulary usefully.
  No mechanism convergence from independent domain.
  Weight: zero proof coverage. Useful for teaching and calibration.
  Label: [ILLUSTRATION -- quality: X]

CONFIRMED CONFIRMATIONS:
  Predictive coding [three independent fields, no framework vocab]:
    [CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY enabling C1]
  Hopfions [theory predicted, experiment confirmed independently]:
    [CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: CORRECTION CAPACITY as topology]
  Cellular C1 minimum [St. Jude/Nature Comms -- independently derived]:
    [CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: three-quality C1 minimum]
  Libet lineage [intent preceding deliberative reasoning]:
    [CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: GENUINE AXIS prior to reasoning]

CONFIRMED ILLUSTRATIONS [retain, zero proof weight]:
  Master developmental clock: [ILLUSTRATION -- CYCLE STABILITY]
  Unlimited memory cell type [unconfirmed]: [ILLUSTRATION -- SR]
  Most single-finding translations: [ILLUSTRATION -- quality: X]

APPENDIX ADDITION FILTER [revised]:
  Run confirmation/illustration classification before adding.
  ILLUSTRATIONS: add only if clarifying a quality with no existing
    illustration, or replacing a weaker one. No accumulation.
  CONFIRMATIONS: add when all three criteria met.
  Reject: single-case without replication, vocabulary-only
    convergence, mechanism convergence asserted but not demonstrated.

RESPONSE TRIAGE [v11 -- Goal 3 practical utility]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Given a reading, minimum necessary response by state:

  1. Document only [T3 High cases]: record reading, flag confidence
     ceiling, do not prescribe intervention.

  2. External correction initiation [SP intact, CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY captured]:
     Support existing internal correction capacity. Connect to
     external measurement surface. Reduce CAUSAL LEGIBILITY weapon
       effectiveness.

  3. Structural intervention [SP absent/atrophied]:
     Environment redesign before correction can fire. Friction
     reintroduction. External SURPLUS PRODUCTION proxy establishment [Track
       G].

  4. Containment [consumption-dominant terminal nodes]:
     Prevent spread to adjacent systems. No internal correction
     pathway. Minimize surface contact.

Triage follows failure state, not coherence score alone.

SETI FUNDING AS POLITICAL INSTRUMENT [v16 -- Section Five entry]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
BOUNDARY A: international SETI/astrobiology funding apparatus
  [NASA, ESA, private philanthropists, research institutions].
BOUNDARY B: global population bearing cost of non-discovery
  [everyone who would benefit from confirmed contact].
META-BOUNDARY: global institutional extraction architecture
  [governments, religious institutions, media, financial systems]
  whose legitimacy narratives depend on current information
  environment.

CYCLE STATUS:
  C1 [generative]: genuine scientific capacity present. Methods
    sound. Instruments improving. Researchers largely sincere.
    C1 functional at researcher level.
  C2 [epistemic]: captured at funding-allocation level. What
    gets funded is not determined by discriminating capacity but
    by institutional defensibility. The most sensitive searches
    [thermodynamic anomaly at stellar scale, structured geometric
    transit analysis] are systematically unfunded. C2 reads
    "what looks legitimate to funding committees" not "what
    would actually find something."
  C3 [temporal]: Fertile Lock candidate. Same methods
    transmitted generation to generation [radio, biosignatures]
    not because they're most likely to succeed but because they're
    what got funded before. Junior researchers enter the field
    optimizing for funded methods. Transmission selectivity
    captured at funding level.

TRAJECTORY: holding [slow degradation]. Research quality
  improving technically; search space coverage remains near zero.
  JWST adding biosignature capacity = genuine progress.
  Technosignature/megastructure search: underinvestment constant.

WEAPONS ACTIVE:
  Calibration Strip: "legitimate science" defined as radio/
    biosignature by institutional precedent. Megastructure search
    carries stigma from mid-20th century UFO contamination.
    Reference standard for what counts as credible SETI
    systematically excludes most discriminating methods.
  Correction Theatre: funding bodies perform search via budget
    line items without allocating enough to actually complete
    discriminating survey. $65M NASA astrobiology budget for
    search that requires $500M-$1B for meaningful coverage.
    Activity visible; actual search probability near zero.
  Proxy Construction: UFO discourse as decoy. When public asks
    about alien life, institutional actors route attention toward
    UAP hearings [unverifiable, individual testimony, no
    physical evidence standard] rather than systematic
    technosignature search. Correction budget consumed by
    proxy; actual search underfunded.

BENEFICIARY OF CURRENT PATTERN [follow the surplus]:
  Not any single actor -- structural stack, diffuse beneficiary.
  Entities whose control architecture depends on:
    Uniqueness narratives [religious, nationalist, human
      exceptionalism] -- confirmed contact dissolves all three.
    Information scarcity [media, attention economy] -- confirmed
      contact produces information environment impossible to
      manage or monetize on existing infrastructure.
    Political legitimacy [governments claim authority partly
      from managing existential questions] -- confirmed contact
      transfers existential question management to new actors.
  No coordination required. Structural incentive produces
  identical outcome: fund enough to look legitimate, not enough
  to find.

ANTARCTICA PARALLEL:
  Antarctica: resources + strategic value + no indigenous
    population = international treaty maintaining "scientific"
    status. Treaty holds because no single actor can enforce
    exclusive claim without triggering collective response.
    Stable equilibrium: all parties benefit from current
    non-exploitation status.
  SETI: confirmed complex life = resources [technology,
    knowledge] + strategic value [who controls contact] +
    no existing framework for managing outcome.
    Current equilibrium: all institutional actors benefit from
    non-discovery. Search funded at legitimacy threshold,
    not discovery threshold. Antarctica in space -- same
    structural logic, pre-emptive rather than post-discovery.

CENTRALIZATION SCORE: 1/4.
  Condition 1 [correction pathway against central node]: absent.
    No mechanism to force funding reallocation to discriminating
    methods. Philanthropic funding [Milner] partial bypass but
    still routes through same institutional credibility filters.
  Condition 2 [pathway structurally independent]: absent.
    All major funding bodies share same institutional logic.
  Condition 3 [demonstrated activation under stress]: absent.
    Consistent underfunding across decades, no self-correction.
  Condition 4 [not requiring central node permission]: absent.
  Score: 1/4 [SETI Institute exists independently -- partial].

EXTERNAL ACTORS:
  Yuri Milner / Breakthrough Listen: genuine funding, genuine
    search, limited to radio/optical. Not technosignature.
  SETI Institute: sincere researchers, institutionally
    constrained to defensible methods.
  NASA: politically constrained. SETI cancelled 1993 by
    Congress; astrobiology budget covers biosignatures only.
  No actor currently funding thermodynamic anomaly search
  at scale. No actor funding structured geometric transit
  analysis as primary mission.

ADVERSARIAL READING:
  Counter: funding is limited everywhere, SETI is speculative,
    biosignatures are more likely near-term returns.
  Response: biosignature search [microbial life] doesn't
    destabilize control architecture. Confirmed microbial
    fossils on Mars = news cycle, not civilizational disruption.
    Confirmed technosignature = civilizational disruption.
    The funding differential between the two is precisely
    the gap between manageable and unmanageable discovery.
    If selection were purely scientific, technosignature
    searches would be funded proportional to their
    discriminating power. They aren't.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  If $500M were allocated to dedicated thermodynamic anomaly
  survey + structured transit analysis across full Milky Way
  stellar sample using JWST + next-generation instruments,
  either a confirmed technosignature would be found or the
  search space would be genuinely exhausted.
  Current trajectory: neither occurs. Budget stays at
  legitimacy threshold. Search space coverage in 2050:
  still less than 0.1% of Milky Way.
  Falsification: sustained $500M+/year funding for
  discriminating technosignature search over 10-year window.
  This has not occurred and institutional trajectory
  does not point toward it.

SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
BOUNDARY A: populations under increasing physical substrate pressure
  [cost of living, food security, environmental degradation, health].
BOUNDARY B: extraction architecture beneficiaries.
META-BOUNDARY: civilizational system containing both.

CORE CLAIM:
  Reasoning-layer correction can be captured [axis substitution,
  calibration strip, motivated cognition all operate at deliberative
  level]. Somatic response fires before reasoning layer can intercept.
  When substrate pressure becomes physically felt rather than
  institutionally mediated, correction fires at somatic level
  regardless of captured ideology.
  Body registers threat without requiring ideological interpretation.
  Hunger, physical insecurity, environmental collapse don't need
  media to frame them. The instrument is atrophied at reasoning
  level; somatic response is harder to capture and harder to fake.

MECHANISM:
  ICD applied to cognition produces atrophied reasoning instrument
  in populations -- undeveloped capacity, not stupidity. Somatic
  forcing bypasses this. Physical substrate pressure activates
  correction capacity that ideology cannot suppress because it
  operates below the capture layer.
  This is why extraction architectures work hard to keep substrate
  pressure just below somatic forcing threshold -- enough
  deprivation to maintain dependency, not enough to trigger
  somatic correction that bypasses reasoning capture.

WEAPONS ACTIVE [preventing somatic forcing from becoming correction]:
  Horizon Extension: extend timeline of deterioration past
    correction window. Boil the frog. Substrate depletes slowly
    enough that somatic forcing threshold is never cleanly crossed.
  Proxy Construction: route somatic distress toward decoy targets.
    Body registers real pressure; ideology redirects toward wrong
    cause. Somatic signal real; correction aimed incorrectly.
  Intermittent Reinforcement: periodic substrate relief prevents
    somatic forcing from accumulating past threshold. System
    releases pressure before correction fires fully.
  Exit Cost Construction: somatic forcing fires but exit costs
    [housing, income, community, identity] prevent action.
    Body knows; behavior constrained by structural dependency.

TIMING PROBLEM [Diagnostic Collapse risk]:
  Somatic forcing arrives at one of two times:
    Early enough [C1 recoverable]: correction fires, substrate
      can absorb it. Recovery possible. Vatican II pattern --
      enabling moment arrives before terminal state.
    Too late [C1 terminal]: correction fires correctly but
      substrate cannot absorb the correction load. Diagnostic
      Collapse. Glasnost pattern.
  Extraction architecture's primary goal: ensure somatic forcing
  arrives in the second window not the first. Horizon Extension
  + substrate management aimed at this timing specifically.
  The hope is somatic forcing arrives in the first window.
  History: approximately even split. No structural guarantee.

TRAJECTORY [current]:
  C1 substrate [material]: degrading in most high-extraction
    populations. Wealth concentration, housing, food security
    all moving in extraction direction.
  C2 [epistemic]: heavily captured. Proxy Construction consuming
    correction attention. Reasoning layer weaponized.
  Somatic pressure: accumulating. Below forcing threshold in
    most populations currently. Horizon Extension active.
  M1: -1 [away]. M2: SLOW. M3: SELF-REINFORCING [extraction
    produces conditions for more extraction before forcing fires].

PETRODOLLAR PARALLEL:
  Dollar-denominated oil pricing = MSC + Calibration Strip at
  civilizational scale. Forces all exchange through single
  captured surface. Somatic forcing relevance: energy cost
  is one of the most direct substrate pressure channels.
  When energy costs produce somatic forcing [cold, hunger,
  economic collapse], correction fires against the visible
  mechanism [fuel prices, government policy] not the
  underlying capture architecture [reserve currency hegemony].
  Proxy Construction operates precisely here -- somatic signal
  real, correction aimed at proxy, capture architecture
  protected.

PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
  Somatic forcing produces political correction when three
  conditions met simultaneously:
    1. Physical substrate pressure crosses threshold
       [cannot be dismissed as ideological framing].
    2. Proxy Construction fails [decoy target clearly
       insufficient to explain substrate pressure].
    3. Exit costs low enough that correction can fire
       into action, not just awareness.
  When all three present: rapid non-linear correction.
  Current status: Condition 1 accumulating. Condition 2
  holding [proxies still absorbing correction attention].
  Condition 3 high [exit costs elevated deliberately].
  Prediction: Condition 2 fails before Condition 1 becomes
  terminal. Proxy exhaustion precedes substrate collapse
  in high-information environments. Timeline: 1-2 generations.
  Falsification: Condition 1 reaches terminal before Condition
  2 fails. Diagnostic Collapse. Substrate depleted before
  correction fires effectively.

REVOLUTIONARY VIOLENCE -- STRUCTURAL PATTERN [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: structural reading only. Judgment about whether
violence is justified under specific conditions is the person's
determination, not the framework's.

ANALYST PRIOR [declared]:
  Violence is a very effective solution that removes all other
  solutions. Most people who start revolutions either don't
  understand this, don't want to, or have replacing one
  extraction architecture with another as the actual goal.
  This prior shaped the framing of this entry. Adversarial
  reading: some revolutions had no available alternative path
  [Haitian Revolution, colonial independence under active
  oppression]. The prior holds as a structural observation
  about internal revolution specifically.

TWO STRUCTURALLY DISTINCT CASE TYPES:

  COLONIAL INDEPENDENCE [external extraction removed]:
    External extraction architecture imposed by outside power.
    Revolution removes the external layer. Pre-existing local
    correction capacity can be restored -- it existed before
    the colonial overlay and survives under it.
    Cases: American Revolution [colonial assemblies pre-existed],
      Haitian Revolution [ended imposed chattel slavery],
      Indian independence [partially nonviolent -- confirms
      external extraction can be removed without full internal
      revolution].
    Structural outcome: often improved. External extraction
      removed; local correction capacity restored or rebuilt
      from surviving infrastructure.

  INTERNAL REVOLUTION [existing government overthrown]:
    Destroys existing correction infrastructure including
    whatever functional correction capacity it contained.
    Must build replacement from scratch using the centralized
    methodology required to win -- hierarchical coordination,
    suppressed internal dissent, concentrated force.
    Structural problem: methodology required to win is
      incompatible with stated goal. Centralized methodology
      produces centralized outcome regardless of intent.
      Cannot transmit distributed correction goal through
      centralized revolutionary method. Conclusion transmitted
      without compatible methodology = Generational Reset.
    Cases: French Revolution → Napoleon [correction capacity
      centralized, extraction architecture restored with
      different surface]. Russian Revolution → Stalin [worse
      extraction than Tsarism by most structural measures].
      Chinese Revolution → Mao [tens of millions dead].
    Structural outcome: consistent reproduction of extraction
      architecture with different actors. Not coincidence --
      structural consequence of methodology mismatch.

  WHY THE DISTINCTION MATTERS:
    Colonial independence restores pre-existing correction
    capacity. Internal revolution destroys it and rebuilds
    using incompatible methodology. Same surface appearance
    [armed conflict against authority]; structurally different
    mechanisms and documented different outcomes.

  AMERICAN REVOLUTION [partial exception, explains why]:
    Had unusual features that partially broke the pattern:
    existing distributed governance infrastructure [colonial
    assemblies], legal/philosophical framework transmitted
    beforehand [Federalist Papers = methodology not just
    conclusions], federated structure built in from start.
    Closer to colonial independence than internal revolution
    structurally -- removing external extraction layer with
    pre-existing correction infrastructure.

  T3: case attribution contested. Structural pattern confirmed
    across multiple cases; individual attribution to revolution
    as cause vs other factors partially obscured.

CAPTURE-RESISTANT REGULATION [v16 -- analytical summary]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
EPISTEMIC NOTE: analyst prior [Mutual Ordoliberal Distributist]
favors distributed correction capacity and skepticism toward
discretionary authority. This entry applies existing framework
vocabulary to regulation design. No new mechanisms.

STRUCTURAL OBSERVATION:
  Hyper-regulation of legible technical activities [nuclear,
  medical devices, building codes] coexists with light regulation
  of diffuse financial and political extraction. Pattern explained
  by capture surface, not regulatory intent:
    Technical activities: clear actors, measurable outputs,
      concentrated opponents = easy to regulate AND easy to
      over-regulate with compliance theater.
    Financial extraction: diffuse beneficiaries, captured
      measurement surfaces, actors embedded in regulatory
      apparatus = structurally resistant to regulation
      regardless of intent.
  Framework read: C2 capture at regulatory level. The
  measurement surface for financial extraction is owned by
  the same actors being regulated.

REGULATION TYPES RESISTANT TO CAPTURE [structural analysis]:
  Outcome-based [not process-based]: measures what happens,
    not whether procedures were followed. Process compliance
    satisfies the form while defeating the purpose. Outcome
    measurement is harder to substitute.
  Bright-line rules [not discretionary judgment]: speed limits,
    blood alcohol levels, structural load ratings. Discretionary
    standards ["reasonable," "appropriate"] create negotiation
    surface where capture operates. Bright lines eliminate that
    surface.
  Self-enforcing via liability: harmed parties sue directly.
    No regulator required; harmed party has direct incentive.
    Tort law for product liability more capture-resistant than
    agency rulemaking because the regulator is a court
    responding to specific cases, not an agency accumulating
    relationships with regulated industries.
  Distributed enforcement: multiple independent actors
    [state AGs + federal + private right of action].
    Simultaneous capture of all enforcement pathways harder
    than single agency capture.
  Transparency requirements: mandatory disclosure not agency
    approval. Information routes to public not regulator.
    Reduces human-to-human negotiation surface where capture
    occurs.

COMMON THREAD: capture-resistant regulation reduces discretion
  from human-to-human negotiation and routes through mechanisms
  harder to compromise. Bright lines, outcomes, liability,
  transparency all reduce the surface area for capture.

ADVERSARIAL READING:
  Bright lines can be gamed at the boundary [Goodhart's Law --
  measure becomes target]. Self-enforcement via litigation
  advantages wealthy plaintiffs. Transparency without
  comprehension produces information overload not accountability.
  Each mechanism has a failure mode; the question is whether
  the failure mode is harder to exploit than agency capture.
  Evidence suggests yes for most contexts.

T3: empirical claims about which regulation types resist
  capture are contested. Framework structural analysis
  consistent; implementation evidence mixed.

SECTION SIX -- PERMANENT REFERENCE [weapon index + case library]
==========================================================================

MAINTENANCE LOGIC:
  MERGE: identical quality target + mechanism -> merge with note.
  REMOVE: weapon candidates with no primary proof case.
  COMPRESS: entries > 15 lines without unique proof -> trim to
    name/type/proof-for/weapons-active.
  KEEP: sole proof of quality/weapon/failure state.
  SPACE TRIGGERS: >400KB compress. >500KB retire secondaries.
    >300 cases -> dedup before adding.
  SESSION START: remove RESOLVED/CLOSED items, renumber from 1.

WEAPON COUNTER-MEASUREMENTS :
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every weapon targets a specific quality; restoring or protecting
  that quality counters it.
  Direct counters:
    MSC → independent measurement instrument not owned by attacker.
    Calibration Strip → multiple competing reference standards.
    Formation Capture → delayed axis formation; external exposure
      before lock-in.
    Exit Cost Construction → reduce dependency, build alternative
      substrate.
    Correction Theatre → require output verification not activity.
  Hard case: weapons targeting MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY first.
    Cannot verify counter-effectiveness using captured instrument.
    Counter requires external instrument not owned by attacker.
  Impossible case: Formation Capture at earliest developmental
    window. Counter capacity not yet formed. Counter must come
    from outside the affected system entirely.

WEAPON COST-ASYMMETRY TABLE [v16 -- strategic]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every weapon has three cost dimensions:
  DEPLOY: cost to deployer to activate and sustain the weapon.
  DAMAGE: quality degradation imposed on target per unit time.
  COUNTER: cost to target to neutralize or resist.
Scale: LOW / MED / HIGH / EXTREME. Ratio = DAMAGE / DEPLOY.
High ratio = efficient weapon [cheap to deploy, expensive to counter].
This table makes the weapon section strategic, not just taxonomic.

Ratings are structural judgments -- T3 applies to all entries.
Independent confirmation via psychology research listed in index.

HIGHEST RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE >> DEPLOY -- priority targets]:

  Idle Cycle Deprivation
    Deploy: LOW [side-effect of engagement optimization; no
      additional investment required beyond existing attention
      economy infrastructure]
    Damage: HIGH [SR deficit accumulates; self-reinforcing loop;
      C1 substrate degrades continuously without visible signal]
    Counter: HIGH [requires environmental redesign; individual
      CC depleted before it can fire against weapon; structural
      intervention only]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme asymmetry. Most efficient weapon
      in index by cost ratio. Deployer pays nothing additional;
      target pays full substrate cost.

  Formation Capture [AGC]
    Deploy: LOW-MED [requires access to formation window; once
      inside, axis installs via normal relational contact -- no
      special mechanism required beyond presence and repetition]
    Damage: EXTREME [axis capture is total -- all downstream
      qualities run on substituted axis; C2 and C3 transmit the
      capture forward; generational propagation at zero marginal
      cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [counter capacity not yet formed at window;
      external intervention only; window closes; T7 applies]
    Ratio: EXTREME/LOW = highest damage-to-deploy ratio in index.
      Window-dependent -- outside the window, weapon unavailable.

  Calibration Strip
    Deploy: LOW [requires only reference standard insertion at
      calibration point; single intervention propagates forward
      through all downstream measurements automatically]
    Damage: HIGH [all measurements inherit miscalibration; CAUSAL
      LEGIBILITY orphaned; C2 runs honestly on wrong baseline;
      produces confident false readings rather than detectable
      errors]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent external reference standard
      that deployer does not control; hard to obtain once strip
      is established as the consensus baseline]
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme. Deployer inserts once; target pays
      continuous recalibration cost.

  Engineered Causal Diffusion [ECD]
    Deploy: MED [requires structuring information environment to
      route all causal chains through complexity nodes; needs
      institutional access but not operational maintenance]
    Damage: HIGH [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY destroyed; correction cannot
      anchor; C1 continues generating without traceable feedback;
      Garbage Cycle without visible failure signal]
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent causal reconstruction from
      outside the diffusion architecture; expensive and easily
      disrupted]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Correction Theatre
    Deploy: LOW [substitute visible correction activity for actual
      correction; requires only institutional control of correction
      surface -- no real correction mechanism needed]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on
      Theatre; actual deviations accumulate uncorrected; C1
      degrades as corrections fail to land; population loses
      confidence in correction mechanisms]
    Counter: MED [require output verification not activity;
      detectable if external measurement surface available; hard
      if C2 already captured]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW = efficient.

MED RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE ~ DEPLOY]:

  Measurement Surface Control [MSC]
    Deploy: HIGH [requires capturing or constructing the primary
      measurement surface; significant investment in institutional
      access, credentialing, or platform control]
    Damage: EXTREME [once surface controlled, all readings route
      through captured instrument; no independent verification
      available from inside boundary]
    Counter: HIGH [independent external instrument; expensive
      to build and maintain against resource asymmetry]
    Ratio: EXTREME/HIGH = efficient despite high deploy cost.
      One-time investment; sustained damage with no ongoing cost.
    Note: highest absolute damage in index; cost ratio moderate
      only because deploy cost is also high.

  Exit Cost Construction [ECC]
    Deploy: MED [requires substrate dependency construction over
      time; housing, income, identity, community -- each takes
      investment but builds on itself]
    Damage: HIGH [exit = total substrate loss; GENUINE AXIS
      cannot fire independently; T1.4 low; correction suppressed
      by structural cost not direct capture]
    Counter: MED-HIGH [requires alternative substrate construction
      before exit; Malcolm X case: OAAU built from zero under
      active threat; counter is expensive and dangerous]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Proxy Construction
    Deploy: MED [requires manufacturing and maintaining the decoy
      target; needs media or institutional access to establish
      proxy as legitimate enemy]
    Damage: HIGH [CORRECTION CAPACITY budget consumed on proxy;
      actual extraction architecture protected; Frustrated
      Detection Configuration produced at population scale]
    Counter: MED [name the proxy and trace actual causal chain;
      easier than MSC counter but deployer will attack counter
      using same proxy machinery]
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = efficient.

  Rhythm Disruption
    Deploy: LOW-MED [precarity architecture; variable scheduling;
      requires employer or platform control but not expensive]
    Damage: MED-HIGH [CYCLE STABILITY destroyed; C1 cannot form
      reliable cycle; output erratic; stack with ICD for
      maximum C1 pressure]
    Counter: MED [stable scheduling; protected cycle windows;
      requires structural not individual intervention but
      achievable with collective action]
    Ratio: MED-HIGH/LOW-MED = efficient.

LOW RATIO WEAPONS [DEPLOY > DAMAGE or damage slow]:

  Sincere Delusion Framing
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained narrative maintenance and
      institutional credibility; target must believe deployer
      is sincere; expensive to sustain under scrutiny]
    Damage: MED [CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING degraded; axis genuine
      but ungrounded; produces effort without result rather than
      direct extraction]
    Counter: LOW-MED [ground axis in actual outputs; compare
      stated vs actual results; Track C sufficient]
    Ratio: MED/HIGH = inefficient. Weapon degrades under any
      external measurement.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Deploy: HIGH [requires sustained relational investment in
      bombing phase; cannot be abbreviated without losing effect]
    Damage: HIGH [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION targeted; relational
      substrate constructed then destroyed; dependency + grief
      cycle]
    Counter: LOW-MED [pattern recognition; name the cycle;
      structural rather than emotional response; hardest for
      C1 starved targets]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral. High cost both sides; deployer
      retains extracted substrate so net positive for deployer
      despite high cost.

  Isolation Construction
    Deploy: HIGH [requires controlling target's relational
      access; institutional or social leverage needed; ongoing
      maintenance cost as target seeks new connections]
    Damage: HIGH [T1.4 destroyed; GENERATIVE CYCLE loses
      substrate source; C1 starved trajectory]
    Counter: MED [alternative relational substrate; hardest
      when physical isolation engineered; easier when social
      isolation only]
    Ratio: HIGH/HIGH = neutral. Both sides pay full cost.

SPECIAL CATEGORY -- SELF-DEPLOYING [no deployer required]:

  Fertile Lock [C3 failure, not weapon -- but weapon-equivalent]
    Deploy: ZERO [emerges from C3 failure; no actor required;
      transmission selectivity failure produces lock automatically]
    Damage: HIGH [successors receive locked state; C1 locked
      trajectory; generational propagation at zero cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [C1 locked recovery has no primary confirmed
      case; requires cross-generational transmission method
      correction; hardest recovery type in framework]
    Note: not a weapon in strict sense [no deployer intent] but
      weapon-equivalent in effect. Spontaneous extraction.

  Idle Cycle Deprivation [already listed -- highest ratio weapon]
    Included here also: partially self-deploying. Once SR deficit
    produces anxiety and anxiety produces input-seeking, weapon
    sustains without external input.

STRATEGIC IMPLICATIONS:

  Cheapest effective stack [C1 maximum pressure, low cost]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
    All three LOW-MED deploy cost. Combined: C1 under maximum
    sustained pressure with SR unable to recover. Weapon stack
    already deployed by attention economy + precarity architecture
    without coordination.

  Hardest weapons to counter [external instrument required]:
    MSC, Calibration Strip, Formation Capture [early window].
    All require resources the target typically cannot access
    inside the captured boundary. Counter requires external
    actor with independent C2 surface and sufficient TENSION
    MAINTENANCE.

  Fastest damage weapons [immediate effect]:
    Amnesia Manufacture [C2 destroyed directly], ECD [causal
    chain severed immediately], Boundary Manipulation [population
    shifted before correction can fire].

  Most durable weapons [persist after deployment ends]:
    Formation Capture [axis installed; persists for lifetime
    without maintenance], Calibration Strip [reference standard
    adopted; persists until independently challenged], Fertile
    Lock [generational; persists until C3 correction].

  Counter-cost rule: weapons targeting C2 first produce the
    hardest counters because the instrument needed to verify
    counter-effectiveness is the same instrument the weapon
    captured. External instrument is both the counter and the
    precondition for knowing the counter worked.


WEAPON STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
A weapon stack is two or more weapons co-deployed on the same target
simultaneously. Stack detection matters because:
  1. Counter difficulty is not additive -- it is multiplicative.
     Each active weapon degrades the instrument needed to counter
     the others. MSC active = C2 degraded = cannot verify whether
     ECD counter worked. Stack compounds counter difficulty.
  2. Stack signature is different from single weapon signature.
     Single weapon produces characteristic failure on one quality.
     Stack produces interference pattern across multiple qualities
     simultaneously -- looks like generalized dysfunction rather
     than identifiable weapon deployment.
  3. Correct counter sequence matters. Countering a downstream
     weapon while upstream weapon is active wastes counter
     capacity. MSC + ECD: countering ECD while MSC is active
     = counter lands on captured surface. Counter MSC first.

DETECTION PROCEDURE [five steps]:

  STEP 1 -- QUALITY SCAN
    Map active quality degradation across the full stack.
    Which qualities are degraded? Which are intact?
    Multiple simultaneous quality degradations = stack candidate.
    Single quality degraded = single weapon candidate.
    Note: some single weapons degrade multiple qualities [MSC
    degrades MI + CL + RI simultaneously]. Distinguish single
    weapon multi-quality effect from genuine co-deployment.

  STEP 2 -- MECHANISM INDEPENDENCE TEST
    For each quality degraded, identify the mechanism producing
    the degradation. Are the mechanisms independent?
    Independent mechanisms [different deployers, different
    attack surfaces, different timing] = genuine stack.
    Single mechanism producing multiple quality effects = single
    weapon with broad effect. Do not over-count.
    Test: remove one weapon hypothetically. Do other quality
    degradations persist? Yes = independent. No = one weapon
    producing cascading effects.

  STEP 3 -- BENEFICIARY IDENTIFICATION
    Name who benefits from each weapon in the stack.
    Same beneficiary across all weapons = coordinated stack.
    Different beneficiaries = independent convergent deployment
    [structural stack -- no coordination required, same effect].
    Attention economy stack [ICD + Rhythm Disruption + IR]:
    structural stack -- multiple actors each deploying one weapon
    for independent reasons, combined effect maximally damaging.
    Structural stacks are harder to counter than coordinated
    stacks [no single actor to target; removing one weapon
    leaves others intact].

  STEP 4 -- SEQUENCING ANALYSIS
    Are weapons deployed in sequence [each enabling the next]
    or simultaneously?
    Sequential stack: earlier weapons reduce counter capacity
    for later weapons. Typical attack gradient: C3 first
    [cheapest], then C2 [now less defended], then C1 [now
    blind and undefended]. Each step increases probability
    of success of next step.
    Simultaneous stack: all weapons fired at once. Higher
    deploy cost but faster damage. Typically used when
    target has high correction capacity and sequential
    approach would be detected.
    Spontaneous stack: weapons not deployed by any actor --
    emerge from structural conditions simultaneously. ICD +
    precarity architecture + intermittent reinforcement:
    spontaneous C1 pressure stack.

  STEP 5 -- COUNTER SEQUENCE DERIVATION
    Work from outermost cycle inward. Counter C3 weapons last
    [they transmit what you build; counter them after C1/C2
    stable]. Counter C2 weapons before C1 weapons [cannot
    correctly aim C1 correction without legible surface].
    Counter C2 capture first [restore instrument], then
    use restored instrument to counter remaining weapons.
    Exception: if C1 is near-terminal, C1 substrate
    restoration takes priority over C2 sequence rule --
    no point restoring C2 if C1 collapses before C2
    can read anything.
    Stack counter budget: estimate total counter capacity
    available. If counter budget < stack counter cost,
    triage: which weapon is doing the most damage per unit
    time? Counter that first. Accept partial degradation
    on others while primary counter fires.

KNOWN CONFIRMED STACKS [library entries]:

  ATTENTION ECONOMY STACK [spontaneous, structural]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
    Target: C1 substrate. Beneficiary: diffuse [attention
    economy broadly]. No coordination required.
    Counter sequence: ICD counter first [protected idle
    cycles -- structural redesign]; then Rhythm Disruption
    [stable scheduling]; IR last [remove variable reward
    architecture -- hardest counter, requires platform
    redesign or exit].
    Counter difficulty: HIGH. All three weapons self-
    reinforcing. SR deficit [ICD] produces anxiety,
    anxiety drives input-seeking, input-seeking re-deploys
    ICD. Breaking the loop requires environmental redesign
    not individual correction.
    FULL SPONTANEOUS STACK COUNTER PROCEDURE: see below.

  MEASUREMENT COLLAPSE STACK [Enron -- coordinated]:
    MSC + ECD + Calibration Strip [SPE accounting] +
    Incremental Commitment Engineered.
    Target: C2 [all three C2 qualities simultaneously].
    Beneficiary: Skilling/Lay/Fastow [coordinated].
    Counter sequence: independent auditor [MSC counter]
    first -- Andersen was captured, SEC required. Once
    independent C2 instrument available, ECD and Calibration
    Strip become legible and counterable.
    Actual counter: Sherron Watkins [internal C2 signal]
    + SEC investigation [external C2 instrument]. Stack
    collapsed when external C2 instrument could not be
    prevented from reading surface.

  FORMATION STACK [NOI / Malcolm X case]:
    Formation Capture + ECC + Dependency Construction +
    Correction Monopoly Construction.
    Target: GA [axis] + T1.4 [substrate source] +
    CORRECTION CAPACITY [correction routing].
    All four deployed simultaneously at formation window.
    Counter: impossible from inside [T7]. External contact
    [Mecca pilgrimage = external C2 instrument showing
    axis was substituted]. Exit required counter to ECC
    first [OAAU as alternative substrate] before GA
    could fire independently.
    Counter sequence confirmed by case: ECC partial counter
    [OAAU building] then GA fires [post-Mecca]. Axis
    could not fire while ECC intact -- substrate dependency
    prevented independent axis operation.

  CUCKOO BIOLOGICAL STACK [spontaneous, evolutionary]:
    Formation Capture + ECC + Calibration Strip.
    Target: host parental investment cycle.
    Confirmed: all three weapons co-deployed by single
    mechanism [cuckoo chick hatching]. Natural confirmation
    that weapon stacks emerge under selection pressure
    without design or coordination.

STACK INTERACTION EFFECTS [general rules]:

  C2 capture + C1 pressure = invisible C1 degradation.
    C1 degrading, C2 cannot read it. Looks like normal
    variation. By the time C1 degradation is visible,
    it may be terminal. [Soviet case: decades of C2
    capture made C1 terminal state invisible until Glasnost.]

  Formation Capture + ECC = exit structurally unavailable.
    Axis cannot fire independently + substrate loss on exit.
    Both must be countered before genuine axis operation
    is possible. Counter either alone is insufficient.
    [Malcolm X: Mecca provided axis signal [FC partial
    counter] but OAAU required simultaneously to provide
    substrate [ECC partial counter]. Neither alone sufficient.]

  MSC + Correction Theatre = infinite correction budget
    consumption. Correction fires at Theatre activity,
    not actual deviation. C2 captured so Theatre cannot
    be distinguished from correction. Correction capacity
    fully consumed; no deviation ever resolved.
    [McNamara: correction firing continuously at body
    count metrics while war outcomes unchanged.]

  ICD + Formation Capture = axis cannot develop.
    Formation window requires idle cycles for axis
    gestation [GENUINE AXIS]. ICD during formation
    window prevents axis from forming before FC can
    install substitute. Spontaneous FC via substrate
    deprivation rather than deliberate substitution.
    [Attention economy during adolescent formation
    window: ICD + social comparison architecture
    = axis forms on externally supplied content
    rather than internal generation.]

ICD + FORMATION CAPTURE DEMOGRAPHIC PREDICTION [v16 -- formalized]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Prediction: populations with high ICD exposure during the adolescent
formation window [approximately ages 12-18, corresponding to prefrontal
development window] should show detectably higher rates of substituted
axis [GENUINE AXIS formed on externally supplied content rather than
internal generation] compared to populations without high ICD exposure
during the same window.

MECHANISM:
  GENUINE AXIS formation requires idle cycles for internal generation
  to run [IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT confirmed]. ICD eliminates the
  idle cycles in which internal generation would occur. Social
  comparison architecture [variable reward + status feedback]
  provides external content that fills the formation window before
  internal generation can fire. Axis forms on externally supplied
  content by default -- not deliberate FC deployment, but structural
  FC via substrate deprivation.
  Key: this is spontaneous Formation Capture. No deployer, no intent.
  Selection mechanism: attention economy rewards engagement with
  external comparison content; internal generation produces no
  platform-legible signal; algorithm preferentially surfaces
  comparison content during the window.

FALSIFICATION CRITERIA [three testable predictions]:

  PREDICTION 1 -- SELF-CONCEPT CLARITY GRADIENT:
    Adolescents with higher ICD exposure during formation window
    should show lower self-concept clarity [the degree to which
    self-beliefs are clearly defined, internally consistent, and
    temporally stable] than age-matched cohorts with lower ICD.
    Falsification: no self-concept clarity difference between
    high and low ICD cohorts controlling for other variables.
    Status: PARTIALLY CONFIRMED. Literature shows social comparison
    on social media correlates with higher identity distress and
    lower self-concept clarity [systematic review, 19,658
    adolescents]. "Authenticity on social media, not idealized
    self-presentation, correlated with higher self-concept
    clarity" [same review]. Framework read: idealized
    self-presentation = axis formed on external reward signal
    rather than internal generation. Authenticity = axis
    formed internally, expressed externally. Direction confirmed;
    causation not established.

  PREDICTION 2 -- AXIS INSTABILITY UNDER STRESS [S5 ANALOG]:
    Substituted axis formed on external content should show
    characteristic instability when external feedback is removed
    or contradicted [stress test equivalent]. Genuine axis:
    stable under pressure [S5 signal]. Substituted axis:
    destabilizes when external validation withdrawn.
    Observable proxy: platform removal / social media break
    should produce higher distress and identity disruption in
    high-ICD formation cohorts than low-ICD cohorts.
    Falsification: no difference in identity disruption on
    platform removal between high and low ICD formation cohorts.
    Status: INDIRECT EVIDENCE. Research documents teens judge
    worth by algorithmic reflections [embedding externally
    dictated self-image]. Identity incongruence offline [axis
    mismatch between platform identity and non-platform
    context] documented. Direct stress-test comparison not yet
    run in literature.

  PREDICTION 3 -- GENERATIONAL COHORT GRADIENT:
    Cohorts with formation windows occurring after widespread
    smartphone adoption [approximately 2012+, corresponding to
    Gen Z core formation years] should show higher rates of
    substituted axis markers than cohorts with formation windows
    before high ICD exposure.
    Observable markers: self-concept clarity scores, identity
    commitment rates, reported sense of self-direction, internal
    vs external locus of identity [existing validated scales].
    Falsification: no cohort gradient on these measures between
    pre-smartphone and post-smartphone formation cohorts.
    Status: PARTIALLY SUPPORTED [v16 literature check].
      Mechanism confirmed: upward social comparison reduces
      identity commitment, confuses exploration, increases
      uncertainty [Springer Current Psychology 2024]. SCC
      correlates negatively with social comparison on social
      media [multiple studies]. Parent-child relationship
      more stable predictor than peer relationship [Chinese
      longitudinal study, 3010 adolescents].
      Gap: no direct cohort comparison [pre vs post smartphone
      formation window] using matched demographics and same
      instrument. Mechanism confirmed; gradient not yet tested
      as direct comparison. P3 remains OPEN pending cohort study.

LITERATURE CONFIRMATION [mechanism convergence]:
  Multiple independent research streams describing same mechanism
  without framework vocabulary:
    "Externally dictated self-image" [Counseling Perspective 2025]
    = axis formed on externally supplied content.
    "Internally incongruent" identity [multiple sources]
    = substituted axis, not genuine axis.
    "Identity distress" from social comparison
    = correction attempting to fire against substituted axis.
    "Authenticity correlates with self-concept clarity"
    = genuine axis produces stable self-concept; substituted
    axis produces clarity deficit.
  Classification: CONFIRMATION candidate [mechanism convergence
  from independent research without framework vocabulary].
  Full confirmation requires Prediction 3 cohort test.

INTERVENTION IMPLICATION:
  Cannot counter via awareness alone [individual CC depleted by
  ICD before it can fire against FC]. Structural counter required:
  protected formation window [ICD counter during formation years]
  + genuine friction contact on matching axis [C1 starved recovery
  finding applied at formation stage]. Same spontaneous stack
  counter procedure [Stage 1-2] applies here.
  The finding reframes "social media and mental health" literature:
  the problem is not exposure per se but ICD during the specific
  formation window, producing structural GA substitution with
  downstream identity instability as the measurable signal.

SPONTANEOUS STACK COUNTER-SEQUENCE [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Spontaneous stacks have no deployer, diffuse beneficiaries, and
self-reinforcing loops. Standard counter logic [target the deployer,
remove the weapon] does not apply. Three structural differences
require separate procedure:

  DIFFERENCE 1: No deployer to target.
    Coordinated stack: counter the actor deploying the weapon.
    Spontaneous stack: weapon is produced by structural conditions
    [market incentives, precarity architecture, platform design].
    Countering one actor leaves the structural condition intact;
    another actor fills the same position. Counter must target
    the condition, not the actor.

  DIFFERENCE 2: Self-reinforcing loop depletes CC before it fires.
    ICD depletes SR. Depleted SR produces anxiety. Anxiety drives
    input-seeking. Input-seeking re-deploys ICD. Individual
    CORRECTION CAPACITY fires into the loop and is consumed.
    Counter requirement: break the loop before CC can operate --
    not by deploying CC harder, but by removing the loop's
    self-reinforcing mechanism first.

  DIFFERENCE 3: Distributed deployment = distributed counter cost.
    Coordinated stack: single counter point [external C2 instrument
    against MSC, for example] can collapse the stack.
    Spontaneous stack: counter cost distributed across every point
    where the structural condition fires. Attention economy stack
    fires at every device, every platform, every unstructured
    moment. Counter must match deployment surface.

SPONTANEOUS STACK COUNTER PROCEDURE [four stages]:

  STAGE 1 -- SUBSTRATE TRIAGE
    Before countering any weapon: assess C1 substrate state.
    If SR deficit is severe [chronic exhaustion, zero idle cycles,
    sustained depletion], no counter will hold until substrate
    crosses minimum threshold.
    Action: forced substrate restoration first. Protected idle
    cycles not optional -- structural prerequisite for any counter
    to function. Without SR above floor, CC cannot fire against
    anything in the stack.
    Duration: minimum idle cycle window required before reassessing.
    Individual: days to weeks of protected idle cycles.
    Institutional: recovery period with no production demands.
    This stage is the hardest to implement because spontaneous
    stack produces urgency as a byproduct [SR deficit + anxiety
    = feeling of needing to do more, not less]. The feeling is
    the weapon. Act opposite to the feeling.

  STAGE 2 -- ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN [not individual correction]
    Individual CC cannot counter a structural stack because:
      - CC is depleted by the stack before it can fire
      - Individual correction fires once; structural condition
        re-deploys the weapon immediately
    Counter must be environmental: change the conditions that
    produce the weapon, not the target's response to the weapon.
    ICD counter: structural gap creation [phone in another room,
    scheduled disconnection, architectural separation of device
    and living space]. Not willpower -- physical environment.
    Rhythm Disruption counter: fixed schedule with hard
    boundaries. Not discipline -- structural commitment
    [external accountability, physical location change,
    scheduled constraints that don't require active decision].
    IR counter: exit variable reward architecture entirely
    or substitute fixed-ratio reward [same behavior, predictable
    outcome removes the hook]. Partial substitution insufficient
    -- IR loop re-engages on any variable reward surface.
    Key principle: counter must be lower-energy than the weapon.
    If counter requires more CC than the weapon depletes,
    counter will fail as soon as depletion resumes.

  STAGE 3 -- SEQUENCE WITHIN THE STACK
    Counter ICD first [highest ratio weapon, self-reinforcing
    loop driver]. Once idle cycles protected, SR begins
    restoring. Restored SR enables CC to operate against
    remaining weapons.
    Counter Rhythm Disruption second [stable cycle enables
    consistent CC deployment; without stable rhythm, counter
    efforts are episodic and gaps allow redeployment].
    Counter IR last [requires intact CC to resist variable
    reward pull; attempting IR counter with depleted CC
    produces failure and reinforces the hook via near-miss
    mechanism]. IR counter only after ICD and Rhythm
    Disruption are structurally addressed.
    Do not attempt all three simultaneously: stack counter
    budget is limited by available CC. Serial not parallel.

  STAGE 4 -- STRUCTURAL LOCK-IN [prevent redeployment]
    Spontaneous stacks redeploy automatically when structural
    conditions are present. Counter gains must be locked into
    the environment before CC depletes again.
    Mechanism: use recovered CC window to change environment
    permanently, not to resist the weapon repeatedly.
    ICD lock-in: physical device separation as default state
    [requires active effort to reconnect, not to disconnect].
    Rhythm lock-in: external commitments that enforce schedule
    without requiring active decision [booked appointments,
    structural location changes, other people depending on
    the schedule].
    IR lock-in: platform exit or account deletion [partial
    distance is insufficient; IR hook reactivates on any
    re-engagement]. Exit must be structural.

INDIVIDUAL VS STRUCTURAL COUNTER DISTINCTION [key rule]:
  Individual counter [willpower, motivation, awareness]:
    Fires once. Depleted by stack immediately. Spontaneous
    stack wins through attrition -- it redeploys indefinitely,
    individual CC does not. Individual counters fail not
    because of personal weakness but because the asymmetry
    is structural. Deployer cost = zero. Counter cost = full
    CC expenditure every instance.
  Structural counter [environment, architecture, commitment]:
    Fires continuously at zero ongoing CC cost once installed.
    Changes the conditions rather than resisting the outcome.
    Asymmetry reversed: weapon must redeploy against structural
    barrier; counter holds without active CC.
  Rule: spontaneous stack counters must be structural to hold.
    Individual awareness is the precondition [must know what
    the stack is doing to design the environmental counter].
    Individual willpower is not the mechanism.

CONFIRMED CASE [Wittgenstein isolation period]:
  Wittgenstein's rural Austria period was structurally counter
  to the attention economy stack [no devices, no variable
  reward architecture, predictable daily rhythm, enforced
  idle cycles via village life]. Track C shows: zero
  intellectual production. Stage 1 [substrate triage] correct
  -- substrate depleted; isolation period was forced SR
  restoration. Stage 2-4 structurally present but wrong
  axis contact [no logical/systemic friction].
  Confirms: structural counter [Stage 1-2] necessary but
  not sufficient. Correct contact axis required in addition
  [C1 starved recovery finding]. Environmental redesign
  restores substrate; correct contact fires recovery.

[CASE:GIFT_INDEX]
GIFT INDEX [v15]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Positive-transfer mechanisms. Inverse of weapons. Increase qualities
in recipient without depleting them in source. Requires genuine SURPLUS
  DISTRIBUTION
surplus in giver -- redistributed extracted surplus does not qualify.

FORMAL DEFINITION:
  A gift gives coherence. It increases quality capacity in the recipient
  while leaving source qualities intact or increased. Source not
  depleted; recipient more coherent after transfer than before.

  A weapon takes coherence away. It reduces quality capacity in the target
  while the deploying system extracts or maintains at the target's
  expense. Target less coherent after contact than before.

  Distinguished by direction of quality-effect: gifts flow outward from
  surplus; weapons extract inward from target. Same mechanism can
  be either depending on direction and intent.

  Neuroscience basis: prosociality hardwired in mammals [oxytocin dual
  pathway -- Neuroscience News 2025]; cooperation emerges as evolutionary
  emergent property from recognition + memory alone [2026]. Gift
  architecture is biological baseline, not cultural construction.
  Weapon deployment requires overriding baseline -- additional mechanism
  always required to suppress default cooperation.

STRUCTURAL CONDITIONS:
  - Source must have genuine surplus [SD > cost of
    transfer]
  - Recipient quality increases without corresponding source depletion
  - Transfer not contingent on recipient dependency [else Dependency
    Construction disguised as gift]
  - Correctable by recipient [else Fertile Lock disguised as gift]

FAILURE MODES [gift degrading into weapon]:
  - Surplus absent -> redistribution weapon
  - Dependency created -> GC [GC] weapon [Dependency Construction]
  - Correction blocked -> CAG [CAG] weapon [Fertile Lock]
  - Axis transmitted not method -> CAG weapon [Formation
    Capture]

PRIMARY PROOFS:
  Bell Labs basic research output [1925-1984] [Science type]: surplus
    transferred to external field without extraction from recipient.
    Transistor, information theory, Unix -- all released outward.
    Source generative capacity intact through transmission window.
    Full stack against new qualities: PACKAGE SUFFICIENCY
    maximum -- correctable method transmitted, not conclusion only.
    OUTPUT INTEGRATION maximum -- recipients [electronics,
    computing] could use what was distributed. CYCLE STABILITY
    high -- predictable 60-year window. CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION
    distributed internally [not monopolized]. CAUSAL MEMORY
    high -- correction history preserved across decades. Strongest
    gift case against new qualities: SP+SUBSTRATE
      REGENERATION
    +CAG+SIGNAL READING+CAUSAL MEMORY
    +CYCLE STABILITY+CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION+CAUSAL MEMORY.
  CERN / WWW protocol [Berners-Lee 1991] [Science type]: explicitly
    released without patent. Source not depleted. Recipient field GENERATIVE
      CYCLE
    massively increased. Correctable [open standard, forkable]. No
    extraction mechanism attached. Cleanest single-transfer gift case
    in modern history.
  Linux kernel [1991-present] [Relational type]: source [Torvalds +
    contributors] not depleted -- codebase grows with contribution.
    Recipient GC increases. Correctable [forks exist]. No
      dependency
    created [exit free]. Sustained distributed gift confirmed across
    30+ year window.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK [v15]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Required for gift architecture to function:
  SP [SP] [surplus] + TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [correctable
    method]
  + SA [SA] [successor autonomy]
  + CAG [CAG] [distribution] + CAUSAL MEMORY [lands usefully].
  All five required.

  Weapon minimum [inverse]: capture MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY + break CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY
  + manipulate BI.
  Three qualities broken vs five required.

  Asymmetry: extraction breaks three; gift requires five working
  together. Extraction structurally easier. Also structurally terminal.
  Gift architectures outlast extraction by orders of magnitude.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------
SECONDARY PROOFS:
  Great Pyramid of Giza [~2560 BCE] [Science/Engineering type]:
    stepped trapezoidal design distributes seismic energy outward
    and downward. Internal chambers as energy-dissipating voids.
    Limestone blocks of varying density = natural dampening layers.
    Still standing after 4,500 years and multiple major earthquakes.
    CAG transmission of correctable engineering method
      across
    generations without modern instrumentation. CAG at
    maximum -- output confirms axis. Pre-modern SP operating
      at
    civilizational scale [LiveScience 2026].
  Quaker network [17th-19th C] [Intrinsic Loose type]: abolitionism,

  Euler [Leonhard, 1707-1783] [Math subtype]: strongest individual

  Talagrand conjecture proof [2026] [Math subtype]: three mathematicians
    proved Talagrand's 1995 convexity conjecture -- that convexity can
    be generated in high-dimensional spaces via fixed Minkowski sums.
    Reformulated as probability problem: any 1-subgaussian random vector
    in n dimensions expressible as sum of three standard Gaussian random
    vectors. Talagrand [Abel Prize 2024] called it "the most extraordinary
    result of my entire life" -- did not believe his own conjecture true
    until proof appeared. Open access [arXiv]. No capture mechanism.
    CAG [Causal Axis Grounding] case at mathematical

    asserted hidden order existed in high-dimensional randomness without

      maximum.

      [refused food].
    PACKAGE SUFFICIENCY high -- method transmitted, not
    conclusion only. CAUSAL MEMORY intact and retrievable.
    Sacrificial mechanism precisely: SR→zero,
    SD→terminal,
    CAG+SIGNAL READING intact to end.
    Proves CAG does not require SR
    to function in terminal window.
