[HDR:GENERATIVE_CYCLE_WEAPONS]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CASE:GENERATIVE_CYCLE_WEAPONS]
GC WEAPONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Dependency Construction
    Psychology parallel: attachment theory [Bowlby] -- borrowed cycle
    at developmental scale. Anxious attachment = cycle cannot run
    without ongoing external input. Cult dependency [Lifton, Singer] --
    exit cost construction documented in exit interviews. Coercive
    control research [Johnson] confirms structural identical mechanism
    in intimate partner violence.

  Exit Cost Construction
    Psychology parallel: sunk cost fallacy [Arkes/Blumer 1985] --
    prior investment increases exit cost independently of current
    value. Entrapment research [Brockner] confirms escalating
    commitment despite negative signals. Cult exit research [Singer]
    documents deliberate exit cost engineering.
    LLM comparison [Psychology Today 2026]: humans hardwired to
    overestimate threats and underestimate resilience. Exit costs
    feel higher than they are by default -- weapon exploits this
    directly. Negativity bias amplifies constructed exit cost.

  Intermittent Reinforcement
    Psychology parallel: Skinner variable-ratio schedule [strongest
    resistance to extinction of any schedule]. Addiction neuroscience
    [Volkow] -- dopamine spike pattern identical under variable reward.
    Trauma bonding research [Herman] confirms correction capacity
    collapse under unpredictable reward/punishment.
    Note: primary mechanism is cycle timing control [CYCLE STABILITY
    Stability] not cycle destruction [GC]. Placed here because
      GC
    is the downstream effect; CYCLE STABILITY is the mechanism. Candidate
      for
    CYCLE STABILITY WEAPONS when that section is built.

  Isolation Construction
    Psychology parallel: sensory deprivation research [Hebb 1954] --
    cycle degradation under input starvation confirmed. Solitary
    confinement literature [Haney] documents structural cycle collapse.
    Coercive control research [Stark] identifies isolation as primary
    mechanism.
    Inverted parallel [Concordia Station 2026]: forced proximity
    without exit capacity produces same CORRECTION CAPACITY collapse from
      opposite
    direction. Too little contact [isolation] and too much forced
    contact [confinement] both destroy boundary integrity. SURPLUS
      PRODUCTION
    misdirects from task correction to interpersonal conflict when
    boundary capacity removed. Both ends of the axis are weapons.

  Scarcity Manufacture
    Psychology parallel: scarcity mindset research [Mullainathan/Shafir
    2013] -- manufactured resource scarcity tunnels cognitive bandwidth,
    reducing correction capacity as structural byproduct. Poverty trap
    literature confirms extraction-as-evidence-of-failure pattern.

SP WEAPONS [Surplus Production -- consumption obscured or
  normalized]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Baseline Shift
    Psychology parallel: adaptation level theory [Helson] -- reference
    point shifts toward prevailing conditions. Hedonic treadmill
    [Brickman/Campbell] -- surplus reads as neutral after normalization.
    Overton Window research confirms political baseline drift mechanism.

  Churn Normalization
    Psychology parallel: burnout research [Maslach] -- high turnover
    normalized as industry standard delays substrate consumption
    recognition. Organizational psychology confirms churn
    normalization as SP [SP] concealment mechanism.

  Coherence Rent
    Psychology parallel: reputation halo effect [Thorndike] -- past
    genuine performance extracted as current credibility without current
    production. Brand equity research confirms extraction of inherited
    coherence without production contribution.

  Desire Manufacture
    Psychology parallel: Bernays propaganda research [1928] -- need
    creation precedes measurement. Behavioral economics [Thaler/
    Sunstein] documents preference construction before stated
    preference measurement. Advertising effectiveness literature
    confirms desire manufacture at population scale.

  Stakeholder Diffusion
    Psychology parallel: diffusion of responsibility [Darley/Latané
    bystander effect] -- larger group = lower individual response.
    Organizational accountability research confirms accountability
    diffusion as structural SP concealment.

  Vision Laundering
    Psychology parallel: motivated reasoning research [Kunda 1990] --
    genuine transformative language adopted by extraction operations
    exploits confirmation bias. Corporate greenwashing literature
    confirms mechanism at institutional scale.

[HDR:CORRECTION_CAPACITY_WEAPONS]

CC WEAPONS [Correction Capacity -- correction absent,

  Altruism Exploitation
    Psychology parallel: empathy-altruism hypothesis [Batson] --
    genuine care creates compliance lever. Caregiver burnout research
    confirms correction capacity collapse under sustained altruism
    exploitation. Codependency literature [Beattie] documents
    structural mechanism.

  Attention Capture
    Psychology parallel: attentional resource theory [Kahneman] --
    finite correction capacity redirected by low-value signal.
    Social media engagement research [Twenge] confirms population-
    scale correction capacity depletion. Tristan Harris/Center for
    Humane Technology documents deliberate deployment.

  Competence Undermining
    Psychology parallel: learned helplessness [Seligman 1967] --
    correction fires, target discards output. Gaslighting research
    [Sweet] documents deliberate competence undermining in intimate
    relationships. Imposter syndrome literature confirms self-
    undermining variant.

  Correction Theatre
    Psychology parallel: organizational change research [Kotter] --
    visible first steps without structural change is documented
    failure pattern. Performative allyship research confirms
    cross-transmission variant [successors inherit performance
    without correction capacity].
    Neuroscience parallel: effort/uncertainty research [Neuroscience
    News 2026] -- humans avoid uncertain effort cost over known
    exertion. MI [MI] weapons [causal illegibility]
    suppress SURPLUS PRODUCTION not by
    making correction hard but by making correction cost unpredictable.
    Engineered uncertainty is more suppressive than engineered
    difficulty. Also supports Friction Deficit -- frictionless
    environments eliminate known-cost exertion loops that train
    correction behavior.
    Cross-ref: Hossenfelder [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY Measurement Integrity
      weapons] --
    AI as uncaptured measurement surface where institutional
    peer-review surface failed.
    Correct placement is MI not SP.

  Credible Commitment Trap
    Psychology parallel: commitment and consistency [Cialdini] --
    irreversible prior commitments increase compliance. Escalation
    of commitment research [Staw] confirms correction option
    elimination through prior investment.

  Exhaustion Engineering
    Psychology parallel: burnout research [Maslach] -- depletion
    through use without destruction of mechanism. Decision fatigue
    [Baumeister] confirms correction capacity consumption as
    structural byproduct of sustained demand. Legal attrition
    research confirms deliberate deployment.

  Love Bombing then Withdrawal
    Psychology parallel: intermittent reinforcement [Skinner] +
    trauma bonding [Herman]. Cult recruitment research [Singer]
    documents deliberate sequence. Narcissistic relationship
    literature confirms structural mechanism.

  Philosophical Disarmament
    Psychology parallel: inoculation theory [McGuire 1961] -- genuine
    insight can lower resistance before consumption engages. Highly
    intelligent targets show greater susceptibility to sophisticated
    manipulation [Stanovich -- dysrationalia]. Scales with target
    intelligence confirmed.

  Sinfulness Framing
    Psychology parallel: shame research [Brown, Tangney] -- natural
    correction signals converted to evidence of personal moral failure.
    Religious trauma research [Winell] documents structural mechanism.
    Shame vs guilt distinction [Tangney] confirms correction capacity
    collapse under shame framing.

  Social Proof Fabrication
    Psychology parallel: Asch conformity experiments [1951] --
    manufactured consensus bypasses individual correction capacity.
    Spiral of silence [Noelle-Neumann] confirms population-scale
    mechanism. Astroturfing research confirms deliberate deployment.

  Thought Stopping
    Psychology parallel: thought suppression research [Wegner -- ironic
    process theory] -- suppression at specific trigger points
    paradoxically strengthens suppressed content. Religious
    indoctrination research [Winell] documents deliberate deployment
    at highest-value examination points.

  Intermittent Reinforcement [Engineered]

    precariat research].


  Long Lock-In [v16 -- weapon variant]
    Any long-duration agreement with no adjustment mechanism
    eliminates CORRECTION CAPACITY structurally. Extraction does
    not require intent -- only that correction cannot fire.
    Mechanism: terms set at signing reflect conditions at signing.
    No adjustment mechanism for technological change, productivity
    divergence, currency manipulation, labor standard arbitrage.
    Each year without adjustment = compounding imbalance. Surplus
    concentrates; costs diffuse to Boundary B populations with
    no correction pathway.
    Distinct from direct ECC: ECC raises exit costs for individuals.
    Long Lock-In eliminates correction capacity at system level --
    affects all parties in Boundary B simultaneously regardless
    of individual exit capacity.
    Co-deployment: typically paired with Calibration Strip
    [aggregate metrics masking distributional effects] and
    Correction Theatre [adjustment programs funded below
    displacement cost].
    ISDS variant [investor-state dispute settlement]: inverts
    correction capacity -- government correction on behalf of
    displaced population becomes legal liability. Correction
    mechanism not just absent but structurally reversed.
    Primary proof: US free trade agreements [NAFTA/PNTR] --
    3-5M manufacturing jobs displaced, community collapse,
    health outcome divergence, opioid crisis correlation.
    Incalculable downstream cost confirmed structurally.
    Deploy cost: LOW [single political event locks in
    indefinitely]. Damage: HIGH [compounds over time].
    Counter: renegotiation mechanism built into agreement
    from signing; sunset clauses; adjustment fund funded
    at actual displacement cost not symbolic level.
    T3: attribution contested. Mechanism confirmed; magnitude
    partially obscured by multiple contributing factors.
    Cross-ref: [CASE:US_FREE_TRADE_AGREEMENTS]

  Correction Inversion [v16 -- promoted from WIP 14]
    Architecture where the act of correcting produces punishment
    for the correcting system. Correction capacity present;
    structurally penalized for firing.
    Distinct from existing weapons:
      ECC: raises individual exit costs.
      Correction Monopoly: monopolizes who can correct.
      Correction Theatre: performs correction without landing.
      Correction Inversion: correction fires, punishment fires
        back. Mechanism works; using it is the liability.
    Mechanism: legal or contractual architecture routes
      consequences of correction back to correcting actor.
      Government corrects extraction on behalf of Boundary B
      = government owes damages to extraction beneficiary.
    Broader than ISDS: defamation against accurate reporting;
      regulatory capture producing liability for enforcement;
      whistleblower retaliation regardless of accuracy;
      anti-SLAPP failures punishing correction with litigation.
    Primary proof [ISDS cases]:
      Vattenfall v. Germany [2012]: nuclear phase-out
        correction triggered Energy Charter Treaty liability.
      Philip Morris v. Uruguay [2010]: cigarette health
        warnings triggered investment treaty arbitration.
      Lone Pine v. Canada [2013]: fracking moratorium
        triggered NAFTA Chapter 11 liability.
      Pattern: legitimate regulatory authority punished
        via treaty mechanism in all three cases.
    Primary proof [anti-SLAPP failures -- v16]:
      Wausau Pilot & Review [Wisconsin]: won defamation
        case, spent ~$200K in legal fees, had to halt
        hiring. Won and still punished. Correction fired
        correctly; punishment fired regardless.
      Murray Energy v. HBO [2017]: resource-asymmetric
        SLAPP. Goal was chilling effect not legal victory.
        Textbook Correction Inversion via litigation cost.
      Population-scale confirmation: 59% of journalists
        sued for defamation altered coverage not because
        reporting was wrong but due to litigation cost
        [Reporters Committee 2022]. Chilling effect
        operating at scale, not just individual cases.
      26 states have no anti-SLAPP law = Correction
        Inversion structurally embedded in legal
        architecture across half of US jurisdictions.
    Psychology parallel: learned helplessness [Seligman].
      Systems punished for correct behavior stop attempting
      correction. Chilling effect is primary ongoing mechanism
      after initial cases establish precedent -- inversion
      does not need to fire continuously to suppress CC.
    Deploy cost: LOW [single treaty clause at signing].
    Damage: HIGH + self-reinforcing [each successful inversion
      increases chilling effect across all observers].
    Counter: exclude inversion clauses at signing. Retrospective:
      domestic legislation asserting regulatory sovereignty
      over treaty obligations.
    T3: mechanism confirmed; chilling effect magnitude estimated.
    Cross-ref: [CASE:US_FREE_TRADE_AGREEMENTS]
  Idle Cycle Deprivation [v16]    Elimination of unstructured recovery intervals to prevent
    SUBSTRATE REGENERATION from firing. Distinct from Rhythm
    Disruption [breaks cycle timing] and Intermittent Reinforcement
    [captures cycle payoff] -- ICD targets the gap between cycles,
    not the cycle itself.
    Mechanism: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default
    mode network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
    fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input -- walks with
    audio, meals with screens, notification architecture -- extracts
    the recovery window before it opens. Exhaustion is SR deficit
    not overwork. Cycle runs fine; substrate it runs on degrades
    because recovery window never opens.
    Structural properties:
      Blame-diffuse: no single actor required. Structural pressure
      + cultural norm + product design + social expectation produce
      identical effect without coordination.
      Self-reinforcing: SR deficit produces anxiety; anxiety drives
      input-seeking behavior that perpetuates SR deficit.
      Invisible: target experiences exhaustion, not the mechanism.
      Standard narrative [overwork, stress, personal failure]
      suppresses accurate diagnosis.
      Scalable: applies identically at individual [attention gap
      elimination], institutional [no recovery between correction
      cycles], and civilizational [extraction past SR rate] scales.
    Failure signature contrast: Rhythm Disruption produces erratic
    output. ICD produces declining substrate with apparently normal
    output until collapse. Different attack surface, different
    presentation -- ICD harder to detect.
    Weapon stack: ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent
    Reinforcement = C1 under maximum sustained pressure with
    substrate unable to regenerate. All three deployed
    simultaneously by attention economy architecture.
    Psychology parallel: default mode network research [Buckner/
    Andrews-Hanna] -- consolidation requires task-negative state.
    Attention restoration theory [Kaplan] -- directed attention
    depletes; idle/undirected restores. Both confirm idle cycles
    as substrate requirement not optional enhancement.
    Attention economy confirmation [Harris -- Time Well Spent;
    Zuboff -- surveillance capitalism]: deliberate gap elimination
    as engagement metric. Revenue model requires continuous
    attention; idle cycle = lost revenue = designed out.
    Structural not conspiratorial -- market incentive selects for
    gap elimination without coordination.
    Primary proof: smartphone notification architecture [gap
    elimination by design -- ~150 phone checks/day documented].
    Confirmed [Harris; platform internal documents; attention
    research].
    Secondary proof: open-plan office architecture [ambient
    interruption preventing consolidation] -- productivity research
    [Mark/UCI] confirms task-switching cost and consolidation
    failure without idle cycles.
    Counter: protected idle cycles. Environmental redesign not
    willpower -- individual CC is depleted by weapon before it
    can fire against it. Counter requires structural not individual
    intervention.
    Weapon cost to deployer: near zero. Side-effect weapon --
    produced by systems optimizing for engagement, not deployed as
    primary weapon. Diffuse beneficiary makes it harder to target:
    no single actor with clear interest in continuation.
    T3 note: deliberate vs structural deployment indistinguishable
    from output. Weapon designation holds regardless of intent.
    Cross-ref: IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT [core theory]; Rhythm
    Disruption [co-deployed]; precarity architecture [Standing].


Every weapon in the index has independent psychological research
confirmation. The framework was built from fictional cases; the
psychology was built from real populations using different methods.
Independent convergence across two evidence types confirms structural
patterns are real not fictional artifacts.

Key researchers whose work independently confirms framework mechanisms:
  Milgram [authority, compliance], Bandura [moral disengagement],
  Cialdini [influence], Kahneman/Tversky [cognitive bias], Lifton
  [thought reform], Herman [trauma], Maslach [burnout], Seligman
  [learned helplessness], Tajfel/Turner [social identity], Loftus
  [memory], Haidt [moral psychology], Bowlby [attachment],
  Mullainathan/Shafir [scarcity], Singer [cult psychology].

No weapon in the index lacks psychological confirmation.
The objection "fiction proves nothing" fails because fictional cases
were used to calibrate the instrument, not to prove the mechanisms.
The mechanisms are confirmed independently.

informative. Not different enough = not added.

[CASE:PHOSPHOPHYLLITE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
Phosphophyllite / Phos [Land of the Lustrous -- Haruko Ichikawa, manga
  2012-2023]
  Type: Primary
  Arc: GENUINE AXIS miscalibrated at formation -> quality accumulation
    without foundation -> TENSION DISSOLUTION at civilizational scale ->
    resolution in pre-axial contact. 108 chapters / Buddhist structure.

  GENUINE AXIS FAILURE AS ENGINE:
    Phos's axis = "being useful / being seen as useful." Externally
    calibrated from formation. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING failure: axis
    sincere but anchored in others' perception not actual outputs.
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY appears present [Phos examines, questions,
    rebels] but all examination is in service of the false anchor.
    The axis is internally active but externally grounded -- examined
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY built on wrong foundation. Everything cascades
    from this: power gains are real, axis stays false, suffering
    compounds with each gain.

  SEVEN TREASURES AS INCOMPLETE TOWER CONSTRUCTION [primary finding]:
    Phos receives qualities out of order and without foundation:
    Gold arms [C1 stability / GENERATIVE CYCLE grounding]
    Agate legs [C1 rhythm / CYCLE STABILITY]
    Pearl eye [compassion -- foundation layer]
    Lapis head [C2 CAUSAL LEGIBILITY / wisdom]
    Received without: Pearl/compassion [foundation], Coral/vitality
    [SUBSTRATE REGENERATION], Silver/purity [REFERENCE INTEGRITY].
    Framework equivalent: upper-level qualities arriving without
    the foundation layer required to hold them.
    Gold arms don't stabilize -- they overwhelm, consume, bind.
    Lapis head sharpens perception but isolates. High CAUSAL
    LEGIBILITY without TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that
    amplifies suffering rather than resolves it.
    Treasures present but unbalanced. Given to someone not yet
    ready to receive them. Each gain produces compounding harm
    rather than compounding benefit because GENUINE AXIS was
    never properly grounded.

  CINEABAR AS CONTRAST CASE:
    Cineabar in isolation = TENSION MAINTENANCE holding without
    external validation. Stays at night patrol because it is
    genuinely the only useful thing available without harming others.
    Not happiness -- stable misery vs Phos's escalating pursuit.
    Resolution [Cineabar with other gems, doing okay] = what happens
    when someone stops trying to fix themselves into belonging.
    Phos's trajectory [god, alone] = what happens when usefulness
    becomes the axis itself and is pursued to its logical conclusion.
    Cineabar needed connection not a new job. Phos never understood
    this because their metric [usefulness] couldn't read it.

  ROCK AT END AS RESOLUTION [L0.5 teaching L4]:
    The unnamed rock is pre-axial -- GENERATIVE CYCLE present,
    GENUINE AXIS absent, no SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY -- and is more
    at peace than a god. "I'm satisfied. I'm good as I am."
    Framework read: pre-axial contentment is not a goal state --
    it's proof that GENUINE AXIS must be internally generated not
    earned through accumulation. The rock doesn't have a better
    axis; it has no axis and therefore no axis failure. The lesson
    is what Phos needed to find in C1 before ascending: axis
    grounded in existing, not in being seen to exist.

  YUMA / PRINCE AS LONG-HORIZON CORRECTION:
    300-year plan using suffering, loss, and change to push Phos
    toward awakening. Correction operating at timescale invisible
    to the subject. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY of the plan absent to Phos
    throughout. Framework parallel: correction can be real and
    occurring while the corrected system reads it as pure harm.
    T2.1 [Causal Memory] failure = cannot retrieve the pattern
    across sufficient timescale to read the correction.

  BUDDHIST STRUCTURE AS FRAMEWORK PARALLEL:
    Three marks of existence map to tower qualities:
    Anatta [non-self / no permanent soul] = GENUINE AXIS must be
      examined not assumed -- permanent axis = Fertile Lock.
    Anicca [impermanence] = SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY requires accepting
      that transmitted content will and should change.
    Dukkha [dissatisfaction / suffering] = result of clinging to
      false axis under impermanence. TENSION DISSOLUTION through
      attachment rather than TENSION MAINTENANCE.

  Proof for: Qualities arriving without foundation producing
    compounding harm not compounding benefit [seven treasures];
    GENUINE AXIS externally calibrated = CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
    failure even when examination is present; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    increase without TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that isolates;
    pre-axial contentment as proof that axis must be internally
    generated not earned; correction operating at timescale
    invisible to subject [T2.1 failure at civilizational scale].
  Source: Land of the Lustrous [Haruko Ichikawa, Kodansha,
    2012-2023]; video essay transcript [May 2026].
  Cross-ref: [REF:PHOSPHOPHYLLITE]

[CASE:ARTEMIDORUS_ONEIROCRITICA]
[PRIMARY_Q:SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION]
Artemidorus / Oneirocritica [real -- ~2nd century CE]
  Type: Primary
  Arc: 1,800-year multi-hop transmission. Greek → Byzantine →
    Islamic → Renaissance. Method transmitted; cosmological
    assumptions largely dropped at each hop.

  T1.2 [OUTWARD LANDING] AS PRIMARY:
    Text exited origin context AND produced capacity in recipients
    at each transmission event. Not just preserved -- actively
    used. Byzantine physicians, Islamic dream interpreters,
    Renaissance scholars all built on the classification system.
    Survival = T1.2 operating repeatedly across 1,800 years.
    Texts that exited without landing [cosmologically incompatible,
    no institutional receiver, no practical utility] didn't survive.
    Three landing conditions confirmed:
    1. Practical utility -- dream interpretation commercially and
       socially useful across cultures. Physicians, rulers, ordinary
       people wanted it. Useful things find patrons.
    2. Format compatibility -- five organized books, clear
       classification system. Method portable across cosmological
       frameworks. Greek gods stripped out; interpretive categories
       still functional under Christian and Islamic assumptions.
    3. Institutional landing points -- Byzantine libraries, Islamic
       scholarship [actively collected Greek technical texts],
       Renaissance recovery. Each hop had an institutional receiver
       with preservation interest.

  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [correctable inheritance]:
    Method [systematic classification of dream types] transmitted
    intact. Cosmological assumptions [Greek pantheon, fate
    architecture] mostly dropped. Clean correctable inheritance --
    successors revised conclusions [what dreams mean] while
    preserving method [how to classify and interpret]. Fertile
    Lock did not occur because the method was separable from the
    cosmology.

  Proof for: T1.2 [Outward Landing] as survival mechanism across
    multi-hop transmission; format compatibility as transmission
    enabler; practical utility as patron-finding mechanism;
    method/cosmology separability enabling correctable inheritance.
  Source: Artemidorus, Oneirocritica [~150 CE]; Greek Reporter
    [May 2026].
  Cross-ref: [REF:ARTEMIDORUS_ONEIROCRITICA]

[CASE:LOOK_OUTSIDE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
Look Outside [Francis Coulombe -- RPG Maker horror]
  Type: Primary
  Arc: Accumulation without correction; terminal single-resolution event.
  Structural note: no active CORRECTION CAPACITY mid-game by design.
    Understanding of outside accumulates progressively with warning
    signals. Ritual at end = sole correction event. Eight endings =
    ritual output varies with accumulated state and player choices.

  CORE MECHANIC [CAUSAL LEGIBILITY as damage type]:
    Outside understanding is the damage. Not exposure [standard horror]
    -- understanding. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY of outside reality progressively
    replacing player's reference standard. Warning system makes chain
    visible -- player can trace own deterioration -- but CORRECTION
    CAPACITY is absent mid-game. Detection without correction = Frustrated
    Detection Configuration at personal scale.

  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY BOUNDARY AS PLAY SURFACE:
    Apartment building = sealed MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY zone.
    Every door, window, conversation = potential boundary event.
    Neighbors as monsters = INPUT FILTERING failure made spatial --
    former reciprocal relationships now signal-ambiguous.
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY is the tactical layer; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    accumulation is the strategic condition.

  TENSION MAINTENANCE AS CENTER POSITION:
    Partial understanding sometimes required for survival and ritual.
    Full understanding [view true form] = worst outcome. Zero
    understanding = default bad ending [world destroyed, Sam passive].
    Playable center = controlled accumulation with deliberate restraint
    at terminal moment. Game pressures both poles simultaneously.

  RITUAL [terminal CORRECTION CAPACITY -- eight outcome structure]:
    Single correction event at game end. What was accumulated AND
    how it was processed determines output. Not volume alone.

    ENDING TAXONOMY [framework read]:
    1. Default [wait it out, don't interfere]:
       Low accumulation, no ritual engagement. World destroyed, 2/3
       humanity lost. CORRECTION CAPACITY deferred and never fired.
       Passive accumulation without processing = worst baseline.

    2. Wrong offerings [incorrect ritual]:
       Accumulation without calibration. Ritual fires but inputs
       corrupt. Sam becomes "living fractal." CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
       of ritual requirements absent -- Garbage Cycle at ritual scale.

    3. Partial offerings [incomplete ritual]:
       Partial calibration. Sam transforms on rooftop -- exposed
       without correct processing. TENSION MAINTENANCE failed at
       terminal moment. Extra hands. Cannot stop.

    4. Partial + guinea pig [fail to defeat abomination]:
       Sam assimilated into Zin Ammon [guinea pig god]. Screaming
       eternally inside its hide. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY dissolved --
       axis consumed by larger system.

    5. Partial + guinea pig [defeat abomination]:
       Victory is false -- defeating part accelerated the whole.
       Zin Ammon consumes Earth then moon. Sam assimilated.
       CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure: intervention aimed at symptom,
       accelerated actual cause [T3.3 Correction Theatre at
       cosmic scale].

    6. All offerings + flee exalted four:
       Ritual complete but Sam abandons L3.5 -- exalted four
       become tyrants, 90% humanity lost. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
       without SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY -- correct method delivered,
       Sam does not stay to complete the exchange.

    7. All offerings + defeat exalted four + decline true form [GOOD]:
       Full ritual, correct offerings, L3.5 exchange completed,
       RESTRAINT at terminal moment [declines to view true form].
       Sam transforms but retains axis. Becomes protector of Earth.
       TENSION MAINTENANCE at maximum -- accepts change, holds
       humanity. Only ending where transformation is generative.
       Framework read: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY sufficient for function,
       not maximum. The good ending requires NOT maximizing
       understanding at the critical moment.

    8. All offerings + defeat exalted four + view true form [WORST]:
       Maximum understanding. Brain repurposed to comprehend visitor.
       Sam becomes mindless consuming entity. Humanity eliminated.
       Framework read: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY at maximum = GENUINE AXIS
       collapse. Understanding past the TENSION MAINTENANCE threshold
       destroys the axis that makes understanding meaningful.

  CORE THEORETICAL FINDING [revised from initial read]:
    The game proves CAUSAL LEGIBILITY has an optimal range, not
    a monotonic value. More understanding is not always better.
    Sufficient understanding + restraint [ending 7] > maximum
    understanding [ending 8]. The good ending requires deliberately
    stopping short of full CAUSAL LEGIBILITY at the critical moment.
    This is TENSION MAINTENANCE applied to epistemic accumulation --
    hold between knowing enough and knowing too much.
    T3.2 [Axis Reality]: ending 7 is the only case where the
    accumulated understanding remains grounded in Sam's axis.
    Endings 5/8 show axis consumed by the thing being understood.

  THREE-POLE FAILURE STRUCTURE [Jasper case]:
    Jasper viewed the visitor directly and retained partial axis --
    confirms threshold is not uniform; axis strength prior to exposure
    and accumulated understanding quality affect survivability.
    Wrong-ritual Jasper = suppressed transformation rather than
    processed. Suppression pressurizes accumulation internally.
    Jasper boss fight = suppressed transformation releasing at
    maximum force, worse than unsuppressed exposure.
    Three poles of TENSION DISSOLUTION:
    [1] Collapse toward full understanding [ending 8] -- axis dissolved
        by maximum CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
    [2] Suppression without processing [wrong-ritual Jasper] -- axis
        held by force, accumulation compounds, catastrophic release.
    [3] Passive non-engagement [ending 1] -- understanding accumulates
        without direction; world destroyed by default.
    Viable center: active processing with deliberate restraint at
    terminal moment. Containment is not processing. Suppression
    is not TENSION MAINTENANCE -- it is deferred TENSION DISSOLUTION.
    Bloodborne parallel [confirmed]: Hunters who contained the beast
    rather than processing it. Beast grows stronger in containment,
    releases at maximum when containment fails. Same three-pole
    structure: dissolution [Full Beast], suppression [Laurence],
    processing [Ending 7 equivalent = rare].
    Cross-ref: [REF:BLOODBORNE] for suppression parallel.

  PROOF CONTRIBUTIONS:
    Primary: Terminal single-correction event as structural variant.
    Primary: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY optimal range [not monotonic] --
      sufficient + restrained > maximum. Epistemic gain past
      TENSION MAINTENANCE threshold = axis destruction.
    Primary: TENSION MAINTENANCE applied to epistemic accumulation
      as the operative survival variable.
    Primary: Suppression ≠ TENSION MAINTENANCE -- deferred
      TENSION DISSOLUTION; accumulation compounds under containment
      and releases catastrophically. Three-pole failure structure
      confirmed [dissolution / suppression / passive].
    Secondary: Threshold non-uniformity [Jasper viewed visitor,
      retained partial axis] -- individual axis strength and
      understanding quality affect survivability, not just quantity.
    Secondary: Frustrated Detection Configuration [warning present,
      correction absent mid-game].
    Secondary: T3.3 Correction Theatre at cosmic scale [ending 5].
    Secondary: Pre-axial entity [visitor] producing RECOGNITION
      without COMPATIBILITY = Extraction Interface by nature.

  Source: Look Outside [Francis Coulombe / FrankieSmileShow,
    Steam/GOG 2025]; story explanation video transcript [May 2026].
  Cross-ref: [REF:LOOK_OUTSIDE]

[CASE:DOROHEDORO_SMOKE_AXIS]

    GENUINE AXIS physically visible and testable -- smoke doesn't
    lie about what you are the way reasoning can.
    Caiman's snake-head [a separate entity inside his mouth that
    can assess sorcerers] = external GA verification instrument
    operating below the sorcerer's conscious access.
  ILLUSTRATION value: teaches GA as expressed-not-introspected.
    The framework's GA verification protocol [process trace +
    behavioral consistency under cost] maps onto how smoke
    functions diegetically.

[CASE:DOROHEDORO_CROSSES]

Devil contracts / crosses [Dorohedoro]
  Type: Primary
  Proof for: Axis substitution marker as physical artifact;
    Formation Capture via contracted power; downstream behavior
    shift toward contract logic.
  Sorcerers who contract with devils gain cross-shaped scars and
    enhanced power. The cross marks the substitution point --
    from this moment the sorcerer's axis has a second organizing
    principle [the contract's terms] competing with the original.
    Over time behavior shifts toward contract logic. The mark is
    permanent and visible: axis substitution made externally
    legible.
  Framework read: crosses = Formation Capture at post-formation
    stage. Not installed at developmental window but accepted
    deliberately under desperation or ambition. Different
    mechanism [consented substitution] from standard FC but
    same downstream effect: axis split, behavior drifts toward
    the substituted organizing principle.
  Distinct from standard FC: the target knows the substitution
    is occurring. Consented axis partial-replacement. Framework
    question: does consent change the downstream effect?
    Evidence from cases: no. Axis drift toward contract logic
    occurs regardless of initial awareness.
  Cross-ref: [REF:DOROHEDORO_CROSSES], [REF:DOROHEDORO_EN],
    [REF:DOROHEDORO_SMOKE_AXIS]

    This is axis-governed restraint -- the quality available,
    deliberately held back for structural reasons.
    S5 = 1: acts against own interest and power when axis
    requires it. Sustained across the full narrative arc.

    fiction is trauma-based [captured axis] or incompetence
    [atrophied]. Nikaido's suppression is reasoned and costly --
    she pays real prices rather than deploy it carelessly.

Vatican II / Counter-Reformation correction cycle
  [real-world -- 1563 to 1965]
  Type: Primary [GAP 1 RESOLVED -- C1 LOCKED RECOVERY confirmed;
    lock formed, persisted 400 years, broken from inside]
  Proof for: C1 locked recovery [institutional scale]; correction
    of the correction methodology itself [L4 partial]; La Saulchoir

  C1 LOCKED STATE [Council of Trent 1563 -- Counter-Reformation]:
    Trent transmitted conclusion [Thomistic Neo-scholasticism as
    the correction of Protestant deviation] not methodology.
    "Constitutions of the Order required all Dominicans to teach
    the doctrine of St. Thomas both in philosophy and theology"
    [continuous from 1274]. Correction methodology locked into
    defensive posture against Reformation: all theology routed
    through scholastic disputation, historical method excluded,
    engagement with modernity treated as threat.
    Lock confirmed by: 1907 anti-Modernist oath [institutional
    enforcement of locked state]; La Saulchoir censure 1942
    [attempted partial correction suppressed].

  PARTIAL CORRECTION ATTEMPT [La Saulchoir 1937-1942]:
    Chenu proposed Thomas ≠ Thomism [historical method applied
    to Aquinas himself]. Congar introduced ecumenical theology.
    Both represented correction of correction methodology --
    examining how the Church was transmitting, not just what.
    Rome suppressed both: Chenu's work placed on Index 1942;
    both barred from teaching. External suppression of internal
    correction attempt = C1 locked confirmation.
    Mechanism: correction fired, lock's enforcement activated,
    correction suppressed. Classic C1 locked pattern.

  RECOVERY MECHANISM [Vatican II 1962-1965]:
    John XXIII rehabilitated Chenu, Congar, de Lubac as council
    experts. Congar directly inspired ten of sixteen conciliar
    texts. Bishops rejected Counter-Reformation draft text at
    first session. "People began to speak of the end of the
    Counter-Reformation." Chenu's historical method absorbed
    into Gaudium et Spes ["signs of the times" = historicized
    theology]. Congar's "autocritique" framed as essential to
    reform -- explicit meta-correction [correction methodology
    itself corrected].
    Recovery mechanism: external C2 instrument [John XXIII as
    new correction authority] rehabilitated suppressed internal
    correction capacity. Lock broke not through internal force
    but through authority-level correction of the enforcement
    mechanism itself. The correction of the correction machinery
    was itself corrected -- L4 partial at civilizational scale.

  WHY THIS IS C1 LOCKED RECOVERY [not prevention]:
    Distinguished from Cistercian [prevented lock at transmission]:
      Counter-Reformation lock formed fully, persisted 400 years,
      produced active enforcement [anti-Modernist oath, censures].
      Vatican II broke an established lock from inside the
      institution -- not at transmission, but at authority level
      after lock was structurally complete.
    Distinguished from Protestant Reformation [parallel correction
    attempt, no lock break]: Protestant reformers broke from
    institution. Vatican II corrected from within the locked
    structure. Internal correction of established lock.

  RECOVERY TRAJECTORY:
    M1: +1 [toward coherence at conciliar moment].
    M2: FAST [four sessions 1962-65, decisive shifts].
    M3: EXTERNALLY DRIVEN [John XXIII's authority = external
      input enabling correction that internal structure had
      suppressed]. Post-Vatican II trajectory controversial --
      some C3 transmission of Vatican II methodology failed
      [similar Protestant Reformation pattern emerging in
      post-conciliar disputes]. Recovery partial; lock
      reformation risk ongoing.
    Note: recovery confirmed at moment of council; long-run
    L4 PROPAGATION of Vatican II correction methodology
    uncertain. C1 locked recovery confirmed; L4 PROPAGATION
    of recovery TBD.

  GAP 1 STATUS: RESOLVED. Primary case confirmed.
    C1 locked recovery [institutional, lock already formed]
    is structurally possible via authority-level correction
    of enforcement mechanism. Requires: external correction
    authority + suppressed internal correction capacity that
    survived the lock + specific enabling moment [death of

[CASE:US_FREE_TRADE_AGREEMENTS]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
US free trade agreements [NAFTA/PNTR/WTO accession -- real-world,
  1994-2001]
  Type: Primary [LONG LOCK-IN extraction confirmed; incalculable
    downstream cost to Boundary B populations]
  Proof for: Long lock-in as correction capacity elimination;
    surplus concentration with cost diffusion; no adjustment
    mechanism = guaranteed extraction compound over time.

  BOUNDARY STRUCTURE:
    Boundary A: corporations, consumers [diffuse], export sectors,
      financial capital beneficiaries.
    Boundary B: domestic manufacturing communities, displaced
      workers, communities dependent on industrial employment.
    Meta-boundary: US political economy including both.
    External B: Mexican agricultural workers displaced by
      subsidized US corn [NAFTA]; Chinese workers absorbed
      into export manufacturing [PNTR].

  LOCK-IN MECHANISM:
    Agreements designed with minimal renegotiation capacity.
    NAFTA Chapter 11 [investor-state dispute settlement]:
      corporations can sue governments for regulatory changes
      reducing expected profit. Correction capacity for Boundary
      B explicitly subordinated to investor protection. The
      correction mechanism was not just absent -- it was
      structurally inverted. Government correction on behalf
      of displaced workers = legal liability under the agreement.
    WTO accession [China PNTR 2001]: permanent normal trade
      relations removed the annual review mechanism that had
      been the only correction lever. One-time political
      decision locked in indefinitely.

  COST ACCOUNTING [Track C]:
    Direct: 3-5 million US manufacturing jobs displaced by
      China trade exposure alone [Autor, Dorn, Hanson --
      independent economic research]. NAFTA manufacturing
      displacement separately documented.
    Downstream [not directly attributable but structurally
      linked]:
        Community collapse in manufacturing-dependent regions.
        Opioid crisis geographic correlation with trade-
          displaced communities [documented independently].
        Wage suppression across non-college workforce
          via competition arbitrage.
        Generational income loss in affected communities
          [children of displaced workers].
        Health outcomes divergence [life expectancy decline
          in specific demographic/geographic groups].
    Incalculable: family structure effects, civic
      participation decline, mental health, identity loss
      from work-community destruction. Real, unquantifiable,
      structurally caused.

  WHY LONG LOCK-IN = EXTRACTION BY STRUCTURE:
    Terms set at signing reflected conditions at signing.
    No adjustment mechanism for: technological change,
      productivity divergence, currency manipulation,
      labor standard divergence, regulatory arbitrage.
    Each year without adjustment = compounding imbalance.
    The agreement didn't need to be designed to extract --
      removing correction capacity guaranteed extraction
      would emerge as conditions changed. Structural not
      intentional [though intent existed in places].
    Beneficiary concentration accelerated over time:
      surplus from productivity gains and arbitrage
      routed to capital [no mechanism to redistribute
      to displaced labor].

  WEAPONS ACTIVE:
    Calibration Strip [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]: GDP and
      aggregate consumer surplus as reference standard.
      Masks distributional effects -- GDP rose, specific
      populations collapsed. Correct aggregate metric,
      wrong calibration for cost distribution.
    Correction Theatre [CORRECTION CAPACITY]: Trade
      Adjustment Assistance [TAA] program = theatre.
      Funded at fraction of displacement cost, bureaucratic
      barriers, historically shown to not restore income
      levels. Activity visible; correction near zero.
    ECC + Correction Monopoly Construction: communities
      with single dominant employer [factory town] had
      exit costs so high that when employer left, no
      correction pathway available. Structural dependency
      eliminated correction capacity before displacement
      occurred.
    Proxy Construction: trade competition routed toward
      domestic political scapegoating [immigrants, domestic
      policy] rather than agreement architecture. Correction
      budget consumed by proxy; agreement structure protected.

  CENTRALIZATION SCORE: 1/4.
    Condition 1 [correction pathway against central node]:
      Chapter 11 inverted -- regulatory correction is
      actionable liability. ISDS mechanism = correction
      pathway against government, not against extraction.
    Conditions 2-4: absent.

  LONG LOCK-IN AS WEAPON VARIANT [confirmed]:
    This case confirms: any long-duration agreement with
    no adjustment mechanism eliminates correction capacity
    structurally. Extraction doesn't require intent;
    it requires only that correction cannot fire.
    The longer the lock and the more conditions diverge
    from signing conditions, the more certain the
    extraction compound.
    Not coincidence that the most locked agreements
    [ISDS-protected, WTO-permanent] produced the most
    concentrated downstream costs.

  T3: economic attribution contested. Autor/Dorn/Hanson
    findings robust but not universally accepted. Track C
    [community collapse, health outcomes] consistent with
    causal link; direct causal chain partially obscured
    by multiple contributing factors. Reading: HIGH
    confidence on mechanism; MODERATE on magnitude.
  Cross-ref: [REF:US_FREE_TRADE_AGREEMENTS],
    [REF:MONT_PELERIN], [REF:SOMATIC_FORCING]

  Type: Primary [DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE -- new case type confirmed]
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [C2 restoration revealing hidden
    terminal C1]; C1 blind False Coherence masking terminal substrate
    state; C2 restoration producing collapse not recovery.
  WIP item 11 resolution: Reading [B] confirmed. Reading [A]
    [falsification of C1 blind stabilization] does not apply --
    the system was not in C1 blind recovery. It was in terminal
    C1 with False Coherence surface. C2 restoration made the
    terminal state legible; collapse was the correct outcome.

  C1 STATE PRE-GLASNOST [substrate audit]:
    Consistent growth rate decline every half-decade 1965-1985.
    Per capita output declining for fifteen years before Glasnost
    [Khanin data; CIA estimates]. GDP growth 4.7% [1980-85]
    declining to 2.9% [1986-90] despite reform investment.
    Oil revenue collapsed 1986 [$120/barrel to $24/barrel] --
    primary external substrate source destroyed simultaneously
    with C2 opening. Budget deficit rose from 2-3% to 6% GNP
    in 1986 alone; 10%+ by 1988.
    Framework read: C1 was not recoverable in 1986. Structural
    substrate depletion trajectory 15+ years. Oil dependency
    = T1.4 single-source substrate. Oil price collapse = T1.4
    failure coinciding with C2 opening. C1 already terminal;
    False Coherence surface was the only thing making it
    legible as functioning.

  C2 STATE [Glasnost mechanism]:
    Glasnost opened information surface [C2 partial restoration].
    Chernobyl [April 1986] immediate test -- partial C2 opening
    failed [party suppressed severity initially]. Information
    surface progressively opened 1987-1989.
    Effect: accumulated deviations [economic, political, ethnic]
    previously hidden by MSC became simultaneously legible.
    Correction burst fired -- not toward recovery, toward
    accurate reading of terminal C1 state. Satellite states
    exited. Internal reform movements activated. Institutional
    correction attempted against backlog of 15+ years of
    hidden deviations.
    C1 could not absorb the correction load -- already terminal.

  DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE MECHANISM [confirmed]:
    Phase 1: False Coherence [C1 terminal, C2 captured].
      System appears functional. Deviations accumulate hidden.
    Phase 2: C2 partial restoration [Glasnost].
      Hidden deviations become legible simultaneously.
      Correction burst fires against full backlog.
    Phase 3: Correction burst reveals terminal C1.
      System cannot correct what it can now see.
      Collapse = accurate diagnosis landing on non-recoverable
      substrate. Not recovery failure -- correct outcome.
    Distinguishing from C1 blind recovery failure:
      C1 blind recovery: C1 recoverable, correction burst
        expected to produce stabilization.
      Diagnostic Collapse: C1 terminal, correction burst
        produces legible collapse not stabilization.
      Diagnostic test: was C1 recoverable before C2 opened?
        Soviet case: no. 15-year decline trajectory confirmed
        independently before Glasnost. Reading [B].

  MOMENTUM [M-score at collapse]:
    M1: -1 [degrading]. M2: FAST [accelerating after 1988].
    M3: SELF-REINFORCING [C2 restoration revealing more
      deviations, each correction attempt consuming remaining
      C1 substrate faster]. M4: C2 restoration + terminal C1
      interaction = Diagnostic Collapse momentum type.

  NEW CASE TYPE CONFIRMED: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE
    Definition: C2 restoration in a system where C1 is already
    terminal produces collapse rather than recovery. The collapse
    is the correct outcome -- accurate diagnosis of a terminal
    state that the False Coherence surface had concealed.
    Distinct from:
      C1 blind recovery failure [C1 was recoverable].
      Simple collapse [no C2 restoration -- just C1 failing].
      Coherence Mimicry [no genuine C2 restoration -- surface
        capture replaced with new surface capture].
    Diagnostic Collapse requires: genuine C2 partial restoration
    + pre-existing terminal C1 + collapse following C2 opening.
    Intervention implication: C2 restoration in a system with
    unknown C1 state requires C1 substrate audit before opening.
    Opening C2 on terminal C1 is irreversible -- once the
    deviations become legible, the correction burst fires
    regardless of whether C1 can absorb it.

[CASE:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Fear & Hunger [Miro Haverinen -- RPG horror, 2018]
  Type: Primary
  Arc: Theological Correction Monopoly via genuine sacrifice captured
    into control architecture. Substrate atrophy from accumulated
    harm without correction outlet.

  ALLL-MER [sacrifice → weapon conversion]:
    Alll-mer's sacrifice was genuine -- real cost, real SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION at theological scale. Then captured: the sacrifice
    was owned and weaponized into a control mechanism for humanity.
    This is distinct from all existing Correction Monopoly cases:
    Word Bearers [false axis from start], Imperium [accumulated
    capture over time]. F&H shows genuine sacrifice → weapon
    conversion as explicit single event. The authenticity of the
    original act is what makes the capture effective -- captured
    genuine surplus is more powerful than fabricated surplus because
    it carries real CAUSAL LEGIBILITY. Believers can trace the
    chain to a real event; the chain just ends at a monopolist.
    Proof: genuine SURPLUS PRODUCTION can be posthumously captured
    and converted to extraction architecture. Original actor's
    intent irrelevant once ownership of the act is established.

  DUNGEON AS PASSIVE SUBSTRATE ATROPHY:
    The dungeon is not a directed weapon [contrast Room 101 --
    directed harm, specific actor]. Accumulated suffering with no
    correction outlet over centuries. Reality breakdown is passive
    consequence of CORRECTION CAPACITY absent at scale for extended
    time. No actor needed -- just removal of correction capacity
    and time. New structural variant: environmental coherence
    collapse without deliberate weapon deployment.
    SUBSTRATE REGENERATION at zero; GENERATIVE CYCLE below threshold;
    no correction firing. Not actively attacked -- just left without
    correction until the substrate cannot hold.

  GOD STRUCTURE [Correction Monopoly at theological scale]:
    Old gods enslaved by new gods. New gods maintain correction
    monopoly over humanity via Alll-mer's captured sacrifice.
    Correction fires only toward monopolist benefit. Characters
    who enter dungeon seeking power are seeking to break or
    inherit the monopoly -- not to restore distributed correction.
    Most endings reproduce the structure they sought to escape.

  Proof for: Genuine sacrifice → weapon conversion [SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION captured posthumously]; passive substrate atrophy
    without directed actor [CORRECTION CAPACITY absent + time];
    theological Correction Monopoly via authentic act capture.
  Weapons active [deployed by god structure]:
    Correction Monopoly [CORRECTION CAPACITY -- theological scale]
    Calibration Strip [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY -- Alll-mer's sacrifice
      as only legitimate reference standard]
    Exit Cost Construction [GENERATIVE CYCLE -- dungeon as total
      substrate dependency]
  Cross-ref: [REF:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]

[CASE:FEAR_AND_HUNGER_TERMINA]
[PRIMARY_Q:TENSION MAINTENANCE]
Fear & Hunger: Termina [Miro Haverinen -- RPG horror, 2023]
  Type: Primary
  Arc: TENSION MAINTENANCE as quantified survival mechanic; COMPATIBILITY
    at axis-dissolution threshold; fear as CORRECTION CAPACITY removal.

  MIND STAT AS TENSION MAINTENANCE MEASURE [unique in library]:
    Mind stat degrades as understanding of Termina accumulates.
    Zero Mind = character breaks. Degradation visible, threshold
    confirmed, recovery possible with specific items.
    Unique because TENSION MAINTENANCE is made numerically legible
    in-game -- not inferred from behavior but measured directly.
    Framework prediction confirmed: TENSION MAINTENANCE has a
    quantifiable threshold below which the system cannot hold.
    Parallel: Look Outside [warning system as TENSION MAINTENANCE
    degradation signal] but Termina is more granular -- incremental
    degradation with partial recovery possible.

  MARRIAGE MECHANIC [COMPATIBILITY as ritual precondition, not guaranteed]:
    Marriage in Termina is not exclusively human-god merger. Any
    biological entities [person-person, person-animal, forced
    pairings] can enter the ritual. Three distinct outcome types:

    Successful merger [god contact]:
      Axis dissolution sufficient to fit god's structure. COMPATIBILITY
      and SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY trade off directly -- more dissolution =
      more COMPATIBILITY, less remaining axis. Best endings: partial
      dissolution, axis held. Worst: full dissolution, no recovery.

    Abomination [ritual without COMPATIBILITY]:
      Ritual executes on mismatched inputs. Not ritual failure [Look
      Outside wrong offerings = ritual fails to fire]. Here ritual
      fires on inputs that cannot structurally complete the merge.
      Forced marriage: coercion removes SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY consent
      -- ritual completes, axis was never freely engaged, output is
      abomination. Neither merged nor intact.
      Person-animal marriage: axial system merging with pre-axial
      [GENERATIVE CYCLE present, GENUINE AXIS absent]. Ritual bridges
      coherence gap; result holds neither original structure.
      Core finding: COMPATIBILITY is a precondition, not produced
      by the ritual. Ritual can execute without COMPATIBILITY and
      abomination is the consistent output. Distinct from Look Outside
      [ritual fails without correct inputs] -- here ritual succeeds
      in executing, fails in output because COMPATIBILITY was absent.

    Forced marriage as weapon:
      Coercion converts the ritual into an extraction mechanism.
      Victim's axis partially dissolved without consent; abomination
      produced; neither party recovers intact. CORRECTION CAPACITY
      absent in the forced party [paralyzed by coercion or Rher].
      Ritual-as-weapon requires: ritual structure [available] +
      CORRECTION CAPACITY suppression in target [Rher provides] +
      willing initiator. All three present in Termina.

  RHER [fear as CORRECTION CAPACITY removal]:
    Rher, god of fear, suppresses correction by making the cost
    of firing correction too high to bear. Distinct from Correction
    Monopoly [someone else owns firing] -- Rher doesn't own it,
    removes the capacity to use it by overwhelming the system with
    fear response. CORRECTION CAPACITY present but unfireable.
    New weapon type: Paralytic Correction Suppression -- fear
    as the mechanism, not capture or redirection.
    Moonless sky [Rher ascending] = population-scale paralytic
    suppression. Correction capacity distributed but frozen.

  TERMINA FESTIVAL [false coherence surface]:
    Surface: folk festival, community gathering, celebration.
    Actual: ritual sacrifice structure, god ascension mechanism.
    MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture at cultural scale -- the festival
    surface is honest to participants who don't know its function.
    Not deliberate deception by most participants; structural
    deception built into the form itself.

  Proof for: TENSION MAINTENANCE as quantifiable threshold [Mind
    stat]; COMPATIBILITY as ritual precondition not ritual output
    [abomination = ritual-without-COMPATIBILITY executing]; forced
    marriage as weapon [CORRECTION CAPACITY suppression + ritual
    structure + willing initiator]; COMPATIBILITY / SUCCESSOR
    AUTONOMY direct tradeoff at maximum contact; fear as Paralytic
    Correction Suppression [new weapon type]; false coherence
    surface built into cultural form.
  Weapons active [Termina / Rher]:
    Paralytic Correction Suppression [CORRECTION CAPACITY -- Rher/fear]
    Calibration Strip [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY -- festival surface]
    Formation Capture [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY -- marriage dissolution]
  Cross-ref: [REF:FEAR_AND_HUNGER_TERMINA]

      correction verification. Structure changed; not just

      prevent the specific failure pattern Cluny exhibited.

      local autonomy + visitation] transmitted as explicit method,
      not conclusion. Hundreds of monasteries founded on this
      structure. The meta-correction mechanism -- distributed
      correction with systematic oversight -- survived transmission.

    not conclusion. Distinct from Cistercian [organizational
    architecture] -- Jesuits transmit individual meta-correction
    procedure as daily institutional practice.

  L4 AUDIT:
    REFLEXIVITY [confirmed]: Ignatian Examen = structured daily
      procedure for examining one's own correction patterns.
      Five steps: gratitude, request for light, review of day,
      ask pardon, look toward tomorrow. Obligatory twice daily
      in Constitutions. Function: feedback from own correction
      outputs back to correction architecture at individual level.
      Ignatius considered it "the single most important spiritual
      exercise." Not object-level correction [examining sins for
      confession] -- meta-level correction [examining how one
      is correcting]. Reflexivity formalized as daily practice.
    INTEGRATION [confirmed]: Examen structurally absorbed into
      Jesuit formation and Constitutions. Not optional or
      described -- mandatory and scheduled. "Even when so busy
      that life encroaches on routine, one ought never to let
      go of some prayer of examen. Let go of examen, and you
      can lose touch with how your busyness serves God, and
      can get manipulated by unexamined emotions." [Tetlow/
      Aschenbrenner] Integration load-bearing: without Examen,
      Jesuit correction mechanism degrades to reactive not
      reflective.
    PROPAGATION [confirmed -- strongest in library]:
      Examen transmitted as procedure not conclusion across
      480 years and 16,000+ Jesuits currently active. Key
      evidence:
        1. Procedure adapted by successor generations without
           losing core mechanism. 1970s revision [conscience
           -> consciousness framing by Aschenbrenner] is
           itself a documented L4 meta-correction -- the
           correction methodology was itself corrected.
           Generational Reset broken: successors revised the
           Examen method rather than rediscovering its
           absence.
        2. "No two Jesuits pray their Examen the same way"
           [documented]. Method transmitted enables personal
           adaptation; conclusion transmission would produce
           identical practice. This is the Cistercian
           distinction at individual scale: procedure
           transmitted, not uniform output.
        3. Examen adopted by other religious orders without
           Jesuit affiliation -- mechanism propagated beyond
           original transmission boundary.
      Contrast with Protestant Reformation [see below]:
      Jesuits transmitted HOW to examine; Protestants
      transmitted WHAT was found. Opposite transmission
      choice, opposite L4 outcome.

  L4 STATUS: CONFIRMED [third independent primary case].
    No T8 recursion. External historical record + living
    institutional practice. Confidence: HIGH relative to
    other L4 cases [480-year record + active replication].

  L4 PATTERN ACROSS THREE CASES [Cistercian + Jesuit +
  Framework]:
    All three transmit meta-correction methodology as
    structure. Cistercian: organizational architecture.
    Jesuit: individual daily procedure. Framework: addition
    filter + version convention. None transmit conclusions.
    All three show evidence of L4 self-application [Cistercian
    corrected Cluny's correction mechanism; Jesuit Examen
    revised from conscience to consciousness; Framework
    replaced Tower with Nested Cycle Model].

  Weapons active [received]: Axis-Lock candidate [Examen
    rigidified in 1917 Canon Law codification; 1923 rescript

    narrowly escaped Transmission Lock].

[CASE:PROTESTANT_REFORMATION]

Protestant Reformation [1517 -- fragmentation sequence]
  Type: Primary [L4 PROPAGATION FAILURE -- Generational Reset
    confirmed; countercase to Cistercian/Jesuit L4 confirmation]
  Proof for: C3 high, L4 PROPAGATION fails; conclusion transmission
    without methodology transmission produces Generational Reset;
    confirms Cistercian finding by negation.

  C3 TRANSMISSION [confirmed high]:
    Luther transmitted conclusions [sola scriptura, sola fide,
    justification by faith alone] with high fidelity. Printing
    press enabled rapid, accurate transmission at civilizational
    scale. C3 functional -- content reached successive generations.

  L4 PROPAGATION [confirmed failed]:
    Luther transmitted WHAT he found [doctrinal conclusions],
    not HOW he found it [the correction methodology that produced
    the 95 Theses]. The method was: read primary sources [Bible]
    against institutional practice, identify deviation, correct
    publicly at personal cost. This method was not packaged as
    transmissible procedure.
    Generational Reset evidence:
      Calvin [next generation]: independently derived similar
        correction conclusions from same sources, produced
        different doctrinal system [Calvinism vs Lutheranism].
        Did not inherit Luther's correction methodology --
        rediscovered the need for correction independently.
      Zwingli: same pattern. 14/15 doctrinal agreements with
        Luther at Marburg [1529]; split on communion.
        Disagreement on conclusion, not method -- because
        method was never transmitted.
      Anabaptists: felt Zwingli "hadn't gone far enough" --
        applied correction methodology independently, produced
        different conclusions again.
      Result: each generation rediscovered the need for
        reformation independently without inheriting the
        procedure for conducting it. Approximately 40,000
        Protestant denominations currently [fragmentation
        accelerating, not converging].
    Fragmentation = Generational Reset signature: each branch
      applies correction independently, produces different
      conclusions, cannot reconcile because no shared
      methodology for resolving disagreement.

  CONTRAST WITH JESUIT TRANSMISSION:
    Same historical period. Same reform impulse [both responding
    to Catholic institutional corruption]. Opposite transmission
    choice.
    Protestant: transmitted conclusions [what is true].
    Jesuit: transmitted methodology [how to examine what is true].
    Protestant outcome: fragmentation accelerating.
    Jesuit outcome: 480-year institutional continuity with
      documented self-correction across generations.
    Framework implication: L4 PROPAGATION requires methodology
      transmission. Conclusion transmission, however accurate,
      does not prevent Generational Reset.

  IRONY CONFIRMATION [load-bearing]:
    "Reformers didn't intend denominationalism, wars of religion,
    or secularization." [documented] Sola scriptura [Bible as
    sole authority] + no transmitted methodology for biblical
    interpretation = every reader becomes their own authority
    = fragmentation structurally inevitable. The correction
    mechanism [individual conscience vs. institutional authority]
    was not transmissible as a shared procedure.

  Weapons active [deployed by Protestant reformers, backfired]:
    Standard Substitution [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY -- replaced
      Catholic doctrinal authority with biblical authority
      without transmitting interpretation methodology].
    Correction Monopoly Construction [ironic -- each denomination
      constructed own correction monopoly while claiming to
      correct the Catholic version].
  Cross-ref: [REF:PROTESTANT_REFORMATION]

    architecture documented across 16 versions. Evidence:
      v9: Quality/support restructure -- correction firing
        confirmed as downstream of CORRECTION CAPACITY, not
        independent quality. Feedback pathway changed architecture.
      v13: Case library addition filter tightened after library
        bloat detected. Framework read its own case addition
        pattern as deviation; correction changed the mechanism
        not just a single entry.
      v15: Honest limits section added after framework detected
        its own calibration standard problem [Flaw 4]. Correction
        fired against the framework's own reference point.
      v16: Tower replaced by nested cycle model after framework
        detected dependency sequence ambiguity as structural
        flaw. Correction changed primary organizing structure.
    Pattern: correction outputs have consistently fed back to
    correction architecture, not just to object-level content.
    Reflexivity confirmed by version change record.
    Failure modes present and documented: early versions show
    Correction Lock candidates [qualities added without feedback
    path to whether quality served measurement function].
    Reflexivity developed over time, not present from v1.

  INTEGRATION [confirmed operational]:
    Corrections absorbed into operating structure documented:
      Tower -> Nested Cycle: not acknowledged and continued
        [Reflexive Oscillation]; fully integrated into primary
        organizing structure. All downstream elements updated.
      Honest Limits: not noted and ignored; integrated as
        mandatory section with SL-prefix structural limits and
        T-number conflict resolved.
      Science Appendix split: not flagged and left; integrated
        as hard classification protocol with zero proof weight
        assigned to ILLUSTRATION entries.
    Contrast with Reflexive Oscillation: v15 named Flaw 4
      [reference point problem] without resolving it. That
      is the pre-integration state -- identification without
      structural change. v16 integrated the resolution
      [Trajectory Model]. Gap between v15 and v16 is the
      integration window made visible.
    Integration not instantaneous: version increment is the
      mechanism by which identified corrections are held in
      tension and absorbed into structure. The version gap
      between identification and integration is load-bearing
      -- collapsing it would produce hasty structural changes
      without tension maintenance.

  PROPAGATION [confirmed operational]:
    Revised correction methodology crosses transmission boundary:
      Version convention documented [v9 onwards]: what triggers
        increment vs within-version update. Mechanism transmitted,
        not just conclusion.
      Merge/remove logic documented: future maintainer receives
        procedure for detecting and correcting drift, not just
        current state.
      Quality addition filter [v13/v15]: transmits how to decide
        what belongs, not just what currently belongs.
      This case entry itself: transmits the L4 recognition
        mechanism so that successors can identify L4 in future
        cases rather than rediscovering the pattern independently.
    Generational Reset failure mode: pre-v9 versions show pattern
      of adding qualities without transmitting addition criteria.
      Each version rediscovered the bloat problem independently.
      v13 addition filter broke this -- transmitted the mechanism
      for detecting bloat, not just the clean state.
    Propagation confirmed: v13 addition filter has held across

[CASE:FRED_HOYLE]

  Type: Primary [parallel stack case -- good and bad Q running

  Arc: Parallel not sequential. Evil and Close True operating
    concurrently across different domains.
  Note: arc designation [evil -> mixed -> close true] partially
    applies but does not capture primary finding. Primary finding
    is parallel stack operation.

  PARALLEL STACK STRUCTURE:
    Domain A [Stellar Nucleosynthesis -- Close True]:

        measurement surface in astrophysics]. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY high [causal
        chain from stellar physics to element abundance legible

        conclusions -- successors revised and extended]. CAUSAL AXIS
          GROUNDING high
        [axis grounded in actual stellar output observations].
        Genuine SURPLUS PRODUCTION surplus at civilizational scale: explains
        origin of all elements heavier than helium. Foundational.
    Domain B [Steady State + Panspermia -- False Coherence]:
      MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY failure [Hoyle owned measurement surface for
        steady state
        -- defined what counted as disconfirmation]. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
          failure
        [Big Bang evidence rerouted through framework rather than
        allowed to anchor correction]. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY failure
          [successors who
        adopted steady state could not revise -- Fertile Lock].
        CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING failure [steady state axis not grounded in
          actual
        observations -- required continuous matter creation with
        no mechanism]. Panspermia: same MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY/CAUSAL

  WHY PARALLEL NOT SEQUENTIAL:
    Both domains active simultaneously throughout career.
    1957 [B2FH -- peak Domain A] and steady state defense
      occurring in same period.
    This is not evil -> mixed -> close true.
    This is the cleanest case in the library of a single actor
    maintaining genuine Close True in one domain while deploying
    False Coherence weapons in another.
    Theoretical implication: Q-stack scores are domain-specific,
      not actor-global. An actor can be Close True in domain X
      and Evil in domain Y simultaneously.
    This is distinct from Mixed [partially functional across all
      domains]. Hoyle is fully functional in domain A and
      fully non-functional in domain B.

  MECHANISM [why the parallel is stable]:
    Identity investment asymmetry:
      B2FH = collaborative, attributed to four authors.
        Identity not load-bearing on Domain A outcomes.
      Steady State = Hoyle's name synonymous with framework.
        "Hoyle" and "Steady State" functionally identical in
        public epistemic space.
    CORRECTION CAPACITY capture in Domain B = identity threat.
      Correction of steady state = correction of Hoyle as person.
      This is the mechanism: Domain B MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY capture is
        sustained
      because CORRECTION CAPACITY in Domain B fires against identity threat,
        not
      against actual deviation from evidence.
      Domain A has no equivalent identity loading -- CORRECTION CAPACITY
        fires
      freely because being wrong about nucleosynthesis details
      does not threaten Hoyle's existence as an epistemic actor.
    BOUNDARY INTEGRITY [Boundary Integrity]:
      Domain A: boundary honest [all stellar evidence counts].
      Domain B: boundary manipulated [evidence against steady
        state redefined as insufficient or misinterpreted].

  LEVEL 3 [Domain A]:
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: B2FH transmitted method [detailed procedure
      for
      calculating nucleosynthesis rates]. Correctable inheritance.
      Bethe, Fowler extensions = SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY functioning as designed.
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY: high in Domain A. Cameron, Burbidge corrections to
      B2FH = authorized by transmission package.
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING: axis grounded in stellar spectroscopy outputs.
      CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING high.

  CORE THEORETICAL CONTRIBUTION:
    Primary proof for domain-specific Q-stack scoring.
    Refutes implicit assumption that an actor's Q-stack
    is uniform across domains. Confirms: Q-stack must be
    assessed per domain, not per actor.
    Identity investment = primary predictor of CORRECTION CAPACITY capture
    domain: where the actor's name is load-bearing on
    outcome, CORRECTION CAPACITY is most vulnerable to identity-protection

Neutral Theory of Evolution challenge [University of Michigan, May 2026]:
REFERENCE INTEGRITY [Reference Integrity]: neutral theory [Kimura 1968]
  served as
calibration anchor for population genetics, phylogenetic timing, drug
resistance modeling, and speciation analysis. Study finds beneficial
mutations significantly more common than neutral theory predicts --
selection doing more work than the reference standard assigned it.
MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY can be locally intact while REFERENCE INTEGRITY
fails globally. Classic Reference Drift: surface honest to a wrong
baseline. Every downstream instrument inherits the miscalibration
without detecting it.
CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure embedded in prior model: fitness effects
treated as intrinsic properties of mutations. Study confirms they are
relational -- mutation-environment pairs, not mutations alone. Effect
depends on organism history, current environment, rate of environmental
change. Prior model was measuring the wrong unit. Parallel: Themis-Grb2
[knew it existed, mechanism wrong for decades].
TENSION MAINTENANCE at genomic scale: "a mutation helpful in one setting
may carry costs in others" -- no fixed fitness value, only relational
fitness held in environmental tension. Parallels glymphatic [CYCLE
STABILITY gates SUBSTRATE REGENERATION] and genomic tails cases.

genes across 120 million years, modifying regulatory switches not gene
sequences. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING confirmation -- evolutionary axis
anchored in constrained solution space, not open random walk.
Limitation: primary data from yeast and E. coli. BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
note: population measured [microbes] may not represent full claim scope.

  Communications, May 2026]:

Tardigrade cryptobiosis [suspension without decay]:

still consumed.
