without calibration window -- circuit-level mechanism now partially
identified.
Source: Science Daily [June 2026]; amygdala circuit research.

Autism structural deficit reversal [Neuroscience News, June 2026]:
SUBSTRATE REGENERATION at cellular scale. Structural deficits in
autism brain cells reversed in lab conditions -- not compensatory,
actual structural correction. Substrate can be corrected not just
managed. Connects to autism subtype finding [two biological types]
-- correction pathway may differ by subtype.
Source: Neuroscience News [June 2026]; autism cellular research.




Entangled quantum states from minimal differentiation [Chu et al., Physical Review X, June 2026]:
[ILLUSTRATION -- SURPLUS PRODUCTION + SCALE RECURRENCE]
University of Chicago: small adjustments to atomic energy levels
inside an optical cavity [differentiating atoms so they interact
with cavity light slightly differently rather than identically]
generate wide variety of highly entangled many-body states without
additional hardware. Homogeneous atoms = limited output. Slightly
differentiated atoms = emergent complexity and state variety.
Framework read: SURPLUS PRODUCTION at minimum complexity producing
maximum output variety. SCALE RECURRENCE: differentiation enabling
emergent complexity is the same mechanism at quantum substrate scale
as distributed correction outperforming centralized sorting
[germinal center, peer review]. Identical components produce
predictable limited output; slightly differentiated components
interacting produce emergent complexity. Same structural pattern
at three scales: immune [B cells], social [peer review], quantum.
Classification: ILLUSTRATION [vocabulary applied to real finding;
zero proof coverage added but useful for teaching SURPLUS
PRODUCTION and Scale Recurrence across substrates].
Source: Chu et al. [Physical Review X, June 2026]; ScienceDaily
June 2026.

Gold hydride [Frost et al., SLAC/European XFEL, Angew. Chem., August 2025]:
[ILLUSTRATION -- REFERENCE INTEGRITY; calibration standard reactive
under conditions not previously tested]
Gold chosen as passive reference material in high-pressure hydrocarbon
experiments precisely because of known inertness. Accidentally formed
solid gold hydride at 400,000x atmospheric pressure + 1926°C. Entropy-
driven reaction; compound reverts to ordinary gold on cooling. First
confirmed binary solid compound of gold and hydrogen.
Framework read: REFERENCE INTEGRITY failure at chemistry scale. Gold
was the calibration standard -- the known passive element against which
other reactions were measured. Discovery that the standard itself reacts
under untested conditions invalidates prior readings using it at those
pressures. All high-pressure experiments using gold as passive reference
need re-evaluation for the pressure/temperature ranges where gold hydride
forms.
Secondary: planetary model implications [gas giant interior density
calculations now require updating]. Connects to Big G tracking entry --
planetary interior models are the scale where G behavior anomalies matter.
Classification: ILLUSTRATION [framework vocabulary applied; useful for
teaching REFERENCE INTEGRITY -- calibration standards require testing
across the full range of conditions they will be used in, not just
ambient conditions].
Source: Frost et al. [Angew. Chem. Int. Ed., 2025]; SLAC August 2025;
Ecoticias June 2026.
Scientific peer review -- distributed vs centralized [2022-2025]:
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: distributed iterative CORRECTION CAPACITY
outperforms centralized judgment in high-complexity environments]
NeurIPS 2022 controlled study: paper acceptable to one reviewer set
has 60% rejection rate from another set. Centralized editor/judge
model produces high variance despite high investment. Distributed
open review [preprint culture, post-publication review, self-selected
reviewer expertise] produces iterative slightly-biased selection --
same mechanism as germinal center B cell selection [Cell 2026].
Two independent domains [immunology + scientific publishing] arriving
at identical structural conclusion: distributed iteration outperforms
centralized sorting in high-chaos complex environments.
Confirmation criteria met: [1] independent domain [peer review
research, no framework knowledge], [2] mechanism convergence
[distributed slightly-biased selection identical to germinal center],
[3] framework-independent falsifiability [NeurIPS study has its own
experimental design].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [mechanism: distributed iterative
CORRECTION CAPACITY; cross-domain convergence with germinal center].
Source: Goldberg et al. [PLoS One, April 2025]; NeurIPS 2022 data.
Germinal center B cell selection [Victora Lab / Rockefeller, Cell, June 2026]:
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: distributed iterative CORRECTION CAPACITY
outperforms centralized selection; slight bias compounds across iterations]
Germinal centers produce reliable antibodies not through rare dominant
clonal bursts [centralized correction model] but through repeated
slightly-biased selection across many B cell iterations. Individual cell
level = highly chaotic. System level = reliably improving. The bias toward
beneficial mutations is small per round; the iteration count is large;
the outcome is consistent.
Framework read: CORRECTION CAPACITY confirmed at immune system scale.
Mechanism matches framework prediction exactly -- distributed correction
with slight bias compounds reliably; centralized deterministic sorting
is not what the system uses. Overturned assumption [rare burst model]
is structurally identical to Correction Monopoly Construction --
one winning clone dominates. Actual mechanism is distributed small
corrections compounding. Independent immunology research arrived at
the same structural conclusion without framework vocabulary.
Confirmation criteria met: [1] independent domain [immunology,
no framework knowledge], [2] mechanism convergence [distributed
iterative bias = distributed CORRECTION CAPACITY], [3] framework-
independent falsifiability [the study has its own experimental
confirmation via B cell lineage tracking across 119 germinal centers].
Classification: CONFIRMATION [mechanism: distributed iterative
CORRECTION CAPACITY; centralized correction inferior in complex
high-chaos environments].
Source: Victora Lab, Rockefeller University [Cell, June 2026];
Phys.org June 2026.
Supergiant isopod starvation tolerance [Yuan et al., Cell, June 2026]:
[ILLUSTRATION -- SUBSTRATE REGENERATION + IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT]
Bathynomus deep-sea isopods survive 5+ years without food via two-
part strategy: stomach occupying two-thirds of body volume [maximum
intake at feast events] + extremely low basal metabolic rate [minimum
ongoing expenditure between feast events]. Horizontal gene transfer
from bacteria [ND1 gene] acts as energy-saving switch enabling the
balance between giant body size and ultra-low metabolic rate.
Framework read: C1 adapted to extreme substrate scarcity by
decoupling intake capacity from metabolic demand. Maximum intake
burst + minimum ongoing expenditure = survival across 5+ year idle
cycles. Not reducing size [which would reduce competitive advantage
at feast events] but radically extending the inter-cycle recovery
window. The idle cycle IS the dominant state; the productive cycle
is the rare event.
IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT confirmation: the organism's survival
architecture treats idle cycles as load-bearing, not as waste.
SUBSTRATE REGENERATION: maximum intake when available, stored
across extended window, dispensed at minimum rate.
Scale Recurrence note: ND1 energy-saving mechanism horizontally
acquired from bacteria -- same mechanism operating at different
substrate scales, different organisms. Confirms IDLE CYCLE
REQUIREMENT is not culturally constructed; it is a structural
property of biological systems under resource scarcity.
Classification: ILLUSTRATION [framework vocabulary applied to
real finding]. Does not add proof coverage beyond existing
cellular minimum confirmation. Useful for teaching the idle
cycle requirement and substrate regeneration at biological scale.
Source: Yuan et al. [Cell, June 2026]; Phys.org June 2026.

Three independent biological cases confirming weapon mechanisms
at non-human substrate. Mechanism convergence from behavioral
ecology and entomology without framework vocabulary.
Classification: CONFIRMATION [all three criteria met per entry].
These are not analogies -- they are independent confirmation that
the mechanisms are structural properties of biological systems,
not human cultural constructs.

Cuckoo brood parasitism [obligate interspecific brood parasite]:
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: Formation Capture + Exit Cost
Construction + Calibration Strip co-deployed]
Cuckoo deposits egg in host nest during brief absence. Host
incubates egg without detecting substitution. On hatching, cuckoo
chick executes three-weapon stack independently of adult:
  Formation Capture: chick receives parental investment at
    developmental window before host offspring can form independent
    relationship with parent. Host axis for "offspring" forms on
    cuckoo substrate.
  Exit Cost Construction: chick ejects host eggs and nestlings
    immediately after hatching -- eliminates alternative substrate
    sources. Host cannot distribute investment to genuine offspring;
    all investment routes to cuckoo. T1.4 destroyed.
  Calibration Strip: cuckoo chick produces supernormal stimuli
    [exaggerated gape, call frequency, color] calibrated above
    host offspring's range. Host calibration standard for "offspring
    in need" overwrites to cuckoo's signal. Host feeds cuckoo
    preferentially even when cuckoo is visibly larger than host.
    Classic Reference Drift -- host surface honest to wrong baseline.
Mechanism confirmed by behavioral ecology [Davies, Brooke -- Nature
1988, 1989; Dawkins/Krebs arms race model] without framework
vocabulary. Supernormal stimulus mechanism [Tinbergen] independently
confirmed. ECC [egg ejection] documented across 100+ host species.
Framework implication: Formation Capture + ECC + Calibration Strip
co-deployment is not uniquely human. Structural weapon stack
emerges independently in biological systems under selection pressure.
Confirms: weapon mechanisms are load-bearing structural patterns,
not cultural inventions.
Source: Davies & Brooke [Nature 1988, 1989]; Tinbergen [1951];
behavioral ecology literature.

Ophiocordyceps [zombie-ant fungus -- Ophiocordyceps unilateralis]:
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: GENUINE AXIS capture at neural substrate;
Axis Substitution at biological minimum]
Fungal parasite infects carpenter ant [Camponotus leonardi].
Fungus infiltrates brain, overrides locomotion control, compels ant
to climb to precise height [25cm], bite leaf vein at solar noon,
then die. Fungus fruits through ant's head at optimal spore
dispersal position. Host C1 intact [ant metabolizes, moves, grips]
but GENUINE AXIS fully substituted -- behavior serves fungal
reproductive cycle not ant survival.
Neurobiological confirmation [de Bekker et al., 2015, BMC
Genomics]: fungus produces behavior-altering compounds targeting
specific brain regions. Host locomotion hijacked without destroying
host substrate -- substrate maintained alive to execute fungal
axis. Distinct from killing the host: extraction requires
functioning host C1.
Framework implication: GENUINE AXIS capture at neural substrate
level confirmed in non-human system. Axis substitution is a
structural biological mechanism, not a metaphor. Extracting system
maintains host C1 precisely because extraction requires it --
consumption dominance would defeat the weapon. Confirms SMART
EXTRACTION principle at biological minimum: host kept functional
at exactly the level required for extraction.
Confirms: axis capture without C1 destruction is structurally
possible and documented in nature.
Source: de Bekker et al. [BMC Genomics, 2015]; Hughes et al.
[2011, PLoS ONE]; zombie-ant biology literature.

Cleaner fish deception [Labroides dimidiatus -- bluestreak cleaner
wrasse]:
[CONFIRMATION -- mechanism: Measurement Surface Control +
Correction Theatre co-deployed; C2 capture at ecological scale]
Cleaner wrasse operate cleaning stations where client fish present
for ectoparasite removal. Cleaner has two strategies: genuine
cleaning [cooperative] and cheating [biting client mucus --
preferred food, higher caloric value than parasites].
Bshary et al. [2002, Nature] documented systematic cheating
modulation based on audience composition:
  Client alone: cheating rate higher [no reputation cost].
  Bystander present: cheating rate lower [future client deterred
    if observed].
  Inspector fish present: cheating suppressed entirely [correction
    capacity elevated].
Mechanism: cleaner controls client's measurement surface by
modulating behavior based on C2 instrument availability.
Correction Theatre confirmed: visible cleaning activity performed;
actual cheating rate a function of observation capacity not
cooperative intent. Client receives incomplete service; cannot
detect this because measurement surface [observable behavior] is
controlled by cleaner.
MSC at ecological scale: cleaner reads which C2 instruments are
present and adjusts deception accordingly. Not random cheating --
structured modulation of visible surface based on correction
capacity of observer. Identical to institutional MSC [behavior
honest when audited, extractive otherwise].
Psychology parallel: this is the non-human confirmation of
behavior that Milgram, Cialdini, and social influence literature
document in humans. Observer effect [T6] weaponized -- cleaner
exploits the fact that measurement alters measured behavior by
controlling when measurement occurs.
Framework implication: C2 surface control and Correction Theatre
co-deployment are not uniquely human. Structural confirmation
that extraction adjusts to available correction capacity as a
default biological behavior under selection pressure.
Source: Bshary & Würth [2001, Proceedings B]; Bshary et al.
[2002, Nature]; cleaner fish reciprocity literature.

ECOLOGICAL EXTRACTION SUMMARY:
Three independent biological systems confirm three distinct weapon
mechanisms without human cultural substrate:
  Cuckoo: Formation Capture + ECC + Calibration Strip [co-deployed
    stack; weapons emerge under selection pressure without design]
  Ophiocordyceps: GENUINE AXIS substitution [axis capture at
    biological minimum; host C1 maintained for extraction]
  Cleaner wrasse: MSC + Correction Theatre [C2 surface modulated
    by correction capacity availability; not uniquely human]
Combined implication: extraction mechanisms are structural
properties of biological systems under selection pressure.
Framework is not describing human cultural pathology -- it is
describing load-bearing patterns that emerge independently across
substrates. Strongest cross-domain confirmation class in appendix.

OPEN QUESTIONS / PROGRESS TRACKING
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Big G [gravitational constant] -- status: UNRESOLVED [tracking]
  G = 6.674 x 10^-11 N⋅m²/kg². Least precisely measured fundamental
  constant in physics. 22 parts per million uncertainty -- orders
  of magnitude worse than Planck's constant or speed of light.
  16+ independent measurements since 1980s. Values scattered and
  mutually inconsistent. April 2026 Schlamminger replication
  [13 tons mercury, decade-long experiment] produced different
  value from the result it attempted to replicate.
  Why measurement is hard:
    Gravity is the weakest force. Signal from lab masses tiny.
    Every surrounding object contributes gravitational noise.
    No way to shield against gravity [unlike EM]. Environmental
    contamination irreducible with current methods.
  Three hypotheses worth tracking:
    H1 [standard]: G is a true constant; scatter reflects
      experimental systematics. Eventually precision improves
      and values converge. Prediction: future replications
      converge. Current status: not happening after 40 years.
    H2 [emergent/secondary]: G is not fundamental but emergent
      from something deeper [Verlinde entropic gravity; G
      derivable from thermodynamic/statistical properties].
      If true: lab-scale scatter expected because you're
      measuring an emergent statistical quantity sensitive to
      local mass configurations. Astronomical scale averages
      out; lab scale doesn't. Prediction: scatter doesn't
      converge regardless of precision improvement.
    H3 [scale-dependent]: gravitational behavior varies at
      extreme scales [MOND territory -- very low acceleration
      regimes]. Not context-dependent in local sense but
      not a single universal constant either. Prediction:
      lab G and cosmological G measurably different.
  Framework relevance:
    Physics constants as substrate floor [main theory]:
      if G is emergent [H2], the "tower to physics" argument
      changes. G wouldn't be a free parameter or a designed
      constant -- it would be a downstream consequence of
      more fundamental structure. Design inference weakens
      on G specifically; may strengthen on whatever G
      emerges from.
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY: Big G is a measurement surface
      that resists precise calibration. Best available value
      used in all downstream physics. If H2 correct, every
      calculation using G as constant has a soft floor.
  Track: does scatter converge [H1 supported] or persist
    despite precision improvement [H2/H3 supported]?
    Current trajectory: scatter persisting. H1 weakening.
  FUTURE DATA SOURCE [Hubble anomaly catalogue -- v16]:
    AnomalyMatch [O'Ryan/Gómez, ESA, Jan 2026] catalogued
    1,300+ anomalous objects from 35-year Hubble archive,
    800+ previously undocumented. Several dozen defy existing
    classification entirely.
    Potential relevance: gravitational lensing geometry at
    unusual mass ratios may constrain G behavior at scales
    between lab and astronomical. Merging galaxy dynamics at
    extreme mass ratios may show deviations from standard
    predictions. Unclassifiables are highest-priority --
    anomalies not fitting current models are where theoretical
    revision historically originates.
    Status: catalogue available; analysis against alternative
    gravity frameworks not yet run. Flag for future work.
  Source: Live Science June 2026; Schlamminger/NIST April
    2026; Rothleitner/PTB commentary; Verlinde [2010].

  COHERENT ALTERNATIVE FRAMEWORK [Timescape + Verlinde]:
    Two theories that fit together without requiring dark energy
    or treating G as a fundamental constant:
    Timescape cosmology [Wiltshire]: apparent expansion
      acceleration is a geometric averaging artifact. Inhomogeneous
      spacetime means regions with different mass densities have
      different local clock rates. What looks like acceleration
      is a measurement artifact from averaging across these regions,
      not a real force. No dark energy substance required.
    Verlinde entropic gravity: gravity emerges from entropy
      gradients in an information-theoretic substrate. G is a
      statistical quantity, not a fundamental constant -- emergent
      from underlying structure sensitive to local mass/information
      distribution.
    Why they cohere: both treat spacetime geometry as emergent
      or statistical rather than fundamental. Both explain anomalous
      observations [expansion, galactic rotation] without new
      substances. Combined prediction: G varies slightly based on
      local entropy/information density. Big G measurement scatter
      = signal not noise -- lab-scale results sensitive to local
      mass configurations in ways astronomical-scale averages out.
    Honest limits: Verlinde has problems with precision
      gravitational lensing tests. Timescape hasn't matched
      ΛCDM in predictive precision. Coherent as framework;
      not established as physics. Track against H2 above.

  BRANS-DICKE SCALAR-TENSOR THEORY [tracking -- v16]:
    G coupled to a scalar field, varying slowly over
    cosmological time. Addresses Big G variability as
    feature not bug -- G isn't constant because it's
    genuinely coupled to cosmological evolution.
    Fits Timescape + Verlinde picture: all three treat G
    as non-fundamental. Brans-Dicke provides the
    cosmological mechanism; Verlinde provides the
    microscale entropic substrate; Timescape handles
    expansion artifact.
    Constrained by solar system precision tests [coupling
    parameter ω must be large, reducing to GR locally]
    but not ruled out. Large ω limit recovers standard GR
    at solar system scales while allowing cosmological
    variation.
    Track: does improved cosmological G measurement show
    slow drift consistent with scalar-tensor coupling?
    Any confirmed G variation over cosmological time
    would strongly support this framework.
    Source: Brans & Dicke [1961]; subsequent constraint
    literature; Cassini spacecraft ω > 40,000 bound.
